Elohim blessed Noach and his sons. He said to them, Be fruitful and multiply and fill the earth.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:2)


וְדַחֲלַתְכוֹן וְאֵימַתְכוֹן תְּהֵי עַל כָּל חֵוַת אַרְעָא וְעַל כָּל עוֹפָא דִשְׁמַיָּא בְּכֹל דִּי תַרְחֵישׁ אַרְעָא וּבְכָל נוּנֵי יַמָּא בְּיֶדְכוֹן יְהוֹן מְסִירִין:


Your fear and terror will be upon every wild beast of the earth, and upon every bird in the heavens. All that creeps on the ground, and all the fish of the sea, are given into your hands.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:3)


כָּל רִחֲשָׁא דְּהוּא חַי לְכוֹן יְהֵי לְמֵיכָל כִּירוֹק עִשְׂבָּא יְהָבִית לְכוֹן יָת כֹּלָּא:


Every moving creature that lives, shall be yours for food; like the green vegetation [which I gave previously] I have [now] given you everything [for food].


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:4)


בְּרַם בִּשְׂרָא בְּנַפְשֵׁיהּ דְּמֵיהּ לָא תֵכְלוּן:


Nevertheless, flesh with its lifeblood, you shall not eat.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:5)


וּבְרַם יָת דִּמְכוֹן לְנַפְשָׁתֵיכוֹן אֶתְבַּע מִיַּד כָּל חַיְתָא אֶתְבְּעִנֵּיהּ וּמִיַּד אֱנָשָׁא **מִיַּד גְּבַר דְּיֵשׁוֹד יָת דְּמָא דַאֲחוֹהִי** אֶתְבַּע יָת נַפְשָׁא דֶאֱנָשָׁא:


However, of the blood of your souls, I will demand an account; from the hand of every beast will I demand it. From the hand of man, even from the hand of man’s own brother, [**from the hand of a man who spills his brother’s blood,**] will I demand an account of man’s soul.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:6)


דְּיֵשׁוֹד דְּמָא דֶאֱנָשָׁא **בְּסַהֲדִין עַל מֵימַר דַּיָּנַיָּא דְּמֵיהּ יִתְּשָׁד** אֲרֵי בְּצַלְמָא דַיְיָ עֲבַד יָת אֱנָשָׁא:


He who spills the blood of man, through man shall his blood be spilled, [**by witnesses according to the word of judges shall his blood be spilled,**] for in the image of Elohim, He made man.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:7)


וְאַתּוּן פּוּשׁוּ וּסְגוּ אִתְיְלִידוּ בְאַרְעָא וּסְגוּ בַהּ:


And now be fruitful and multiply, spread throughout the earth and multiply on it.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:8)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְנֹחַ וְלִבְנוֹהִי עִמֵּיהּ לְמֵימָר:


Elohim said to Noach and to his sons with him, saying,


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:9)


וַאֲנָא הָא אֲנָא מֵקִים יָת קְיָמִי עִמְּכוֹן וְעִם בְּנֵיכוֹן בַּתְרֵיכוֹן:


Behold, I am making a covenant with you and with your offspring after you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:10)


וְעִם כָּל נַפְשָׁא חַיְתָא דְּעִמְּכוֹן בְּעוֹפָא בִבְעִירָא וּבְכָל חֵיוַת אַרְעָא דְּעִמְּכוֹן מִכֹּל נַפְקֵי תֵבוֹתָא לְכֹל חַיַּת אַרְעָא:


And with every living creature that is with you, with the birds, the animals, and all the beasts of the earth with you, all who departed from the ark, including every living creature on earth.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:11)


וְאָקֵם יָת קְיָמִי עִמְּכוֹן וְלָא יִשְׁתֵּיצֵי כָל בִּשְׂרָא עוֹד מִמֵּי טוֹפָנָא וְלָא יְהֵי עוֹד טוֹפָנָא לְחַבָּלָא אַרְעָא:


I will keep My covenant with you, that never again will all flesh be cut off by flood waters, never again will flood waters destroy the earth.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:12)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ דָּא אָת קְיָם דִּי אֲנָא יָהֵב בֵּין **מֵימְרִי** וּבֵינֵיכוֹן וּבֵין כָּל נַפְשָׁא חַיְתָא דִּי עִמְּכוֹן לְדָרֵי עַלְמָא:


Elohim said, This is the sign of the covenant that I pledge between Myself [**My Word**] and you, and between every living creature that is with you, for the generations of all time.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:13)


יָת קַשְׁתִּי יְהָבִית בַּעֲנָנָא וּתְהִי לְאָת קְיָם בֵּין **מֵימְרִי** וּבֵין אַרְעָא:


I have set My [rain] bow in the clouds, and it shall be a sign of the covenant between Myself [**My Word**] and the earth.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:14)


וִיהִי בַּעֲנָנוּתִי עֲנָנָא עַל אַרְעָא וְתִתְחֲזֵי קַשְׁתָּא בַּעֲנָנָא:


At a time when I bring clouds over the earth, the [rain] bow will be seen in the clouds.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:15)


וּדְכִירְנָא יָת קְיָמִי דִּי בֵין **מֵימְרִי** וּבֵינֵיכוֹן וּבֵין כָּל נַפְשָׁא חַיְתָא בְּכָל בִּשְׂרָא וְלָא יְהֵא עוֹד מַיָּא לְטוֹפָנָא לְחַבָּלָא כָל בִּשְׂרָא:


I shall remember My covenant that is between Myself [**My Word**] and you, and between every living soul in all flesh, that never again will flood waters destroy all flesh.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:16)


וּתְהֵי קַשְׁתָּא בַּעֲנָנָא וְאֵחֲזִנַּהּ לְמִדְכַּר קְיַם עָלָם בֵּין **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ וּבֵין כָּל נַפְשָׁא חַיְתָא בְּכָל בִּשְׂרָא דִּי עַל אַרְעָא:


The [rain]bow shall be in the clouds, and I will see it to remember the eternal covenant between [**the Word of**] Elohim and between every living soul that is in all flesh that is on the earth.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:17)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְנֹחַ דָּא אָת קְיָם דִּי אֲקֵמִית בֵּין **מֵימְרִי** וּבֵין כָּל בִּשְׂרָא דִּי עַל אַרְעָא:


Elohim said to Noach, This is the sign of the covenant that I have established between Myself [**My Word**] and between all flesh that is on the earth.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:18)


וַהֲווֹ בְנֵי נֹחַ דִּי נְפָקוּ מִן תֵּבוֹתָא שֵׁם וְחָם וָיָפֶת וְחָם הוּא אֲבוּהִי דִכְנָעַן:


The sons of Noach, who went out of the ark were Sheim, Yafes and Cham; Cham was the father of Canaan.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:19)


תְּלָתָא אִלֵּין בְּנֵי נֹחַ וּמֵאִלֵּין **אִתְבַּדָּרוּ** כָּל אַרְעָא:


These three were the sons of Noach, and from these, the whole world developed [**was spread out**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:20)


וְשָׁרֵי נֹחַ גְּבַר **פָּלַח בְּאַרְעָא** וּנְצִיב כַּרְמָא:


Noach began to be a man of the soil [**a man who worked the land**] and he planted a vineyard.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:21)


וּשְׁתִי מִן חַמְרָא וּרְוִי וְאִתְגְּלִי בְּגוֹ מַשְׁכְּנֵיהּ:


He drank of the wine and was intoxicated. He [then] uncovered himself in his tent.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:22)


וַחֲזָא חָם אֲבוּהִי דִכְנַעַן יָת עֶרְיְתָא דַאֲבוּהִי וְחַוִּי לִתְרֵין אֲחוֹהִי **בְּשׁוּקָא**:


Cham the father of Canaan saw the nakedness of his father, and told it to his brothers [who were] Outside [**in the marketplace**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:23)


וּנְסֵיב שֵׁם וָיֶפֶת יָת כְּסוּתָא וְשַׁוִּיוּ עַל כְּתַף תַּרְוֵיהוֹן וַאֲזָלוּ מְחַזְּרִין וַחֲפִיאוּ יָת עֶרְיְתָא דַאֲבוּהוֹן וְאַפֵּיהוֹן מְחַזְּרִין וְעֶרְיְתָא דַאֲבוּהוֹן לָא חֲזוֹ:


Sheim and Yafes took the cloak, placed it on their shoulders, and walked backwards, covering the nakedness of their father. They faced away from him, and did not see the nakedness of their father.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:24)


וְאִתְּעַר נֹחַ מֵחַמְרֵיהּ וִידַע יָת דִּי עֲבַד לֵיהּ בְּרֵיהּ זְעֵירָא:


Noach awoke from his wine [induced sleep] and he realized what had been done to him by his youngest son.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:25)


וַאֲמַר לִיט כְּנָעַן עֶבֶד פָּלַח עַבְדִין יְהֵי לַאֲחוֹהִי:


He said, Cursed is Canaan. A slave of slaves shall he be to his brothers.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:26)


וַאֲמַר בְּרִיךְ יְיָ אֱלָהֵהּ דְּשֵׁם וִיהֵי כְנַעַן עַבְדָא לְהוֹן:


He then said, Blessed is Adonoy, God of Sheim; may Canaan be his slave.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:27)


יַפְתֵּי יְיָ לְיֶפֶת וְיַשְׁרֵי **שְׁכִינְתֵּיהּ** בְּמַשְׁכְּנֵיהּ דְּשֵׁם וִיהֵי כְנַעַן עַבְדָא לְהוֹן:


Elohim will enlarge Yefes, but He [**His Shechina**] will dwell in the tents of Sheim, and Canaan will be their slave.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:28)


וַחֲיָא נֹחַ בָּתַר טוֹפָנָא תְּלַת מְאָה וְחַמְשִׁין שְׁנִין:


After the Flood, Noach lived three hundred and fifty years.


###### Onkelos Genesis 9:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/9:29)


וַהֲווֹ כָל יוֹמֵי נֹחַ תְּשַׁע מְאָה וְחַמְשִׁין שְׁנִין וּמִית:


All the days of Noach totaled nine hundred and fifty years, and he died.



## Chapter 10

###### Onkelos Genesis 10:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:1)


וְאִלֵּין תּוּלְדַת בְּנֵי נֹחַ שֵׁם חָם וָיָפֶת וְאִתְיְלִידוּ לְהוֹן בְּנִין בָּתַר טוֹפָנָא:


These are the histories of Noach’s sons, Sheim, Cham and Yafes. Children were born to them after the Flood.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:2)


בְּנֵי יֶפֶת גּוֹמֶר וּמָגוֹג וּמָדַי וְיָוָן וְתוּבָל וּמֶשֶׁךְ וְתִירָס:


The sons of Yafes were: Gomer, Magog, Modai, Yavan, Tuval, Meshech, and Tiras.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:3)


וּבְנֵי גּוֹמֶר אַשְׁכְּנַז וְרִיפַת וְתוֹגַרְמָה:


The sons of Gomer were: Ashkenaz, Riphas and Togarmah.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:4)


וּבְנֵי יָוָן אֶלִישָׁה וְתַרְשִׁישׁ כִּתִּים וְדֹדָנִים:


The sons of Yavan were: Elishah, Tarshish, Kittim and Dodanim.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:5)


מֵאִלֵּין אִתַּפְרָשׁוּ נַגְוַת עַמְמַיָּא בְּאַרְעֲהוֹן גְּבַר לְלִישָׁנֵיהּ לְזַרְעֲיָתְהוֹן בְּעַמְמֵיהוֹן:


From these the nations were separated into islands, [set apart] in their own lands, each to its language, according to their families in their nations.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:6)


וּבְנֵי חָם כּוּשׁ וּמִצְרַיִם וּפוּט וּכְנָעַן:


The sons of Cham were: Cush, Mitzraim, Put and Canaan.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:7)


וּבְנֵי כוּשׁ סְבָא וַחֲוִילָה וְסַבְתָּה וְרַעְמָה וְסַבְתְּכָא וּבְנֵי רַעְמָה שְׁבָא וּדְדָן:


The sons of Cush were: Seva, Chavilah, Savtah, Raamah, and Savtecha. The sons of Raamah were: Sheva and Dedan.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:8)


וְכוּשׁ אוֹלִיד יָת נִמְרֹד הוּא שָׁרֵי לְמֵהֲוֵי גִבַּר **תַּקִּיף** בְּאַרְעָא:


Cush produced [a son] Nimrod. He began to be [**mighty and**] powerful on earth.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:9)


הוּא הֲוָה **גִבַּר תַּקִּיף** קֳדָם יְיָ עַל כֵּן יִתְאֲמַר כְּנִמְרֹד **גִּבַּר תַּקִּיף** קֳדָם יְיָ:


He was a mighty hunter [**mighty and powerful**] before Adonoy. It is therefore said, Like Nimrod, a mighty hunter [**mighty and powerful**] before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:10)


וַהֲוָה רֵישׁ מַלְכוּתֵיהּ בָּבֶל וְאֶרֶךְ וְאַכַּד וְכַלְנֵה בְּאַרְעָא דְ**בָבֶל**:


The beginning of his kingdom was Babylon, Erech, Akad and Kalneh in the land of Shinar [**Babylon**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:11)


מִן אַרְעָא (נ"א עֵיצָה) הַהִיא **נְפַק אֲתוּרָאָה וּבְנָא** יָת נִינְוֵה וְיָת רְחֹבֹת (נ"א רְחוֹבֵי) קַרְתָּא וְיָת כָּלַח:


Asshur departed that land, and he built [**The Assyrians departed that land and built**] Nineveh, Rechovos Iyr and Kalach.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:12)


וְיָת רֶסֶן בֵּין נִינְוֵה וּבֵין כָּלַח הִיא קַרְתָּא רַבְּתָא:


[He also built] Resen between Nineveh and Kalach. This was the great city.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:13)


וּמִצְרָאֵי (נ"א וּמִצְרַיִם) אוֹלִיד יָת לוּדָאֵי וְיָת עֲנָמָאֵי וְיָת לְהָבָאֵי וְיָת נַפְתּוּחָאֵי:


Mitzraim produced [the following nations]: Ludim, Anamim, Lehavim and the Naftuchim.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:14)


וְיָת פַּתְרוּסָאֵי וְיָת כַּסְלוּחָאֵי דִּי נְפָקוּ מִתַּמָּן פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי וְיָת **קַפּוּטְקָאֵי**:


[Also] the Pasrusim, and the Kasluchim; from whom came the Plishtim and the Kaphtorim [**Kaputkim**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:15)


וּכְנַעַן אוֹלִיד יָת צִידוֹן בָּכְרֵיהּ וְיָת חֵת:


Canaan produced his first-born, Sidon, and Cheis.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:16)


וְיָת יְבוּסָאֵי וְיָת אֱמוֹרָאֵי וְיָת גִּרְגָּשָׁאֵי:


[And] the Yevusite, the Amorite, the Girgashite,


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:17)


וְיָת חִוָּאֵי וְיָת עַרְקָאֵי וְיָת **אַנְתּוֹסָאֵי**:


The Chivite, the Arkite, the Sinite [**Antosite**],


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:18)


וְיָת אַרְוָדָאֵי וְיָת צְמָרָאֵי וְיָת חֲמָתָאֵי וּבָתַר כֵּן **אִתְבַּדָּרוּ** זַרְעֲיַת כְּנַעֲנָאֵי:


The Arvadite, the Tzemarite, and the Chamasite. Later, the families of the Canaanites [further] developed [**were spread out**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:19)


וַהֲוָה תְּחוּם כְּנַעֲנָאֵי מִצִּידוֹן מָטֵי לִגְרָר עַד עַזָּה מָטֵי לִסְדוֹם וַעֲמוֹרָה וְאַדְמָה וּצְבוֹיִם עַד לָשַׁע:


The borders of the Canaanites were from Sidon toward Gerar until Gaza, toward Sedom, Amorah, Admah, and Tzevoyim, until Lasha.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:20)


אִלֵּין בְּנֵי חָם לְזַרְעֲיָתְהוֹן לְלִישָׁנְהוֹן בְּאַרְעָתְהוֹן בְּעַמְמֵיהוֹן:


These are the sons of Cham according to their families, and their languages, in their lands and in their nations.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:21)


וּלְשֵׁם אִתְיְלִיד אַף הוּא אֲבוּהוֹן דְּכָל בְּנֵי עֵבֶר אֲחוּהִי דְּיֶפֶת רַבָּא:


Sons were also born to Sheim, father of the Children of the Other Side. He was the brother of Yefes, the elder.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:22)


בְּנֵי שֵׁם עֵילָם וְאַשּׁוּר וְאַרְפַּכְשַׁד וְלוּד וַאֲרָם:


The sons of Sheim were: Elam, and Asshur, Arpachshad, Lud, and Aram.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:23)


וּבְנֵי אֲרָם עוּץ וְחוּל וְגֶתֶר וָמַשׁ:


The sons of Aram were: Uz, Chul, Geser and Mash.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:24)


וְאַרְפַּכְשַׁד אוֹלִיד יָת שָׁלַח וְשֶׁלַח אוֹלִיד יָת עֵבֶר:


Arpachshad produced Shelach, and Shelach produced Eiver.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:25)


וּלְעֵבֶר אִתְיְלִידוּ תְּרֵין בְּנִין שׁוּם חַד פֶּלֶג אֲרֵי בְּיוֹמוֹהִי אִתְפְּלִיגַת אַרְעָא וְשׁוּם אֲחוּהִי יָקְטָן:


Two sons were born to Eiver, the name of one was Peleg, for in his days the earth[’s inhabitants] were dispersed, and his brothers name was Yaktan.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:26)


וְיָקְטָן אוֹלִיד יָת אַלְמוֹדָד וְיָת שָׁלֶף וְיָת חֲצַרְמָוֶת וְיָת יָרַח:


Yaktan produced Almodad, Shelef, Chatzarmaves, and Yerach,


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:27)


וְיָת הֲדוֹרָם וְיָת אוּזָל וְיָת דִּקְלָה:


[also] Hadoram, Uval, and Diklah,


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:28)


וְיָת עוֹבָל וְיָת אֲבִימָאֵל וְיָת שְׁבָא:


[also] Oval, Avimaeil, and Sheva,


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:29)


וְיָת אוֹפִר וְיָת חֲוִילָה וְיָת יוֹבָב כָּל אִלֵּין בְּנֵי יָקְטָן:


[also] Ophir, Chavilah, and Yovav. All these were sons of Yaktan.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:30)


וַהֲוָה מוֹתְבָנְהוֹן מִמֵּשָׁא מָטֵי לִסְפַר טוּר מָדִינְחָא:


Their dwelling place was from Meishah toward Sephor, the eastern mountain.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:31)


אִלֵּין בְּנֵי שֵׁם לְזַרְעֲיָתְהוֹן לְלִישָׁנְהוֹן לְאַרְעָתְהוֹן בְּעַמְמֵיהוֹן:


These are the sons of Sheim, according to their families and their languages, in their lands and in their nations.


###### Onkelos Genesis 10:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/10:32)


אִלֵּין זַרְעֲיַת בְּנֵי נֹחַ לְתוּלְדָתְהוֹן בְּעַמְמֵיהוֹן וּמֵאִלֵּין אִתַּפְרָשׁוּ עַמְמַיָּא בְּאַרְעָא בָּתַר טוֹפָנָא:


These are the families of the sons of Noach, according to their descendants and in their nations. From these the nations were separated on the earth after the Flood.



## Chapter 11

###### Onkelos Genesis 11:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:1)


וַהֲוָה כָל אַרְעָא לִישָׁן חָד וּמַמְלַל חָד:


The whole earth had one language, and conforming words.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:2)


וַהֲוָה בְּמִטַּלְהוֹן **בְּקַדְמֵיתָא** וְאַשְׁכָּחוּ בִּקְעֲתָא בְּאַרְעָא דְ**בָבֶל** וִיתִיבוּ תַמָּן:


When they journeyed from the east, [**at the beginning,**] they found a valley in the land of Shinar [**Babylon**] and they settled there.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:3)


וַאֲמָרוּ גְּבַר לְחַבְרֵיהּ הָבוּ **נִרְמֵי** לִבְנִין וְנִשְׂרְפִנּוּן **בְּנוּרָא** (יוֹקַדְתָּא) וַהֲוַת לְהוֹן לְבִנְתָּא לְאַבְנָא וְחֵימָרָא הֲוָה לְהוֹן לְשִׁיעַ:


They said one to another, Come, let us mold [**throw**] bricks and bake them thoroughly [**in a fire**]. They then had bricks to use as stone, and the clay for mortar.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:4)


וַאֲמָרוּ הָבוּ נִבְנֵי לָנָא קַרְתָּא וּמַגְדְּלָא וְרֵישֵׁיהּ מָטֵי עַד **צֵית** שְׁמַיָּא וְנַעְבֵּיד לָנָא שׁוּם דִּילְמָא נִתְבַּדַּר עַל אַפֵּי כָל אַרְעָא:


Then they said, Come, we will build ourselves a city, and a tower whose top will reach the [**height of the**] heavens. Thus we will make ourselves a name, so that we will not be scattered all over the face of the earth.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:5)


**וְאִתְגְּלִי יְיָ לְאִתְפְּרָעָא עַל עוֹבָדֵי** קַרְתָּא וּמַגְדְּלָא דִּי בְנוֹ בְּנֵי אֱנָשָׁא:


Adonoy descended to see [**Adonoy became revealed in order to punish them because of the building of**] the city and the tower that the sons of man built.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:6)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ הָא עַמָּא חַד וְלִישָׁן חַד לְכָלְּהוֹן וְדֵין דְּשָׁרִיוּ לְמֶעְבָּד וּכְעַן לָא יִתִּמְנַע מִנְהוֹן כֹּל דִּחַשִּׁיבוּ לְמֶעְבָּד:


Adonoy said, Behold, they are one people, all having one language, and this is their first undertaking. Now will nothing be unattainable for them, [to do] whatever they have a mind to do?


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:7)


הָבוּ **נִתְגְּלִי** וּנְבַלְבֵּל תַּמָּן לִישָׁנְהוֹן דִּלָא יִשְׁמְעוּן אֱנַשׁ (גְּבַר) לִישַׁן חַבְרֵיהּ:


Come, let us descend [**let us be revealed**] and jumble their language, that they will not understand one another’s language.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:8)


וּבַדַּר יְיָ יָתְהוֹן מִתַּמָּן עַל אַפֵּי כָל אַרְעָא וּמְנָעוּ לְמִבְנֵי (נ"י וְאִתְמְנָעוּ מִלְּמִבְנֵי) קַרְתָּא:


Adonoy scattered them from there all over the face of the earth, and they stopped building the city.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:9)


עַל כֵּן קְרָא שְׁמַהּ בָּבֶל אֲרֵי תַמָּן בַּלְבֵּל יְיָ לִישַׁן כָּל אַרְעָא וּמִתַּמָּן בַּדָּרִנּוּן יְיָ עַל אַפֵּי כָּל אַרְעָא:


Therefore He called its name, Bavel, for this was the place where Adonoy jumbled the language of all the earth; and from there, Adonoy scattered them all over the face of the earth.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:10)


אִלֵּין תּוּלְדַת שֵׁם שֵׁם בַּר מְאָה שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד יָת אַרְפַּכְשָׁד תַּרְתֵּין שְׁנִין בָּתַר טוֹפָנָא:


These are the histories of Sheim. Sheim was one hundred years old, and he had Arpachshad two years after the Flood.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:11)


וַחֲיָא שֵׁם בָּתַר דְּאוֹלִיד יָת אַרְפַּכְשָׁד חֲמֵשׁ מְאָה שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד בְּנִין וּבְנָן:


Sheim lived after he had Arpachshad, five hundred years, and he had sons and daughters.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:12)


וְאַרְפַּכְשַׁד חֲיָא תְּלָתִין וַחֲמֵשׁ שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד יָת שָׁלַח:


Arpachshad lived thirty-five years and had Shelach.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:13)


וַחֲיָא אַרְפַּכְשַׁד בָּתַר דְּאוֹלִיד יָת שֶׁלַח אַרְבַּע מְאָה וּתְלָת שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד בְּנִין וּבְנָן:


Arpachshad lived, after he had Shelach, four hundred and three years, and he had sons and daughters.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:14)


וְשֶׁלַח חֲיָא תְּלָתִין שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד יָת עֵבֶר:


Shelach lived thirty years and had Eiver.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:15)


וַחֲיָא שֶׁלַח בָּתַר דְּאוֹלִיד יָת עֵבֶר אַרְבַּע מְאָה וּתְלָת שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד בְּנִין וּבְנָן:


After he had Eiver, Shelach lived four hundred and three years, and he had sons and daughters.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:16)


וַחֲיָא עֵבֶר תְּלָתִין וְאַרְבַּע שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד יָת פָּלֶג:


Eiver lived thirty-four years and had Peleg.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:17)


וַחֲיָא עֵבֶר בָּתַר דְּאוֹלִיד יָת פֶּלֶג אַרְבַּע מְאָה וּתְלָתִין שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד בְּנִין וּבְנָן:


After he had Peleg, Eiver lived four hundred and thirty years, and he had sons and daughters.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:18)


וַחֲיָא פֶלֶג תְּלָתִין שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד יָת רְעוּ:


Peleg lived thirty years and had Re’u.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:19)


וַחֲיָא פֶלֶג בָּתַר דְּאוֹלִיד יָת רְעוּ מָאתָן וּתְשַׁע שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד בְּנִין וּבְנָן:


After he had Re’u, Peleg lived two hundred and nine years, and had sons and daughters.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:20)


וַחֲיָא רְעוּ תְּלָתִין וְתַרְתֵּין שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד יָת שְׂרוּג:


Re’u lived thirty-two years and had Serug.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:21)


וַחֲיָא רְעוּ בָּתַר דְּאוֹלִיד יָת שְׂרוּג מָאתָן וּשְׁבַע שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד בְּנִין וּבְנָן:


After he had Serug, Re’u lived two hundred and seven years, and had sons and daughters.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:22)


וַחֲיָא שְׂרוּג תְּלָתִין שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד יָת נָחוֹר:


Serug lived thirty years and had Nachor.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:23)


וַחֲיָא שְׂרוּג בָּתַר דְּאוֹלִיד יָת נָחוֹר מָאתָן שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד בְּנִין וּבְנָן:


After he had Nachor, Serug lived two hundred years, and had sons and daughters.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:24)


וַחֲיָא נָחוֹר עֶשְׂרִין וּתְשַׁע שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד יָת תָּרַח:


Nachor lived twenty-nine years and had Terach.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:25)


וַחֲיָא נָחוֹר בָּתַר דְּאוֹלִיד יָת תֶּרַח מְאָה וּתְשַׁע עֲשַׂר שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד בְּנִין וּבְנָן:


After he had Terach, Nachor lived one hundred and nineteen years, and had sons and daughters.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:26)


וַחֲיָא תֶרַח שִׁבְעִין שְׁנִין וְאוֹלִיד יָת אַבְרָם יָת נָחוֹר וְיָת הָרָן:


Terach lived seventy years and had Avram, Nachor and Haran.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:27)


וְאִלֵּין תּוּלְדַת תֶּרַח תֶּרַח אוֹלִיד יָת אַבְרָם יָת נָחוֹר וְיָת הָרָן וְהָרָן אוֹלִיד יָת לוֹט:


These are the histories of Terach. Terach produced Avram, Nachor, and Haran. Haran produced Lot.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:28)


וּמִית הָרָן עַל אַפֵּי תֶּרַח אֲבוּהִי בְּאַרְעָא דְיַלָּדוּתֵיהּ בְּאוּרָא דְכַסְדָּאֵי:


Haran died during the lifetime of his father Terach, in the land of his birth in Ur Kasdim.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:29)


וּנְסִיב אַבְרָם וְנָחוֹר לְהוֹן נְשִׁין שׁוּם אִתַּת אַבְרָם שָׂרָי וְשׁוּם אִתַּת נָחוֹר מִלְכָּה בַּת הָרָן אֲבוּהָא דְמִלְכָּה וַאֲבוּהָא דְיִסְכָּה:


Avram and Nachor married. The name of Avram’s wife was Sarai, and the name of Nachor’s wife was Milkah, the daughter of Haran [who was] the father of Milkah and Yiskah.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:30)


וַהֲוַת שָׂרַי עֲקָרָה לֵית לָהּ וְלָד:


Sarai was barren, she had no child.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:31)


וּדְבַר תֶּרַח יָת אַבְרָם בְּרֵיהּ וְיָת לוֹט בַּר הָרָן בַּר בְּרֵיהּ וְיָת שָׂרַי כַּלָּתֵיהּ אִתַּת אַבְרָם בְּרֵיהּ וּנְפָקוּ עִמְּהוֹן מֵאוּרָא דְכַסְדָּאֵי לְמֵיזַל לְאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן וַאֲתוֹ עַד חָרָן וִיתִיבוּ תַמָּן:


Terach took his son, Avram, and Lot, the son of Haran, his grandson, and Sarai, his daughter-in-law, the wife of his son, Avram. With them they departed from Ur Kasdim, to go to the land of Canaan. [When] they came to Charan [however], they settled there.


###### Onkelos Genesis 11:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/11:32)


וַהֲווֹ יוֹמֵי תֶרַח מָאתָן וַחֲמֵשׁ שְׁנִין וּמִית תֶּרַח בְּחָרָן:


The lifetime of Terach was two hundred and five years. Terach died in Charan.



## Chapter 12

###### Onkelos Genesis 12:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְאַבְרָם אִיזֵל לָךְ מֵאַרְעָךְ וּמִיַּלָּדוּתָךְ וּמִבֵּית אֲבוּךְ לְאַרְעָא דִּי אַחֲזִנָּךְ:


Adonoy said to Avram, Go from your land, from your birthplace, and from your father’s house, [and go] to the land that I will show you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:2)


וְאֶעְבְּדִנָּךְ לְעַם סַגִּי וֶאֱבָרֵכִנָּךְ וַאֲרַבֵּי שְׁמָךְ **וּתְהֵא מְבָרָךְ**:


I will make you into a great nation, I will bless you and make your name great; and you will be a blessing [*and you will be blessed*].


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:3)


וֶאֱבָרֵךְ מְבָרְכָךְ וּמְלַטְטָךְ אֵילוּט וְיִתְבָּרֲכוּן **בְּדִילָךְ** כֹּל זַרְעֲיַת אַרְעָא:


I will bless those who bless you, and he who curses you, I will curse; and through you [*and because of you*], will be blessed all the families of the earth.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:4)


וַאֲזַל אַבְרָם כְּמָא דִמַלֵּיל עִמֵּיהּ יְיָ וַאֲזַל עִמֵּיהּ לוֹט וְאַבְרָם בַּר שִׁבְעִין וַחֲמֵשׁ שְׁנִין בְּמִפְּקֵיהּ מֵחָרָן:


Avram went as Adonoy had told him, and Lot went with him. Avram was seventy-five years old when he left Charan.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:5)


וּדְבַר אַבְרָם יָת שָׂרַי אִתְּתֵיהּ וְיָת לוֹט בַּר אֲחוּהִי וְיָת כָּל קִנְיָנְהוֹן דִּי קְנוֹ וְיָת נַפְשָׁתָא דְּשַׁעְבִּידוּ לְאוֹרַיְתָא בְחָרָן וּנְפָקוּ לְמֵיזַל לְאַרְעָא דִכְנַעַן וַאֲתוֹ לְאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן:


Avram took his wife Sarai, Lot, his brother’s son, all their possessions they had acquired, and the souls [people] that they had made [converted] in Charan, and they set out to go to the land of Canaan. They came to the land of Canaan.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:6)


וַעֲבַר אַבְרָם בְּאַרְעָא עַד אֲתַר שְׁכֶם עַד מֵישַׁר מוֹרֶה וּכְנַעֲנָאָה בְּכֵן בְּאַרְעָא:


Avram passed [traveled] through the land until the place of Shechem, until the Plain of Moreh. The Canaanites were then in the land.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:7)


**וְאִתְגְּלִי** יְיָ לְאַבְרָם וַאֲמַר לִבְנָךְ אֶתֵּן יָת אַרְעָא הָדָא וּבְנָא תַמָּן מִדְבְּחָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ **דְּאִתְגְּלִי** לֵיהּ:


Adonoy appeared [*became revealed*] to Avram and said: To your descendants I will give this land. There he built an altar to [*before*] Adonoy Who had appeared [*been revealed*] to him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:8)


**וְאִסְתַּלַּק** מִתַּמָּן לְטוּרָא מִמַּדְנַח לְבֵית אֵל וּפְרַס מַשְׁכְּנֵיהּ בֵּית אֵל מִמַּעַרְבָא וְעַי מִמָּדִינְחָא וּבְנָא תַמָּן מִדְבְּחָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ **וְצַלִּי בִּשְׁמָא** דַיְיָ:


From there he moved [*ascended*] to the mountain that was east of Beis-Eil and set up his tent. Beis-Eil was to the west and Ai was to the east. There he built an altar to [*before*] Adonoy, and he proclaimed the Name, [*and he prayed in the Name,*] Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:9)


וּנְטַל אַבְרָם אָזֵל וְנָטֵל לְדָרוֹמָא:


Avram journeyed, traveling steadily southward.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:10)


וַהֲוָה כַפְנָא בְּאַרְעָא וּנְחַת אַבְרָם לְמִצְרַיִם לְאִתּוֹתָבָא תַמָּן אֲרֵי תַקִּיף כַּפְנָא בְּאַרְעָא:


There was a famine in the land. Avram went down to Egypt to live there temporarily, for the famine was severe in the land.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:11)


וַהֲוָה כַּד (נ"י אַרֵי) קְרֵיב לְמֵיעַל לְמִצְרָיִם וַאֲמַר לְשָׂרַי אִתְּתֵיהּ הָא כְעַן יְדָעִית אֲרֵי אִתְּתָא שַׁפִּירַת חֵיזוּ אָתְּ:


As he came near and was about to enter Egypt, he said to his wife Sarai, Behold, I now realize that you are a woman of beautiful appearance.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:12)


וִיהֵי כַּד יֶחֱזוּן יָתִיךְ מִצְרָאֵי וְיֵימְרוּן אִתְּתֵיהּ דָּא וְיִקְטְלוּן יָתִי וְיָתִיךְ יְקַיְמוּן:


It will happen when the Egyptians see you that they will say: This is his wife. They will kill me and let you live.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:13)


אִמְרִי **כְעַן** אֲחָתִי אָתְּ בְּדִיל דְּיֵיטַב לִי בְּדִילָךְ וְתִתְקַיֵּם נַפְשִׁי **בְּפִתְגָּמָיְכִי**:


Please [*Now*] say [therefore] that you are my sister, so that it will go well with me for your sake, and my life will be spared because of you [*through your words*].


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:14)


וַהֲוָה כַּד עַל אַבְרָם לְמִצְרָיִם וַחֲזוֹ מִצְרָאֵי יָת אִתְּתָא אֲרֵי שַׁפִּירָא הִיא לַחֲדָא:


And it was when Avram was entering Egypt, that the Egyptians beheld the woman, because she was very beautiful.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:15)


וַחֲזוֹ יָתַהּ רַבְרְבֵי פַרְעֹה וְשַׁבָּחוּ יָתַהּ לְפַרְעֹה וְאִדַּבָּרַת אִתְּתָא לְבֵית פַּרְעֹה:


Pharaoh’s officials saw her and praised her to Pharaoh. The woman was taken to Pharaoh’s house.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:16)


וּלְאַבְרָם אוֹטִיב בְּדִילַהּ וַהֲווֹ לֵיהּ עָאן וְתוֹרִין וַחֲמָרִין וְעַבְדִין וְאַמְהָן וְאַתְנָן וְגַמְלִין:


He treated Avram well for her sake. He thus acquired sheep, oxen, donkeys, male and female slaves, female donkeys and camels.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:17)


**וְאַיְתִי** יְיָ **עַל** פַּרְעֹה מַכְתָּשִׁין רַבְרְבִין וְעַל אֱנַשׁ בֵּיתֵיהּ עַל עֵיסַק שָׂרַי אִתַּת אַבְרָם:


Adonoy afflicted Pharaoh with severe plagues [*brought severe plagues upon Pharaoh*], and also his household, because of Sarai, Avram’s wife.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:18)


וּקְרָא פַרְעֹה לְאַבְרָם וַאֲמַר מָה דָא עֲבַדְתְּ לִי לְמָא לָא חַוֵּיתָא לִי אֲרֵי אִתְּתָךְ הִיא:


Pharaoh summoned Avram and said: What is this that you have done to me? Why did you not tell me that she is your wife?


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:19)


לְמָא אֲמַרְתְּ אֲחָתִי הִיא וּדְבָרִית יָתַהּ לִי לְאִתּוּ וּכְעַן הָא אִתְּתָךְ דְּבַר וְאִיזֵיל:


Why did you say, She is my sister, so that I should take her to myself for a wife? Now here is your wife, take [her] and go.


###### Onkelos Genesis 12:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/12:20)


וּפַקֵּיד עֲלוֹהִי פַּרְעֹה גּוּבְרִין וְאַלְוִיאוּ יָתֵיהּ וְיָת אִתְּתֵיהּ וְיָת כָּל דִּילֵיהּ:


Pharaoh assigned men to him and they escorted him and his wife and all that was his.



## Chapter 13

###### Onkelos Genesis 13:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:1)


וּסְלִיק אַבְרָם מִמִּצְרַיִם הוּא וְאִתְּתֵיהּ וְכָל דִּילֵיהּ וְלוֹט עִמֵּיהּ לְדָרוֹמָא:


Avram went up from Egypt; he, his wife, and all that he had, together with Lot into the south.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:2)


וְאַבְרָם תַּקִּיף לַחֲדָא בִּבְעִירָא בְּכַסְפָּא וּבְדַהֲבָא:


Avram was very wealthy in livestock, silver and gold.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:3)


וַאֲזַל לְמַטְלָנוֹהִי מִדָּרוֹמָא וְעַד בֵּית אֵל עַד אַתְרָא דִּי **פְרַס** תַּמָּן מַשְׁכְּנֵיהּ בְּקַדְמֵיתָא בֵּין בֵּית אֵל וּבֵין עָי:


He continued on his travels from the south to Beis-Eil, until the place where he originally had [*set up*] his tent, between Beis-Eil and Ai.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:4)


לַאֲתַר מַדְבְּחָא דִּי עֲבַד תַּמָּן בְּקַדְמֵיתָא **וְצַלִּי** תַמָּן אַבְרָם בִּשְׁמָא דַיְיָ:


To the site of the altar which he made there at first; and there Avram called [*prayed*] in the Name of Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:5)


וְאַף לְלוֹט דְּאָזֵיל עִם אַבְרָם הֲוָה עָאן וְתוֹרִין וּמַשְׁכְּנִין:


Lot who accompanied Avram also had sheep, cattle and tents.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:6)


וְלָא סוֹבָרַת יָתְהוֹן אַרְעָא לְמִיתַב כַּחֲדָא אֲרֵי הֲוָה קִנְיָנְהוֹן סַגִּי וְלָא יְכִילוּ לְמִיתַב כַּחֲדָא:


The land was unable to support them that they might dwell together, for their wealth was so great that they were unable to dwell together.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:7)


וַהֲוַת מַצּוּתָא בֵּין רָעֵי בְּעִירֵיהּ דְּאַבְרָם וּבֵין רָעֵי בְּעִירֵיהּ דְּלוֹט וּכְנַעֲנָאָה וּפְרִזָּאָה בְּכֵן יָתִיב בְּאַרְעָא:


There was a quarrel between the herdsmen of Avram’s flocks and the herdsmen of Lot’s flocks. The Canaanites and the Perizzites were then living in the land.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:8)


וַאֲמַר אַבְרָם לְלוֹט לָא **כְעַן** תְּהֵי מַצּוּתָא בֵּינָא וּבֵינָךְ וּבֵין רַעֲוָתִי וּבֵין רַעֲוָתָךְ אֲרֵי גוּבְרִין אַחִין אֲנָחְנָא:


Avram said to Lot: Please [*Now*], let there not be strife between me and you, and between my herdsmen and your herdsmen, for we are kinsmen.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:9)


הֲלָא כָל אַרְעָא קֳדָמָךְ אִתְפְּרַשׁ **כְּעַן** מִלְּוָתִי אִם אַתְּ **לְצִפּוּנָא** וַאֲנָא **לְדָרוֹמָא** וְאִם אַתְּ **לְדָרוֹמָא** וַאֲנָא **לְצִפּוּנָא**:


Is not all the land before you? Please [*Now*] separate from me, if [you go] to the left [*north*], I [will go] to the right [*south*], and if [you go] to the right [*south*], I [will go] to the left [*north*].


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:10)


וּזְקַף לוֹט יָת עֵינוֹהִי וַחֲזָא יָת כָּל מֵישַׁר יַרְדְּנָא אֲרֵי כֻלַּהּ **בֵּית** שַׁקְיָא קֳדָם חַבָּלוּת יְיָ יָת סְדוֹם וְיָת עֲמֹרָה כְּגִינְתָא דַיְיָ כְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם מָטֵי לְצֹעַר:


Lot raised his eyes and saw the entire Jordan Plain, that it was [*an*] abundantly watered [*place*]; before Adonoy destroyed Sedom and Amorah, it was like Adonoy’s garden, like the land of Egypt, going towards Zoar.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:11)


וּבְחַר לֵיהּ לוֹט יָת כָּל מֵישַׁר יַרְדְּנָא וּנְטַל לוֹט **מִלְּקַדְמִין** וְאִתַּפְרָשׁוּ גְּבַר מִלְּוַת אֲחוּהִי:


Lot chose for himself the entire Jordan Plain; Lot journeyed from the east [*before*], and they separated one from another.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:12)


אַבְרָם יָתֵב בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן וְלוֹט יָתֵב בְּקִירְוֵי מֵישְׁרָא וּפְרַס עַד סְדוֹם:


Avram lived in the land of Canaan; and Lot lived in the cities of the Plain, setting up his tents as far as Sedom.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:13)


וֶאֱנָשִׁין (ס"א וַאֲנָשֵׁי) דִּסְדוֹם בִּישִׁין **בְּמָמוֹנְהוֹן** וְחַיָּבִין **בִּגְוִיָּתְהוֹן** **קֳדָם** יְיָ לַחֲדָא:


The people of Sedom were wicked [*with their property*] and sinful [*with their bodies*] towards [*before*] Adonoy, exceedingly.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:14)


וַיְיָ אֲמַר לְאַבְרָם בָּתַר דְּאִתְפָּרֶשׁ לוֹט מֵעִמֵּיהּ זְקוֹף כְּעַן עֵינָךְ וַחֲזֵי מִן אַתְרָא דִּאַתְּ תַּמָּן לְצִפּוּנָא וּלְדָרוֹמָא וּלְמָדִינְחָא וּלְמַעֲרָבָא:


Adonoy said to Avram after Lot had separated from him: Raise your eyes and look about from the place where you are, to the north, to the south to the east and to the west.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:15)


אֲרֵי יָת כָּל אַרְעָא דִּי אַתְּ חָזֵי לָךְ אֶתְּנִנַּהּ וְלִבְנָךְ עַד עָלָם:


For all the land that you see I give to you and to your descendants forever.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:16)


וַאֲשַׁוֵּי יָת בְּנָךְ **סַגִּיאִין כְּעַפְרָא דְאַרְעָא כְּמָא דִּי לָא אֶפְשַׁר לִגְבַר לְמִמְנֵי יָת עַפְרָא דְאַרְעָא אַף בְּנָךְ לָא יִתִּמְנוּן**:


I will make your descendants [*numerous*] as the dust of the earth; so that if a man can count the dust of the earth, then your descendants too will be countable [*just as it is impossible for a man to count the dust of the earth, so too your descendants will not be countable*].


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:17)


קוּם הַלִּיךְ בְּאַרְעָא לְאָרְכַּהּ וְלִפְתָיַהּ אֲרֵי לָךְ אֶתְּנִנַּהּ:


Rise, walk through the land—its length and breadth—for to you I will give it.


###### Onkelos Genesis 13:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/13:18)


וּפְרַס אַבְרָם וַאֲתָא וִיתֵב בְּמֵישְׁרֵי מַמְרֵא דִּי בְחֶבְרוֹן וּבְנָא תַמָּן מִדְבְּחָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ:


Avram set up his tent, and he came and settled in the Plains of Mamrei which are in Chevron, and he built there an altar to [*before*] Adonoy.



## Chapter 14

###### Onkelos Genesis 14:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:1)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמֵי אַמְרָפֶל מַלְכָּא **דְבָבֶל** אַרְיוֹךְ מַלְכָּא דְאֶלָּסָר כְּדָרְלָעֹמֶר מַלְכָּא דְעֵילָם וְתִדְעָל **מַלְכָּא דְעַמְמֵי**:


This happened in the days of Amraphel, king of Shinar [*Babylon*], Aryoch, king of Elasar, Kedorlaomer, king of Ailam, and Tidal, king of Goyim [*king of peoples*].


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:2)


סְדָרוּ (נ"י עֲבַדוּ) קְרָבָא עִם בֶּרַע מַלְכָּא דִסְדֹם וְעִם בִּרְשַׁע מַלְכָּא דַעֲמֹרָה שִׁנְאָב מַלְכָּא דְאַדְמָה וְשֶׁמְאֵבֶר מַלְכָּא דִצְבוֹיִם וּמַלְכָּא דְבֶלַע הִיא צֹעַר:


They waged war against Bera, king of Sedom, Birsha, king of Amorah, Shinav, king of Admah, Shemeiver, king of Tzevoyim, and the king of Bela, which is Zoar.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:3)


כָּל אִלֵּין **אִתְכְּנָשׁוּ לְמֵישַׁר חַקְלַיָּא** הוּא **אֲתַר** יַמָּא דְמִלְחָא:


All these joined together [*assembled*] at the valley of Siddim [*the plain of the fields*], which is [now] [*the place of*] the Salt Sea.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:4)


תַּרְתֵּא עַשְׂרֵי שְׁנִין פְּלָחוּ יָת כְּדָרְלָעֹמֶר וּתְלָת עַשְׂרֵי שְׁנִין מְרָדוּ:


[For] twelve years they had served Kedorlaomer, and for thirteen years they rebelled.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:5)


וּבְאַרְבַּע עַשְׂרֵי שְׁנִין אֲתָא כְדָרְלָעֹמֶר וּמַלְכַיָּא דְּעִמֵּיהּ וּמְחוֹ יָת **גִּבָּרַיָּא** דִּי בְעַשְׁתְּרוֹת קַרְנַיִם וְיָת **תַּקִּיפַיָּא** דִּבְהֶמְתָּא וְיָת **אֵימְתָנֵי** דִּבְשָׁוֵה קִרְיָתָיִם:


In the fourteenth year, Kedorlaomer came together with the kings that were with him and they defeated the Rephaim [*the mighty ones*] in Ashteros Karnayim, the Zuzim [*the powerful ones*] in Ham, the Aimim [*the ones that incite fear*] at Shaveh Kiryasayim,


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:6)


וְיָת חוֹרָאֵי דִּי בְטוּרְהוֹן דְּשֵׂעִיר עַד **מֵישַׁר** פָּארָן **דִּסְמִיךְ עַל** מַדְבְּרָא:


And the Chorites in their hills of Seir, as far as Eil [*the Plain of*] Paran which borders [*is next to*] the wilderness.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:7)


וְתָבוּ וַאֲתוּ לְ**מֵישַׁר פִּלּוּג דִּינָא** הִיא **רְקָם** וּמְחוֹ יָת כָּל חֲקַל עֲמַלְקָאָה וְאַף יָת אֱמוֹרָאָה דְּיָתֵיב **בְּעֵין גֶּדִי**:


They turned back and came to Ein Mishpat [*the plain of compromise*] which is Kadeish [*Rekem*], and they struck all the territory of Amaleik, as well as the Emorites who lived in Chazazon Tamar [*Ein Gedi*].


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:8)


וּנְפַק מַלְכָּא דִסְדוֹם וּמַלְכָּא דַעֲמוֹרָה וּמַלְכָּא דְאַדְמָה וּמַלְכָּא דִצְבוֹיִם וּמַלְכָּא דְבֶלַע הִיא צוֹעַר וְסַדָּרוּ עִמְּהוֹן קְרָבָא **בְּמֵישַׁר חַקְלַיָּא**:


The king of Sedom set out, along with the king of Amorah, the king of Admah, the king of Tzevoyim, and the king of Bela, which is Zoar, and engaged them in battle in the Valley of Siddim [*in the plain of the fields*].


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:9)


עִם כְּדָרְלָעֹמֶר מַלְכָּא דְעֵילָם וְתִדְעָל מַלְכָּא **דְעַמְמִין** וְאַמְרָפֶל מַלְכָּא **דְבָבֶל** וְאַרְיוֹךְ מַלְכָּא דְאֶלָּסָר אַרְבְּעָה מַלְכִין לָקֳבֵיל חַמְשָׁא:


Against Kedorlaomer, king of Eilam, Sidal, king of Goyim [*king of peoples*], Amraphel, king of Shinar [*Babylon*], and Aryoch, king of Elasar; [there were] four kings against the five.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:10)


**וּמֵישַׁר חַקְלַיָּא** בֵּירִין **בֵּירִין מַסְקַן חֵימָרָא** וַעֲרָקוּ מַלְכָּא דִסְדוֹם וַעֲמוֹרָה וּנְפָלוּ תַמָּן וּדְאִשְׁתָּאָרוּ לְטוּרָא עֲרָקוּ:


The Valley of Siddim [*The plain of the fields*] was [full of] mortar pits [*pits from which they raise up mortar*]; [and when] the Kings of Sedom and Amorah fled they fell there [into the pits], while the remainder fled to the mountains.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:11)


**וּשְׁבוֹ** יָת כָּל קִנְיָנָא דִסְדוֹם וַעֲמוֹרָה וְיָת כָּל מֵיכָלְהוֹן וַאֲזָלוּ:


They took [*captured*] all the wealth of Sedom and Amorah and all their provisions and they went on their way.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:12)


**וּשְׁבוֹ** יָת לוֹט וְיָת קִנְיָנֵיהּ בַּר אֲחוּהִי דְאַבְרָם וַאֲזָלוּ וְהוּא יָתֵב בִּסְדוֹם:


They took [*captured*] Lot and his wealth. [Lot was] Avram’s nephew. Then they went on their way. He had been living in Sedom.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:13)


וַאֲתָא מְשֵׁיזָבָא וְחַוִּי לְאַבְרָם עִבְרָאָה וְהוּא שָׁרֵי בְּמֵישְׁרֵי מַמְרֵא אֱמוֹרָאָה אֲחוּהִי דְאֶשְׁכּוֹל וַאֲחוּהִי דְעָנֵר וְאִנּוּן אֱנָשֵׁי קְיָמֵיהּ דְּאַבְרָם:


The one who had escaped came and told Avram the Ivri, who was living in the Plains of Mamrei the Emorite, the brother of Eshkol and Aneir. They were Avram’s allies.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:14)


וּשְׁמַע אַבְרָם אֲרֵי אִשְׁתְּבִי אֲחוּהִי וְזָרֵיז יָת **עוּלֵמוֹהִי** יְלִידֵי בֵיתֵיהּ תְּלָת מְאָה וְתַמְנֵי עֲסַר וּרְדַף עַד דָּן:


When Avram heard that his kinsman was taken captive, he armed his trained servants [*lads*] who were born in his household, three hundred and eighteen [men], and he pursued [them] until Dan.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:15)


וְאִתְפְּלֵג עֲלֵיהוֹן לֵילְיָא הוּא וְעַבְדוֹהִי וּמְחוֹנוּן וּרְדָפִנּוּן עַד חוֹבָה דִּי **מִצִּפּוּנָא** לְדַמָּשֶׂק:


He divided [his men] against them at night, he and his servants and he attacked them. He pursued them until Chovah, which is to the left [*north*] of Damascus.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:16)


וַאֲתֵב יָת כָּל קִנְיָנָא וְאַף יָת לוֹט **בַּר אֲחוּהִי** וְקִנְיָנֵיהּ אֲתֵיב וְאַף יָת נְשַׁיָּא וְיָת עַמָּא:


He brought back all the property. His kinsman [*His brother’s son*] Lot and his possessions he also brought back, as well as the women and the people.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:17)


וּנְפַק מַלְכָּא דִסְדוֹם לְקַדָּמוּתֵיהּ בָּתַר דְּתָב מִלְּמִמְחֵי יָת כְּדָרְלָעוֹמֶר וְיָת מַלְכַיָּא דִּי עִמֵּיהּ **לְמֵישַׁר מַפְנָא הוּא אֲתַר בֵּית רֵיסָא דְמַלְכָּא**:


The King of Sedom went out to meet him after his return from defeating Kedorlaomer and the kings that were with him, to the Valley of Shaveh which is the King’s Valley [*to the barren plain, which is the place of the racetrack of the king*].


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:18)


וּמַלְכִּי צֶדֶק מַלְכָּא **דִירוּשְׁלֶם** אַפֵּיק לְחֵם וַחֲמָר וְהוּא **מְשַׁמֵּשׁ** קֳדָם אֵל עִלָּאָה:


Malki Zedek, king of Shaleim [*Yerushalayim*], brought out bread and wine. He was a Kohein of [*He was serving before*] the Most High Almighty.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:19)


וּבָרְכֵיהּ וַאֲמָר בְּרִיךְ אַבְרָם לְאֵל עִלָּאָה דִּקִנְיָנֵיהּ שְׁמַיָּא וְאַרְעָא:


He blessed him and said: Blessed be Avram to the Most High, Almighty Possessor of heaven and earth.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:20)


וּבְרִיךְ אֵל עִלָּאָה דִּמְסַר סָנְאָךְ בִּידָךְ וִיהַב לֵיהּ חַד מִן עַסְרָא מִכֹּלָּא:


And blessed be the Most High Almighty Who has delivered your enemies into your hand. [Avram] gave him a tenth of everything.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:21)


וַאֲמַר מַלְכָּא דִסְדוֹם לְאַבְרָם הַב לִי נַפְשָׁתָא וְקִנְיָנָא דְּבַר (נ"א סַב) לָךְ:


The King of Sedom said to Avram: Give me the people, and take the possessions for yourself.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:22)


וַאֲמַר אַבְרָם לְמַלְכָּא דִסְדוֹם אֲרֵימִית יְדַי **בִּצְלוֹ קֳדָם** יְיָ **קֳדָם** אֵל עִלָּאָה דִּקִנְיָנֵיהּ שְׁמַיָּא וְאַרְעָא:


Avram said to the King of Sedom: I have lifted my hand [*in prayer*] to [*before*] Adonoy [*before*] the Most High Almighty, Possessor of heaven and earth.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:23)


אִם מִחוּטָא וְעַד עַרְקַת מְסָנָא וְאִם אֶסַּב מִכָּל דִּי לָךְ וְלָא תֵימַר אֲנָא אַעְתָּרִית יָת אַבְרָם:


Neither a thread nor a shoelace! And I will not take anything of yours, so you will not [be able] to say, I have made Avram wealthy.


###### Onkelos Genesis 14:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/14:24)


לְחוֹד (נ"א בַּר) מִדִּאֲכָלוּ עוּלֵמַיָּא וְחוּלַק גֻּבְרַיָּא דִּי אֲזָלוּ עִמִּי עָנֵר אֶשְׁכֹּל וּמַמְרֵא אִנּוּן יְקַבְּלוּן חֲלָקְהוֹן:


I am excluding myself; only what the lads have eaten, and the portion of the men who went with me, Aneir, Eshkol and Mamrei, they may take their portion.



## Chapter 15

###### Onkelos Genesis 15:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:1)


בָּתַר פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין הֲוָה פִּתְגָּמָא דַיְיָ עִם אַבְרָם בִּנְבוּאָה לְמֵימָר לָא תִדְחַל אַבְרָם **מֵימְרִי תְּקוֹף לָךְ** אַגְרָךְ סַגִּי לַחֲדָא:


After these events, the word of Adonoy came to Avram in a vision, saying: Fear not Avram, I am your shield [*My Word is your strength*], your reward is very great.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:2)


וַאֲמַר אַבְרָם יְיָ אֱלֹּהִים מַה תִּתֶּן לִי וַאֲנָא אָזֵיל בְּלָא וְלָד **וּבַר פַּרְנָסָא הָדֵין דִּבְבֵיתִי** הוּא דַּמַּשְׂקָאָה אֱלִיעֶזֶר:


Avram said: My Master, God, what will You give me since I continue to be childless, and the manager of my household [*this supplier who is in my house*] is Eliezer of Damascus.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:3)


וַאֲמַר אַבְרָם הָא לִי לָא יְהַבְתְּ וְלָד וְהָא בַר בֵּיתִי יָרִית יָתִי:


And Avram said: Behold, You have not given me children, and thus one of my household will be my heir.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:4)


וְהָא פִּתְגָּמָא דַּיְיָ עִמֵּיהּ לְמֵימַר לָא יִרְתִנָּךְ דֵּין אֱלָהֵין **בַּר דְּתוֹלִיד** הוּא יִרְתִנָּךְ:


Suddenly the word of Adonoy came to him, saying: That one will not be your heir; only he that will come from within your body [*a son that you will beget*] will be your heir.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:5)


וְאַפֵּיק יָתֵיהּ לְבָרָא וַאֲמַר אִסְתְּכִי כְּעַן לִשְׁמַיָּא וּמְנֵי כּוֹכְבַיָּא אִם תִּכּוֹל לְמִמְנֵי יָתְהוֹן וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ כְּדֵין יְהוֹן בְּנָךְ:


He [then] took him outside and said: Look towards the heavens and count the stars if you are able to count them. He then said to him: So [numerous] will your descendants be.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:6)


וְהֵימִין **בְּמֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ וְחָשְׁבַהּ לֵיהּ לְזָכוּ:


He believed in [*the Word of*] Adonoy, and this He accounted to him for righteousness.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:7)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ אֲנָא יְיָ דִּאַפֵּקְתָּךְ מֵאוּרָא דְכַשְׂדָּאֵי לְמִּתַּן לָךְ יָת אַרְעָא הָדָא לְמֵירְתַהּ:


He said to him: I am Adonoy who brought you out of Ur Kasdim, to give to you this land as an inheritance.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:8)


וַאֲמָר יְיָ אֱלֹּהִים בְּמָה אִדַּע אֲרֵי אֵרְתִינַהּ:


And he said: My Master, God, how will I know that I will inherit it?


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:9)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ **קָרֵב קֳדָמַי** עֶגְלִין תְּלָתָא וְעִזִּין תְּלָתָא וְדִכְרִין תְּלָת וְשַׁפְנִינָא וּבַר יוֹנָא:


He said to him: Bring Me [*Sacrifice before Me*] three heifers, three goats and three rams, a dove, and a young pigeon.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:10)


**וְקָרֵב קֳדָמוֹהִי** יָת כָּל אִלֵּין **וּפַלִּיג יָתְהוֹן בְּשָׁוֶה** וִיהַב פַּלְגַיָּא פִּלּוּג לָקֳבֵל חַבְרֵיהּ וְיָת עוֹפָא לָא פַלִּיג:


He brought [*sacrificed*] all these to [*before*] Him; he split them in the middle [*into two equal parts*], and placed each piece opposite the other. The bird, however, he did not split.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:11)


וּנְחַת עוֹפָא עַל פַּגְלַיָּא וְאַפְרַח יָתְהוֹן אַבְרָם:


Birds of prey [vultures] descended upon the carcasses, but Avram drove them away.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:12)


וַהֲוָה שִׁמְשָׁא לְמֵיעַל וְשִׁנְתָא נְפָלַת עַל אַבְרָם וְהָא אֵימָה קְבַל סַגִּי נְפָלַת עֲלוֹהִי:


As the sun was setting, a deep sleep fell upon Avram; and behold, a dread of deep darkness fell upon him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:13)


וַאֲמַר לְאַבְרָם מִדַּע תִּדַּע אֲרֵי דַיָּרִין יְהוֹן בְּנָךְ בְּאַרְעָא דְלָא דִילְהוֹן וְיִפְלְחוּן בְּהוֹן וִיעַנּוּן יָתְהוֹן אַרְבַּע מְאָה שְׁנִין:


And He [Adonoy] said to Avram: Know for sure that your descendants will be foreigners in a land that is not theirs. They will enslave them and oppress them [for] four hundred years.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:14)


וְאַף יָת עַמָּא דִּיִפְלְחוּן בְּהוֹן דָּיִין אֲנָא וּבָתַר כֵּן יִפְקוּן בְּקִנְיָנָא סַגִּי:


But also that nation whom they will serve I will judge; afterwards they will leave with great wealth.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:15)


וְאַתְּ תֵּיעוֹל לְוַת אֲבָהָתָךְ בִּשְׁלָם תִּתְקְבַר בְּסֵיבוּ טָבָא:


You will join your fathers in peace; you will be buried at a good old age.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:16)


וְדָרָא רְבִיעָאָה יְתוּבוּן הָכָא אֲרֵי לָא שְׁלִים חוֹבָא דֶּאֱמוֹרָאָה עַד **כְּעָן**:


The fourth generation will return here, for the sin of the Emorites will not be complete until then [*now*].


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:17)


וַהֲוָה שִׁמְשָׁא עַלַּת וְקַבְלָא הֲוָה וְהָא תַנּוּר דִתְנַן וּבְעוּר דְּאֶשָּׁתָא דִּי עֲדָא בֵּין פַּלְגַיָּא הָאִלֵּין:


The sun set and it became very dark; and behold, a smoking furnace and a flaming torch had passed between these pieces.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:18)


בְּיוֹמָא הַהוּא גְּזַר יְיָ עִם אַבְרָם קְיָם לְמֵימָר לִבְנָךְ יְהָבִית יָת אַרְעָא הָדָא מִן נַהֲרָא דְמִצְרַיִם וְעַד נַהֲרָא רַבָּא נַהֲרָא פְרָת:


On that day Adonoy made a covenant with Avram, saying: To your descendants I have given this land, from the river of Egypt as far as the great river, the Euphrates.


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:19)


יָת **שַׁלְמָאֵי** וְיָת קְנִזָּאֵי וְיָת קַדְמוֹנָאֵי:


[The lands of] the Kenites [*Shalmites*], Kenizites, Kadmonites,


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:20)


וְיָת חִתָּאֵי וְיָת פְּרִזָּאֵי וְיָת **גִּבָּרָאֵי**:


The Chittites, Perizites, the Refa’im [*the mighty ones*],


###### Onkelos Genesis 15:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/15:21)


וְיָת אֱמוֹרָאֵי וְיָת כְּנַעֲנָאֵי וְיָת גִּרְגָּשָׁאֵי וְיָת יְבוּסָאֵי:


The Emorites, Canaanites Girgashites and Yevusites.



## Chapter 16

###### Onkelos Genesis 16:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:1)


וְשָׂרַי אִתַּת אַבְרָם לָא יְלִידַת לֵיהּ וְלַהּ אַמְתָא מִצְרֵיתָא וּשְׁמַהּ הָגָר:


Avram’s wife Sarai had not borne him children. She had an Egyptian handmaid whose name was Hagar.


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:2)


וַאֲמַרַת שָׂרַי לְאַבְרָם הָא כְּעַן מַנְעַנִי יְיָ מִלְּמֵילַד עוּל **כְּעַן** לְוַת אַמְתִי מָאִם אִתְבְּנֵי מִנַּהּ וְקַבִּיל אַבְרָם לְמֵימַר שָׂרָי:


Sarai said to Avram: See now, Adonoy has restrained me from having children; pray [*now*], come to my handmaid perhaps I will be built-up through her. Avram hearkened to the voice of Sarai.


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:3)


וּדְבָרַת שָׂרַי אִתַּת אַבְרָם יָת הָגָר מִצְרֵיתָא אַמְתַהּ מִסּוֹף עֲשַׂר שְׁנִין לְמִתַּב אַבְרָם בְּאַרְעָא דִּכְנָעַן וִיהָבַת יָתַהּ לְאַבְרָם בַּעְלַהּ לֵיהּ לְאִנְתּוּ:


Sarai, the wife of Avram took Hagar the Egyptian, her handmaid, at the end of ten years that Avram had lived in the Land of Canaan and gave her to Avram her husband to be his wife.


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:4)


וְעַל לְוַת הָגָר וְעַדִּיאַת וַחֲזַת אֲרֵי עַדִּיאַת וּקְלַת רִבָּנְתַּהּ בְּעֵינָהַהּ:


He came to Hagar and she conceived. When she realized that she was pregnant, her mistress became slighted in her eyes.


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:5)


וַאֲמַרַת שָׂרַי לְאַבְרָם **דִּין לִי עֲלָךְ** אֲנָא יְהָבִית אַמְתִי לָךְ וַחֲזַת אֲרֵי עַדִּיאַת וּקְלֵית בְּעֵינָהַהּ יְדִין יְיָ בֵּינִי וּבֵינָךְ:


Sarai said to Avram: The insult against me is your fault [*I have a judgment against you*]. I gave my maid to you and when she saw that she had conceived, I became slighted in her eyes. Let Adonoy judge between me and you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:6)


וַאֲמַר אַבְרָם לְשָׂרַי הָא אַמְתִיךְ בִּידִיךְ עִבִידִי לַהּ כִּדְתַקִּין בְּעֵינָיְכִי וְעַנִיתַהּ שָׂרַי וְעָרְקַת מִקֳּדָמָהַהּ:


Avram said to Sarai: [The fate of] your maid is in your hands, do with her as you see fit. Sarai dealt harshly with her, and she [Hagar] ran away from her.


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:7)


וְאַשְׁכְּחַהּ מַלְאָכָא דַיְיָ עַל עֵינָא דְמַיָּא בְּמַדְבְּרָא עַל עֵינָא בְּאָרְחָא **דְחַגְרָא**:


An angel of Adonoy found her by a spring of water in the desert, at the spring on the road to Shur [*Chagar*].


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:8)


וַאֲמַר הָגָר אַמְתָא דְשָׂרַי מְנָן אַתְּ אָתְיָא וּלְאָן אַתְּ אָזְלָא וַאֲמַרַת מִן קֳדָם שָׂרַי רִבָּנְתִּי אֲנָא עָרְקָא (נ"י עֲרָקַת):


And he said: Hagar, handmaid of Sarai, from where have you come and where are you going? She said: I am running away from my mistress, Sarai.


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:9)


וַאֲמַר לַהּ מַלְאֲכָא דַיְיָ תּוּבִי לְוַת רִבּוֹנְתִּךְ וְאִשְׁתַּעְבְּדִי תְּחוֹת יְדָהָא:


The angel of Adonoy said to her: Return to your mistress and submit yourself to her.


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:10)


וַאֲמַר לַהּ מַלְאֲכָא דַיְיָ אַסְגָּאָה אַסְגֵּי יָת בְּנָיְכִי וְלָא יִתִּמְנוּן מִסְּגֵי:


The angel of Adonoy said to her: I will greatly multiply your descendants that they can not be counted because of their great number.


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:11)


וַאֲמַר לַהּ מַלְאֲכָא דַיְיָ הָא אַתְּ מְעַדְיָא וְתֵלְדִין בָּר וְתִקְרִין שְׁמֵיהּ יִשְׁמָעֵאל אֲרֵי קַבִּיל יְיָ צְלוֹתִיךְ:


The angel of Adonoy said to her: Behold you will conceive, and give birth to a son. You shall name him Yishmael, for Adonoy has heard [*accepted*] your prayer.


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:12)


וְהוּא יְהֵא **מָרוֹד בֶּאֱנָשָׁא הוּא יְהֵא צְרִיךְ לְכֹלָּא וִידָא דִבְנֵי אֲנָשָׁא יְהוֹן צְרִיכִין לֵיהּ** וְעַל אַפֵּי כָל אֲחוֹהִי יִשְׁרֵי:


He will be a wild, uncivilized man [*rebel against people*]. His hand [will be] against everyone [*He will need everyone*] and everyone’s hand will be against him [*and everyone’s hand will need him*]; and in the presence of all his brothers he will dwell.


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:13)


**וְצַלִּיאַת** בִּשְׁמָא דַיְיָ דְּמִתְמַלֵּל עִמַּהּ **אֲמַרַת** אַתְּ הוּא אֱלָהָא דְחָזֵי **כֹלָּא אֲרֵי אֲמַרַת**הַבְרַם הָכָא (נ"י הַאַף אֲנָא) **שָׁרֵתִי חָזְיָא בָּתַר דְּאִתְגְּלִי לִי**:


She called [*She prayed in*] the Name Adonoy Who had spoken to her: [*She said*] You are Almighty Who sees [*everything*], for she said: Have I also seen here [a vestige] after my having seen? [*Have I also began to see here [a vestige] after it was revealed to me?*]


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:14)


עַל כֵּן קְרָא לִבְאֵרָא **בְּאֵרָא דְמַלְאַךְ קַיָּמָא אִתַּחֲזִי עֲלַהּ** הָא (הִיא) בֵּין רְקָם וּבֵין **חַגְרָא**:


Therefore the well was called: Be’er Lachai Ro’i [*the well at which a living angel appeared*]; it is between Kadeish [*Rekem*] and Bared [*Chagar*].


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:15)


וִילִידַת הָגָר לְאַבְרָם בָּר וּקְרָא אַבְרָם שׁוּם בְּרֵיהּ דִּילִידַת הָגָר יִשְׁמָעֵאל:


Hagar bore Avram a son and Avram named his son, that Hagar bore him, Yishmael.


###### Onkelos Genesis 16:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/16:16)


וְאַבְרָם בַּר תְּמָנָן וְשִׁתְּ שְׁנִין כַּד יְלִידַת הָגָר יָת יִשְׁמָעֵאל לְאַבְרָם:


Avram was eighty-six years old when Hagar bore Yishmael to Avram.



## Chapter 17

###### Onkelos Genesis 17:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:1)


וַהֲוָה אַבְרָם בַּר תִּשְׁעִין וּתְשַׁע שְׁנִין **וְאִתְגְּלִי** יְיָ לְאַבְרָם וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ אֲנָא אֵל שַׁדַּי **פְּלַח** קֳדָמַי וֶהֱוֵי שְׁלִים:


When Avram was ninety-nine years old, Adonoy appeared [*became revealed*] to Avram and said to him: I am Almighty Shaddai, walk [*worship*] before Me and be perfect.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:2)


וְאֶתֵּן קְיָמִי בֵּין **מֵימְרִי** וּבֵינָךְ וְאַסְגֵּי יָתָךְ לַחֲדָא לַחֲדָא:


I will give My covenant between Me [*My Word*] and you, and I will multiply you[r descendants] exceedingly.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:3)


וּנְפַל אַבְרָם עַל אַפּוֹהִי וּמַלֵּיל עִמֵּיהּ יְיָ לְמֵימָר:


Avram fell on his face. Elohim spoke with him, saying:


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:4)


אֲנָא הָא (גְזַר בנ"י איננו) קְיָמִי עִמָּךְ וּתְהֵי לְאַב סַגִּי עַמְמִין:


As for Me, here is My covenant with you; you shall be the father of a multitude of nations.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:5)


וְלָא יִתְקְרֵי עוֹד יָת שְׁמָךְ אַבְרָם וִיהֵי שְׁמָךְ אַבְרָהָם אֲרֵי אַב סַגִּי עַמְמִין יְהָבִתָּךְ:


No longer shall your name be called Avram, but your name shall be Avraham, for the father of a multitude of nations I have appointed you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:6)


וְאַפֵּישׁ יָתָךְ לַחֲדָא לַחֲדָא וְאֶתְּנִנָּךְ **לְכִנְשִׁין** וּמַלְכִין **דְּשַׁלִּיטִין בְּעַמְמַיָּא** מִנָּךְ יִפְקוּן:


I will make you exceedingly fruitful and I will make you into nations [*assemblies*]; and kings [*who rule over nations*] will descend from you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:7)


וַאֲקֵים יָת קְיָמִי בֵּין **מֵימְרִי** וּבֵינָךְ וּבֵין בְּנָךְ בַּתְרָךְ לְדָרֵיהוֹן לִקְיָם עָלָם לְמֵהֲוֵי לָךְ לֵאלָהָא וְלִבְנָךְ בַּתְרָךְ:


I will sustain My covenant between Me [*My Word*] and you, and between your descendants after you throughout their generations as an eternal covenant, to be a God to you, and to your descendants after you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:8)


וְאֶתֵּן לָךְ וְלִבְנָךְ בַּתְרָךְ יָת אַרְעָא תּוֹתָבוּתָךְ יָת כָּל אַרְעָא דִכְנַעַן לְאַחֲסָנַת עָלָם וְאֶהֱוֵי לְהוֹן לֵאלָהָא:


I will give to you, and to your descendants after you, the land of your temporary residence, all the land of Canaan as an eternal possession, and I will be a God to them.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:9)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְאַבְרָהָם וְאַתְּ יָת קְיָמִי תִטָּר אַתְּ וּבְנָךְ בַּתְרָךְ לְדָרֵיהוֹן:


Elohim said to Avraham: And as for you, you must preserve My covenant, you and your descendants after you throughout their generations.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:10)


דָּא קְיָמִי דִּי תִטְּרוּן בֵּין **מֵימְרִי** וּבֵינֵיכוֹן וּבֵין בְּנָךְ בַּתְרָךְ מִגְזַר לְכוֹן כָּל דְּכוּרָא:


This is My covenant which you must preserve between Me [*My Word*] and you, and your descendants after you: every male among you shall be circumcised.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:11)


וְתִגְזְרוּן יָת בִּשְׂרָא דְעָרְלָתְכוֹן וּתְהֵי (נ"י וּתְהֵא) לְאָת קְיָם בֵּין **מֵימְרִי** וּבֵינֵיכוֹן:


You shall circumcise the flesh of your foreskin. This shall be the sign of the covenant between Me [*My Word*] and you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:12)


וּבַר תְּמַנְיָא יוֹמִין יִתְגְּזַר (נ"י יִגְזַר) לְכוֹן כָּל דְּכוּרָא לְדָרָתֵיכוֹן יְלִידֵי בֵיתָא וּזְבִינֵי כַסְפָּא מִכֹּל בַּר עַמְמִין דְּלָא מִבְּנָךְ הוּא:


At the age of eight days every male among you must be circumcised, throughout your generations; he that is a house-born [slave] or one that was bought with money from any stranger who is not your descendant.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:13)


אִתְגַּזָּרָא יִתְגַּזְּרוּן (נ"י מִגְזַר יִגְזַר) יְלִיד בֵּיתָךְ וּזְבִין כַּסְפָּךְ וּתְהֵי קְיָמִי בְּבִשְׂרֵיכוֹן לִקְיָם עָלָם:


Your house-born slaves must be circumcised, and also those bought with your money. This shall be My covenant in your flesh as an eternal covenant.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:14)


וְעָרֵל דְּכוּרָא דִּי לָא יִגְזַר יָת בְּשַׂר עָרְלָתֵיהּ וְיִשְׁתֵּיצֵי **אֱנָשָׁא** הַהִיא מְעַמֵּיהּ יָת קְיָמִי **אַשְׁנִי**:


An uncircumcised male, who will not circumcise his foreskin, that soul [*man*] shall be cut off from its people; he has broken [*changed*] My covenant.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:15)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְאַבְרָהָם שָׂרַי אִתְּתָךְ לָא תִקְרֵי יָת שְׁמַהּ שָׂרָי אֲרֵי שָׂרָה שְׁמַהּ:


And Elohim said to Avraham [As for] Sarai your wife, do not call her by the name Sarai, for Sarah is her name.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:16)


וֶאֱבָרֵךְ יָתַהּ וְאַף אֶתֵּן מִנַּהּ לָךְ בָּר וֶאֱבָרֲכִנַּהּ וּתְהֵי **לְכִנְשִׁין** מַלְכִין **דְּשַׁלִּיטִין בְּעַמְמַיָּא** מִנַּהּ יְהוֹן:
