I will bless her, and I will also give you a son through her. I will bless her, and she will be [a mother] of nations [*assemblies*], kings of peoples [*who rule over nations*] will descend from her.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:17)


וּנְפַל אַבְרָהָם עַל אַפּוֹהִי **וַחֲדִי** וַאֲמַר בְּלִבֵּיהּ הַלְבַר מְאָה שְׁנִין יְהֵי וְלַד וְאִם שָׂרָה הֲבַת תִּשְׁעִין שְׁנִין תְּלִיד:


Avraham fell on his face and laughed [*rejoiced*]. He said in his heart: Can a hundred year old man have children? Shall Sarah, who is ninety years old give birth?


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:18)


וַאֲמַר אַבְרָהָם **קֳדָם** יְיָ לְוַי יִשְׁמָעֵאל יִתְקַיָּם קֳדָמָךְ:


And Avraham said to [*before*] Elohim: May it be granted that Yishmael live before You.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:19)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ בְּקוּשְׁטָא שָׂרָה אִתְּתָךְ תְּלִיד לָךְ בָּר וְתִקְרֵי יָת שְׁמֵיהּ יִצְחָק וַאֲקֵים יָת קְיָמִי עִמֵּיהּ לִקְיָם עָלָם לִבְנוֹהִי בַתְרוֹהִי:


Elohim said: Indeed, your wife Sarah will bear you a son, and you will name him Yitzchok. I will establish My covenant with him as an eternal covenant to his descendants after him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:20)


וְעַל יִשְׁמָעֵאל **קַבֵּלִית צְלוֹתָךְ** הָא בָּרֵכִית יָתֵיהּ וְאַפֵּשׁ יָתֵיהּ וְאַסְגֵּי יָתֵיהּ לַחֲדָא לַחֲדָא תְּרֵין עֲשַׂר רַבְרְבַיָּא יְלִיד וְאֶתְּנִנֵּיהּ לְעַם סַגִּי:


And as for Yishmael, I have heard you [*I have accepted your prayer*]. I have blessed him, and I will make him fruitful, and will increase him exceedingly. He will become the father of twelve princes, and I will make him into a great nation.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:21)


וְיָת קְיָמִי אָקִים עִם יִצְחָק דִּי תְלִיד לָךְ שָׂרָה לְזִמְנָא הָדֵין בְּשַׁתָּא אָחֳרַנְתָּא:


But I will establish My covenant with Yitzchok, who Sarah will bear to you at this time next year.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:22)


וְשֵׁיצֵי לְמַלָּלָא עִמֵּיהּ וְאִסְתַּלַּק **יְקָרָא** דַיְיָ מֵעִלָּווֹהִי דְּאַבְרָהָם:


And when He finished speaking with him, [*the Glory of*] Elohim ascended from Avraham.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:23)


וּדְבַר אַבְרָהָם יָת יִשְׁמָעֵאל בְּרֵיהּ וְיָת כָּל יְלִידֵי בֵיתֵיהּ וְיָת כָּל זְבִינֵי כַסְפֵּיהּ כָּל דְּכוּרָא בַּאֲנָשֵׁי בֵּית אַבְרָהָם וּגְזַר יָת בִּשְׂרָא דְעָרְלָתְהוֹן בִּכְרַן יוֹמָא הָדֵין כְּמָא דִּי מַלִּיל עִמֵּיהּ יְיָ:


Avraham took his son, Yishmael, and all those that were born in his household, and all that he had bought with his money, every male member of Avraham’s household, and he circumcised the flesh of their foreskin on that very day as Elohim had said to him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:24)


וְאַבְרָהָם בַּר תִּשְׁעִין וּתְשַׁע שְׁנִין כַּד גְּזַר בִּשְׂרָא דְעָרְלְתֵיהּ:


Avraham was ninety-nine years old when he was circumcised in the flesh of his foreskin.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:25)


וְיִשְׁמָעֵאל בְּרֵיהּ בַּר תְּלָת עַשְׂרֵי שְׁנִין כַּד גְּזַר יָת בִּשְׂרָא דְעָרְלְתֵיהּ:


And his son Yishmael was thirteen years old when he circumcised the flesh of his foreskin.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:26)


בִּכְרַן יוֹמָא הָדֵין אִתְגְּזַר (נ"י גְּזַר) אַבְרָהָם וְיִשְׁמָעֵאל בְּרֵיהּ:


On that very day Avraham and his son Yishmael were circumcised.


###### Onkelos Genesis 17:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/17:27)


וְכָל אֱנָשֵׁי בֵיתֵיהּ יְלִידֵי בֵיתָא וּזְבִינֵי כַסְפָּא מִן בַּר עַמְמִין אִתְגְּזָרוּ (נ"י גְּזָרוּ) עִמֵּיהּ:


All the men of his household, those born in his household, and bought with money from a stranger, were circumcised with him.



## Chapter 18

###### Onkelos Genesis 18:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:1)


**וְאִתְגְּלִי** לֵיהּ יְיָ **בְּמֵשְׁרֵי** מַמְרֵא וְהוּא יָתֵב בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא כְּמֵיחַם יוֹמָא:


Adonoy appeared [**became revealed**] to him in the groves [**plains**] of Mamrei and he was sitting at the door of the tent in the heat of the day.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:2)


וּזְקַף עֵינוֹהִי וַחֲזָא וְהָא תְּלָתָא גֻבְרִין (נ"י גַבְרִין) קָיְמִין עִלָּווֹהִי וַחֲזָא וּרְהַט לְקַדָּמוּתְהוֹן מִתְּרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא וּסְגִיד עַל אַרְעָא:


He lifted his eyes and saw, and behold three men were standing over him. He saw [them], and ran from the door of the tent to greet them, and he bowed down to the earth.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:3)


וַאֲמָר יְיָ אִם **כְּעַן** אַשְׁכָּחִית רַחֲמִין **קֳדָמָךְ** (נ"י בְּעֵינָיךְ) לָא כְעַן תְּעִבַּר מֵעַל עַבְדָּךְ:


He said, My Master, if I have found favor in Your eyes [**before you**], please [**now**] do not bypass your servant.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:4)


**יִסְּבוּן כְּעַן זְעֵיר מַיָּא** וְאַסְחוּ רַגְלֵיכוֹן וְאִסְתְּמִיכוּ תְּחוֹת אִילָנָא:


Let a bit of water be brought [**They will take a bit of water**] and wash your feet. Rest yourselves under the tree.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:5)


וְאֶסַּב פִּתָּא דְלַחְמָא וּסְעִידוּ לִבְּכוֹן בָּתַר כֵּן תַּעֲבָרוּן אֲרֵי עַל כֵּן עֲבַרְתּוּן עַל עַבְדְּכוֹן וַאֲמָרוּ כֵּן תַּעְבֵּיד כְּמָא דִּי מַלֵּלְתָּא:


I will get bread and you will sustain your heart. Afterwards you will continue on your way, since you have passed by your servant. They said, Fine, do as you have said.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:6)


וְאוֹחִי אַבְרָהָם לְמַשְׁכְּנָא לְוַת שָׂרָה וַאֲמַר אוֹחָא תְּלָת סְאִין קִמְחָא דְסָלְתָּא לוּשִׁי וְעִבִידִי גְרִיצָן:


Avraham hurried to Sarah’s tent and said, Hurry! [take] three measures of the finest flour; knead it and make cake-rolls.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:7)


וּלְוַת תּוֹרֵי רְהַט אַבְרָהָם וּדְבַר בַּר תּוֹרֵי רַכִּיךְ וְטַב וִיהַב לְעוּלֵמָא וְאוֹחִי לְמֶעְבַּד יָתֵיהּ:


Avraham ran to the cattle, and took a tender, choice calf. He gave it to the lad. and hurried to prepare it.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:8)


וּנְסִיב שְׁמַן וַחֲלָב וּבַר תּוֹרֵי דִּי עֲבַד וִיהַב קֳדָמֵיהוֹן וְהוּא **מְשַׁמֵּשׁ** עִלָּוֵיהוֹן תְּחוֹת אִילָנָא וַאֲכָלוּ:


He took butter, milk, and the calf he had prepared, and set it before them. He stood over them [**served them**] under the tree, and they ate.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:9)


וַאֲמָרוּ לֵיהּ אָן שָׂרָה אִתְּתָךְ וַאֲמַר הָא בְמַשְׁכְּנָא:


They said to him, Where is Sarah, your wife? He said Here, in the tent.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:10)


וַאֲמַר מִתַּב אִתּוּב לְוָתָךְ **כְּעִדָּן דְּאַתּוּן קַיָּמִין** וְהָא בָר לְשָׂרָה אִתְּתָךְ וְשָׂרָה שְׁמַעַת בִּתְרַע מַשְׁכְּנָא וְהוּא אֲחוֹרוֹהִי:


He said I will return to you next year [**at this time when you will be alive**], and Sarah, your wife will have a son. Sarah was listening at the door of the tent, that was behind him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:11)


וְאַבְרָהָם וְשָׂרָה סִיבוּ עַלּוּ בְּיוֹמִין פְּסַק מִלְּמֶהֱוֵי לְשָׂרָה אוֹרַח כִּנְשַׁיָּא:


Avraham and Sarah were old, well on in years. Sarah no longer had the way of women.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:12)


וְחַיְכַת שָׂרָה בִּמְעָהַהּ לְמֵימָר בָּתַר **דְּסֵיבִית** הֲוַת לִי **עוּלֵימוּ** וְרִבּוֹנִי סִיב:


Sarah laughed to herself saying, Now that I am worn out [**old**], shall I have the pleasure [of a son] [**youthfulness**], my master being [also] an old man.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:13)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְאַבְרָהָם לְמָא דְנָן חַיְכַת שָׂרָה לְמֵימַר הַבְרַם בְּקוּשְׁטָא אוֹלִיד וַאֲנָא סֵיבִית:


Adonoy said to Avraham, Why did Sarah laugh saying, Can I really give birth when I am old?


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:14)


**הֲיִתְכַּסֵּא** מִן **קֳדָם** יְיָ פִּתְגָּמָא לִזְמַן אִתּוּב לְוָתָךְ **כְּעִדָּן דְּאַתּוּן קַיָּמִין** וּלְשָׂרָה בָר:


Is anything too far removed [**hidden**] from [**before**] Adonoy? At the appointed time I will return to you, at this time of life [**when you will be alive**], and Sarah will have a son.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:15)


וְכַדִּיבַת שָׂרָה לְמֵימָר לָא חַיֵּכִית אֲרֵי דְחֵילָת וַאֲמַר לָא **בְּרָם** חַיָּכְתְּ:


Sarah denied it saying, I did not laugh, for she was afraid. He said, Not so, for [**but**] you did laugh.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:16)


וְקָמוּ מִתַּמָּן גֻּבְרַיָּא וְאִסְתְּכִיאוּ עַל אַפֵּי סְדוֹם וְאַבְרָהָם אָזֵל עִמְּהוֹן **לְאַלְווֹאֵיהוֹן**:


The men stood up from where they were, and they gazed upon Sedom. Avraham went with them to send them [**escort them**] [on their way].


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:17)


וַיְיָ אֲמָר הַמְכַסֵּי אֲנָא מֵאַבְרָהָם דִּי אֲנָא עָבֵד:


Adonoy said, Shall I conceal from Avraham what I am about to do?


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:18)


וְאַבְרָהָם מֵהֲוָה יֶהֱוֵי לְעַם סַגִּי וְתַקִּיף וְיִתְבָּרְכוּן **בְּדִילֵיהּ** כֹּל עַמְמֵי אַרְעָא:


Avraham is indeed to become a great and mighty nation, and through him [**and because of him**] shall be blessed all the nations of the world.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:19)


**אֲרֵי גְלִי קֳדָמַי** (גי' רמב"ן יְדַעְתִּנֵיהּ) **בְּדִיל דִּי יְפַקֵּד יָת בְּנוֹהִי** וְיָת אֱנַשׁ בֵּיתֵיהּ בַּתְרוֹהִי וְיִטְּרוּן **אָרְחָן דְּתַקְּנָן קֳדָם יְיָ** לְמֶעְבַּד צְדַקְתָּא וְדִינָא בְּדִיל אַיְתִי יְיָ עַל אַבְרָהָם יָת דִּמַלֵּל עֲלוֹהִי:


For I have given him special attention because he commands his children [**For it is known to Me that he will command his children**], and his household after him, and they will preserve the way of Adonoy [**the ways that are good in the eyes of Adonoy**], doing charity and justice, so that Adonoy will bring upon Avraham all that which He has spoken of him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:20)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ קְבֵלַת דִּסְדוֹם וַעֲמוֹרָה אֲרֵי סְגִיאַת וְחוֹבָתְהוֹן אֲרֵי תְקִיפַת לַחֲדָא:


[Thus] Adonoy said, The wailing concerning Sedom and Amorah is so great, and their sin is so very grave.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:21)


**אִתְגְּלִי כְעַן וְאֶדּוּן** הֲכִי קְבִלְתְּהוֹן דְּעַלַּת **לִקֳדָמַי** עֲבָדוּ **אֶעְבֵּד עִמְּהוֹן גְּמֵירָא** (אִם לָא תָיְבִין) **וְאִם תָּיְבִין לָא אֶתְפְּרָע**:


I will descend now and see [**I will become revealed now and judge**], if their wailing which has come to [**before**] Me is indicative of their conduct; destruction [shall come upon them] [**I will destroy them**]. If not I will know. [**If they will repent, I will not punish them.**]


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:22)


וְאִתְפְּנִיאוּ מִתַּמָּן גֻּבְרַיָּא וַאֲזָלוּ לִסְדוֹם וְאַבְרָהָם עַד כְּעַן **מְשַׁמֵּשׁ בִּצְלוֹ** קֳדָם יְיָ:


The men turned from where they were, and went toward Sedom. Avraham was still standing [**serving with prayer**] before Adonoy.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:23)


וּקְרֵב אַבְרָהָם וַאֲמָר **הֲבִרְגַז** תְּשֵׁיצֵי זַכָּאָה עִם חַיָּבָא:


Avraham came forward and said, Will You [actually] destroy the righteous with the wicked [**in anger**]?


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:24)


**מָאִים** אִית חַמְשִׁין זַכָּאִין בְּגוֹ קַרְתָּא **הֲבִרְגַז** תְּשֵׁיצֵי וְלָא תִשְׁבּוֹק לְאַתְרָא בְּדִיל חַמְשִׁין זַכָּאִין דִּי בְגַוַּהּ:


Suppose [**Perhaps**] there are fifty righteous people in the midst of the city, will You still destroy it [**in anger**], and not bear with the place for the sake of the fifty righteous people inside it?


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:25)


**קוּשְׁטָא אִנּוּן דִּינָךְ מִלְּמֶעְבַּד** כְּפִתְגָּמָא הָדֵין לְקַטָּלָא זַכָּאָה עִם חַיָּבָא וִיהֵי זַכָּאָה כְּחַיָּבָא **קוּשְׁטָא אִנּוּן דִּינָךְ** דְּדָיִן (נ"י הֲדַיַן) כָּל אַרְעָא לָא (נ"י בְּרַם) יַעְבֵּד דִּינָא:


It would be sacrilege [**Your judgments are true therefore it is impossible**] [to attribute] to You such an act, to kill the righteous with the wicked, treating the righteous and the wicked alike. It would be sacrilege to attribute this to You [**Your judgments are true**]: Shall the Judge of all the earth not do justice?


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:26)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ אִם אַשְׁכַּח בִּסְדוֹם חַמְשִׁין זַכָּאִין בְּגוֹ קַרְתָּא וְאֶשְׁבּוֹק לְכָל אַתְרָא בְּדִילְהוֹן:


Adonoy said; If, in Sedom, I find fifty righteous within the city, I will bear with the entire place for their sake.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:27)


וַאֲתֵב אַבְרָהָם וַאֲמָר הָא כְעַן שָׁרֵיתִי לְמַלָּלָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ וַאֲנָא עָפָר וּקְטָם:


Avraham responded and said, Here I have begun to speak to [**before**] my Master, and I am but dust and ashes.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:28)


**מָאִים** יַחְסְרוּן חַמְשִׁין זַכָּאִין חַמְשָׁא הַתְחַבֵּל בְּחַמְשָׁא יָת כָּל קַרְתָּא וַאֲמַר לָא אֲחַבֵּל אִם אַשְׁכַּח תַּמָּן אַרְבְּעִין וְחַמְשָׁא:


But suppose [**perhaps**] they lack five of the fifty righteous? Will You destroy all the city because of five? He said, I will not destroy if I find forty-five there.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:29)


וְאוֹסֵיף עוֹד לְמַלָּלָא **קֳדָמוֹהִי** וַאֲמַר **מָאִים** יִשְׁתַּכְּחוּן תַּמָּן אַרְבְּעִין וַאֲמַר לָא **אֶעְבֵּד גְּמֵרָא** בְּדִיל אַרְבְּעִין:


He [Avraham] continued to speak to [**before**] Him and said, Suppose [**Perhaps**] there are forty found there? He said, I will not do it [**inflict destruction**] for the sake of the forty.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:30)


וַאֲמַר לָא כְעַן יִתְקֹף קֳדָם (נ"י רוּגְזָא) דַיְיָ וַאֲמַלֵּל **מָאִים** יִשְׁתַּכְּחוּן תַּמָּן תְּלָתִין וַאֲמַר לָא **אֶעְבֵּד גְּמֵרָא** אִם אַשְׁכַּח תַּמָּן תְּלָתִין:


He said, Let not my Master show anger and I will [continue] to speak. Suppose [**Perhaps**] thirty are found there? He said, I will not do it [**inflict destruction**] if I find thirty there.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:31)


וַאֲמַר הָא כְעַן שָׁרֵיתִי לְמַלָּלָא **קֳדָם** יְיָ **מָאִים** יִשְׁתַּכְּחוּן תַּמָּן עֶשְׂרִין וַאֲמַר לָא אֲחַבֵּל בְּדִיל עֶשְׁרִין:


He said, Here I wished to speak to [**before**] my Master. Suppose [**Perhaps**] twenty are found there? He said, I will not destroy for the sake of the twenty.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:32)


וַאֲמַר **לָא כְעַן יִתְקֹף קֳדָם יְיָ** וֶאֱמַלֵּל בְּרַם זִמְנָא הָדָא מָאִים יִשְׁתַּכְּחוּן תַּמָּן עַשְׂרָא וַאֲמַר לָא אֲחַבֵּל בְּדִיל עַשְׂרָא:


He said, Let not my Master show anger [**Let there not be anger before my Master**], and I will speak just once more. Suppose [**Perhaps**] ten are found there? He said, I will not destroy for the sake of the ten.


###### Onkelos Genesis 18:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/18:33)


**וְאִסְתַּלַּק יְקָרָא דַיְיָ** כַּד שֵׁצִי לְמַלָּלָא עִם אַבְרָהָם וְאַבְרָהָם תָּב לְאַתְרֵיהּ:


Adonoy departed [**The glory of Adonoy ascended**] when He finished speaking to Avraham, and Avraham returned to his place.



## Chapter 19

###### Onkelos Genesis 19:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:1)


וְעַלּוּ תְּרֵין מַלְאָכַיָּא לִסְדוֹם בְּרַמְשָׁא וְלוֹט יָתֵב בְּתַרְעָא (נ"י בִּתְרַע) דִסְדוֹם וַחֲזָא לוֹט וְקָם לְקַדָּמוּתְהוֹן וּסְגִיד עַל אַפּוֹהִי עַל אַרְעָא:


The two angels came to Sedom in the evening, while Lot was sitting at the gate of Sedom. Lot saw them, and he got up to greet them, and he bowed with his face to the ground.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:2)


וַאֲמַר **בְּבָעוּ כְעַן** רִבּוֹנַי זוּרוּ כְעַן לְבֵית עַבְדְּכוֹן וּבִיתוּ וְאַסְחוֹ רַגְלֵיכוֹן וּתְקַדְּמוּן וּתְהָכוּן לְאָרְחֲכוֹן וַאֲמָרוּ לָא אֶלָּהֵן בִּרְחוֹבָא נְבִית:


He said, Behold [**Please**] now my lords, please [**now**] turn in to your servant’s house. Stay over night, bathe your feet, and get up early and continue on your way. They said, No, we will spend the night in the street.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:3)


וְאַתְקֵף בְּהוֹן לַחֲדָא וְזָרוּ לְוָתֵיהּ וְעַלּוּ לְבֵיתֵיהּ וַעֲבַד לְהוֹן מִשְׁתְּיָא וּפַטִּיר אֲפָא לְהוֹן וַאֲכָלוּ:


He urged them greatly, and they turned in to him and came to his house. He made a feast for them, and baked matzos [**for them**], and they ate.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:4)


עַד לָא שְׁכִיבוּ וַאֲנָשֵׁי קַרְתָּא אֲנָשֵׁי סְדוֹם אַקִּיפוּ עַל בֵּיתָא מֵעוּלֵימָא וְעַד סָבָא כָּל עַמָּא מִסּוֹפֵיהּ:


They had not yet lain down when the men of the city, the men of Sedom, surrounded the house—young and old alike—all the people from one end [of the city] to the other end.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:5)


וּקְרוֹ לְלוֹט וַאֲמָרוּ לֵיהּ אָן גֻּבְרַיָּא דִּי אֲתוֹ לְוָתָךְ לֵילְיָא אַפֵּקִנּוּן לְוָתָנָא וְנִדַּע יָתְהוֹן:


They called to Lot and said to him, Where are the men who came to you tonight? Bring them out to us that we may know them.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:6)


וּנְפַק לְוָתְהוֹן לוֹט לְתַרְעָא וְדָשָׁא אֲחַד בַּתְרוֹהִי:


Lot went out to them in front of the entrance, shutting the door behind him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:7)


וַאֲמַר בְּבָעוּ **כְעַן** אַחַי לָא תַבְאִישׁוּן:


He said, My brothers, please [**now**] do not act wickedly.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:8)


הָא כְעַן לִי תַּרְתֵּין בְּנָן דִּי לָא יְדָעִנּוּן גְּבַר אַפֵּק כְּעַן יָתְהָן לְוָתְכוֹן וְעִיבִידוּ לְהֵן כִּדְתַקֵּן בְּעֵינֵיכוֹן לְחוֹד לְגֻבְרַיָּא הָאִלֵּין לָא תַעְבְּדוּן מִדַּעַם אֲרֵי עַל כֵּן עַלּוּ בִּטְלַל שֵׁרוּתִי:


Behold! I have two daughters who have never known a man, I will bring them out to you, and do with them as you please; only do nothing to these men, since after all, they came under the shelter of my roof.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:9)


וַאֲמָרוּ קְרֵב לְהַלָּא וַאֲמָרוּ חַד אֲתָא לְאִתּוֹתָבָא וְהָא דָיִין דִּינָא כְּעַן נַבְאֵשׁ לָךְ מִדִּילְהוֹן וּתְקִיפוּ בְגַבְרָא בְלוֹט לַחֲדָא וּקְרִיבוּ לְמִתְּבַר דָּשָׁא:


They said, Step back out of the way! They said, This one came as an immigrant, and now he wants to be a judge. We will now deal worse with you than with them. They pushed hard against Lot, and came near to break the door.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:10)


וְאוֹשִׁיטוּ גֻבְרַיָּא יָת יְדֵיהוֹן וְאַיְתִיאוּ יָת לוֹט לְוָתְהוֹן לְבֵיתָא וְיָת דָּשָׁא אֲחָדוּ:


The men put out their hands, and pulled Lot to them, into the house, and closed the door.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:11)


וְיָת גֻּבְרַיָּא דִּי בִתְרַע בֵּיתָא מְחוֹ בְּשַׁבְרִירַיָּא מִזְּעֵירָא וְעַד רַבָּא וּלְאִיוּ לְאַשְׁכָּחָא תַרְעָא:


The men who were at the entrance of the house, they struck with blindness—young and old alike—so that they wearied themselves trying to find the entrance.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:12)


וַאֲמָרוּ גֻבְרַיָּא לְלוֹט עוֹד מָן לָךְ הָכָא חַתְנָא וּבְנָךְ וּבְנָתָךְ וְכֹל דִּי לָךְ בְּקַרְתָּא אַפֵּיק מִן אַתְרָא:


The men said to Lot, Who else do you have here—a son-in-law, your sons, your daughters? Whoever you have in the city, bring them out of his place.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:13)


אֲרֵי מְחַבְּלִין אֲנַחְנָא יָת אַתְרָא הָדֵין אֲרֵי סְגִיאַת קְבִלְתְּהוֹן קֳדָם יְיָ וְשַׁלְחָנָא יְיָ לְחַבָּלוּתַהּ:


We are going to destroy this place, for the wailing concerning them has become great in the presence of Adonoy; and Adonoy has sent us to destroy it.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:14)


וּנְפַק לוֹט וּמַלִּיל עִם חַתְנוֹהִי נָסְבֵי בְנָתֵיהּ וַאֲמַר קוּמוּ פּוּקוּ מִן אַתְרָא הָדֵין אֲרֵי מְחַבֵּל יְיָ יָת קַרְתָּא וַהֲוָה כִּמְחָיִךְ בְּעֵינֵי חַתְנוֹהִי:


Lot went out and spoke to his sons-in-law who had married his daughters. He said [to them], Get up! Get out of this place, for Adonoy is going to destroy the city! He appeared as a comedian in the eyes of his sons-in-law.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:15)


וּכְמִסַּק צַפְרָא הֲוָה וּדְחִיקוּ מַלְאָכַיָּא בְּלוֹט לְמֵימָר קוּם דְּבַר יָת אִתְּתָךְ וְיָת תַּרְתֵּין בְּנָתָךְ **דְּאִשְׁתְּכַחַן מְהֵימְנָן עִמָּךְ** דִּילְמָא תִלְקֵי בְּחוֹבֵי קַרְתָּא:


At the break of dawn, the angels urged Lot on, saying, Get up! Take your wife and your two daughters who are here [**who are found to be faithful to you**], lest you be swept away in the iniquity of the city.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:16)


וְאִתְעַכַּב וְאַתְקִיפוּ גֻבְרַיָּא בִּידֵיהּ וּבִידָא דְאִתְּתֵיהּ וּבְיַד תַּרְתֵּין בְּנָתֵיהּ כִּרְחַם (נ"י כַּד חָס) יְיָ עֲלוֹהִי וְאַפְּקוּהִי וְאַשְׁרוּהִי מִבָּרָא לְקַרְתָּא:


He hesitated, and the men grabbed his hand and the hand of his wife and two daughters, for Adonoy had pity on him. They brought him out and placed him outside the city.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:17)


וַהֲוָה כַּד אַפִּיקוּ יָתְהוֹן לְבָרָא וַאֲמַר **חוּס** עַל נַפְשָׁךְ לָא תִסְתְּכִי לַאֲחוֹרָךְ וְלָא תְקוּם בְּכָל **מֵישְׁרָא** לְטוּרָא אִשְׁתֵּזֵב דִּלְמָא תִלְקֵי:


When they were brought out [of the city], he [the angel] said, Escape [**Have pity**] for your life! Do not look back! Do not remain anywhere in the valley [**plain**]. Escape to the mountain, lest you be swept away.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:18)


וַאֲמַר לוֹט לְהוֹן **בְּבָעוּ כְעַן רִבּוֹנָי** (נ"י יְיָ):


Lot said to them, Please, not so my Master. [**Please, now, my Master.**]


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:19)


הָא כְעַן אַשְׁכַּח עַבְדָךְ רַחֲמִין **קֳדָמָךְ** וְאַסְגֵּיתָא **טֵיבוּתָךְ** דִּי עֲבַדְתָּ עִמִּי לְקַיָּמָא יָת נַפְשִׁי וַאֲנָא לֵית אֲנָא יָכִיל לְאִשְׁתֵּזָבָא לְטוּרָא דִּילְמָא תְעַרְעִנַּנִי בִשְׁתָּא וְאֵימוּת:


Behold your servant has found favor in Your eyes [**before You**]. Great is your kindness [**goodness**] that you have done with me, to keep me alive. I cannot escape to the mountain, lest the evil attach itself to me and I die.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:20)


הָא כְעַן קַרְתָּא הָדָא קְרִיבָא לְמֵעִירוֹק לְתַמָּן וְהִיא זְעֵירָא אִשְׁתֵּזַב כְּעַן תַּמָּן הֲלָא זְעֵירָא הִיא וְתִתְקַיַּם נַפְשִׁי:


Behold, please, this city is near [enough] to flee there. It is insignificant. Let me escape there! It is insignificant, and my life will be saved.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:21)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ הָא נְסֵיבִית אַפָּךְ אַף לְפִתְגָּמָא הָדֵין בְּדִיל דְּלָא לְמֵהְפַּךְ יָת קַרְתָּא **דִּבְעֵיתָא עֲלַהּ**:


He said to him, See I have also given you consideration regarding this. I will not overturn the city that you mentioned [**for which you have beseeched**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:22)


אוֹחִי לְאִשְׁתֵּזֵב תַּמָּן אֲרֵי לָא אִכּוּל לְמֶעְבַּד פִּתְגָּמָא עַד מֵיתָךְ לְתַמָּן עַל כֵּן קְרָא שְׁמָא דְקַרְתָּא צוֹעַר:


Hurry, escape there, for I can do nothing until you get there. The city was therefore called Zoar.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:23)

The sun had risen upon the earth, when Lot came to Zoar.


###### Onkelos Genesis 19:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/19:24)

Adonoy caused to rain upon Sedom and Amorah—sulfur and fire—from [**before**] Adonoy, from heaven.

He overturned these cities, and the entire plain, and all those who lived in the cities and all that grew upon the ground.

His [Lot’s] wife looked behind him; and she became a pillar of salt.

Lot went up from Zoar and lived in the mountain; and his two daughters went with him, for he was afraid to live in Zoar. He lived in a cave, he and his two daughters.

The older girl said to the younger, Our father is old, and there is no man [left] on earth who will come into us in the normal manner.

Lot’s two daughters became pregnant from their father.

## Chapter 20

###### Onkelos Genesis 20:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:1)


וּנְטַל מִתַּמָּן אַבְרָהָם לְאַרְעָא דָרוֹמָא וִיתֵב בֵּין **רְקָם** וּבֵין **חַגְרָא** וְאִתּוֹתַב בִּגְרָר:


Avraham journeyed from there to the land of the Negev [**south**], and he lived between Kadeish [**Rekem**] and Shur [**Chagar**]. He lived for a while in Gerar.


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:2)


וַאֲמַר אַבְרָהָם עַל שָׂרָה אִתְּתֵיהּ אֲחָתִי הִיא וּשְׁלַח אֲבִימֶלֶךְ מַלְכָּא דִגְרָר וּדְבַר יָת שָׂרָה:


Avraham said regarding Sarah, his wife, She is my sister. Avimelech, king of Gerar sent [messengers] and took Sarah.


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:3)


**וַאֲתָא מֵימַר מִן קֳדָם יְיָ לְוַת אֲבִימֶלֶךְ** בְּחֶלְמָא דְלֵילְיָא וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ הָא אַתְּ מִית עַל עֵיסַק אִתְּתָא דִדְבַרְתָּא וְהִיא **אִתַּת גְּבָר**:


Elohim came to Avimelech [**Word came from before Elohim to Avimelech**] in a dream at night, and said to him, Behold, you shall die because of the woman you took, for she is a married woman [**a man’s wife**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:4)


וַאֲבִימֶלֶךְ לָא קְרֵב לְוָתַהּ וַאֲמַר יְיָ הֲעָם אַף זַכַּאי תִּקְטוֹל:


Avimelech had not come near to her. He said, My Master, will You also kill an innocent nation?


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:5)


הֲלָא הוּא אֲמַר לִי אֲחָתִי הִיא וְהִיא אַף הִיא אֲמָרַת אָחִי הוּא **בְּקַשִּׁיטוּת** לִבִּי **וּבְזַכָּאוּת** יְדַי עֲבָדִית דָּא:


Did he not say to me, She is my sister? And she also said, He is my brother. With an innocent [**upright**] heart and clean [**righteous**] hands I did this.


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:6)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ **מֵימַר מִן קֳדָם יְיָ** בְּחֶלְמָא **אַף קֳדָמַי גְּלֵי** אֲרֵי **בְּקַשִּׁיטוּת** לִבָּךְ עָבָדְתָּ דָּא וּמְנָעִית אַף אֲנָא יָתָךְ מִלְּמֶחְטֵי **קֳדָמָי** עַל כֵּן לָא שְׁבַקְתָּךְ לְמִקְרַב לְוָתַהּ:


Elohim said to him [**Word from Elohim was said to him**] in a dream, I also know [**it is also revealed before me**] that you did this with an innocent [**upright**] heart. I also prevented you from sinning against [**before**] Me. That is why I did not give you the chance to touch her.


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:7)


וּכְעַן אֲתֵיב אִתַּת גַּבְרָא אֲרֵי נְבִיָּא הוּא וִיצַלֵּי עֲלָךְ וּתְחֵי וְאִם לֵיתָךְ מְתִיב דַּע אֲרֵי מֵימַת תְּמוּת אַתְּ וְכָל דִּי לָךְ:


Now return the man’s wife, for he is a prophet. He will pray for you and you will live. If you do not return her, you should know that you will surely die—you and all that is yours.


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:8)


וְאַקְדֵּים אֲבִימֶלֶךְ בְּצַפְרָא וּקְרָא לְכָל עַבְדּוֹהִי וּמַלִּיל יָת כָּל פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין **קֳדָמֵיהוֹן** וּדְחִילוּ גוּבְרַיָּא לַחֲדָא:


Avimelech got up early in the morning, and called all his servants. He spoke all these words in their ears [**before them**], and the men were very frightened.


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:9)


וּקְרָא אֲבִימֶלֶךְ לְאַבְרָהָם וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ מַה עָבַדְתָּ לָנָא וּמַה חָבִית (נ"י חָטֵית) לָךְ אֲרֵי אַיְתֵיתָא עֲלַי וְעַל מַלְכוּתִי חוֹבָא רַבָּא עוֹבָדִין דִּי לָא כָשְׁרִין לְאִתְעֲבָדָא עֲבַדְתָּ עִמִּי:


Avimelech called Avraham and said to him, What have you done to us? What wrong did I do to you that you brought on me and upon my kingdom such a great sin? Deeds that ought not to be done, you have done to me.


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:10)


וַאֲמַר אֲבִימֶלֶךְ לְאַבְרָהָם מָה חָזֵיתָ אֲרֵי עֲבַדְתָּ יָת פִּתְגָּמָא הָדֵין:


Avimelech [then] said to Avraham, What did you see that you did such a thing?


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:11)


וַאֲמַר אַבְרָהָם אֲרֵי אֲמָרִית לְחוֹד לֵית דַּחַלְתָּא דַיְיָ בְּאַתְרָא הָדֵין וְיִקְטְלֻנַּנִי עַל עֵיסַק אִתְּתִי:


Avraham said, Because I said [thought] there is no fear of Elohim in this place, and they will kill me [in order] to take my wife.


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:12)


וּבְרַם בְּקוּשְׁטָא אֲחָתִי בַּת אַבָּא הִיא בְּרַם לָא בַת אִמִּי וַהֲוַת לִי לְאִנְתּוּ:


In any case, she is my sister, the daughter of my father, but not the daughter of my mother, and she became my wife.


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:13)


**וַהֲוָה כַּד טָעוּ עַמְמַיָּא בָּתַר עוֹבָדֵי יְדֵיהוֹן יָתִי קָרִיב יְיָ לְדַחַלְתֵּיהּ מִבֵּית אַבָּא** וַאֲמָרִית לַהּ דֵּין (נ"י דָּא) **טֵיבוּתִיךְ** דִּי תַעְבְּדִי עִמִּי לְכָל אַתְרָא **דִּנְהַךְ לְתַמָּן** אֱמָרִי עֲלַי אָחִי הוּא:


When Elohim caused me to wander from my father’s house [**When the nations strayed after the works of their hands, Elohim brought me close to His worship from my father’s house**], I said to her, this is the kindness [**goodness**] you shall do for me. At every place we come [**go**] to, say of me, He is my brother.


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:14)


וּדְבַר אֲבִימֶלֶךְ עָאן וְתוֹרִין וְעַבְדִין וְאַמְהָן וִיהַב לְאַבְרָהָם וַאֲתֵיב לֵיהּ יָת שָׂרָה אִתְּתֵיהּ:


Avimelech took sheep, cattle, male and female slaves, and he gave them to Avraham. He [also] returned his wife Sarah to him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:15)


וַאֲמַר אֲבִימֶלֶךְ הָא אַרְעִי קֳדָמָךְ בִּדְתַקִּין בְּעֵינָיךְ תִּיב:


Avimelech said, Behold, my land is before you. Live wherever you see fit.


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:16)


וּלְשָׂרָה אֲמַר הָא יְהָבִית אֶלֶף **סַלְעִין** דִּכְסַף לְאָחִיךְ **הָא הוּא לִיךְ כְּסוּת דִּיקָר (עַיְנִין) חֳלַף דִּשְׁלָחִית דְּבַרְתִּיךְ וַחֲזֵית יָתִיךְ וְיָת כָּל דְּעִמָּךְ וְעַל כָּל מַה דַּאֲמָרַת אִתּוֹכָחַת**:


To Sarah, he said, Behold I have given to your brother a thousand pieces [**shekalim**] of silver. It will be for you a [compensational] eye covering for all who are with you, that you may face every one. [**It will be for you a garment of honor, in return for my having sent for you, and taken you, and seeing you and all that is with you. And regarding everything that you said, you were just.**]


###### Onkelos Genesis 20:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:17)


וְצַלִּי אַבְרָהָם קֳדָם יְיָ וְאַסִּי יְיָ יָת אֲבִימֶלֶךְ וְיָת אִתְּתֵיהּ וְאַמְהָתֵיהּ **וְאִתְרְוָחוּ**:


Avraham prayed to [**before**] Elohim, and Elohim healed Avimelech, his wife, and his female slaves, and they gave birth [**and they were relieved**].

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/20:18)

For Adonoy had restrained every womb in the house of Avimelech according to the word of Sarah, Avraham’s wife.



## Chapter 21

###### Onkelos Genesis 21:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:1)


וַייָ דְּכִיר יָת שָׂרָה כְּמָא דִּי אֲמָר וַעֲבַד יְיָ לְשָׁרָה כְּמָא דִּי מַלִּיל:


Adonoy remembered Sarah as He had said, and Adonoy did for Sarah as He had spoken.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:2)


וְעַדִּיאַת וִילִידַת שָׂרָה לְאַבְרָהָם בַּר לְסִיבְתוֹהִי לִזְמַן דִּי מַלִּיל יָתֵיהּ יְיָ:


She conceived and Sarah gave birth to Avraham’s son in his old age, at the designated time that Elohim had declared.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:3)


וּקְרָא אַבְרָהָם יָת שׁוּם בְּרֵיהּ דְּאִתְיְלִיד לֵיהּ דִּילִידַת לֵיהּ שָׂרָה יִצְחָק:


Avraham named his son that was born to him, to which Sarah had borne to him, Yitzchok.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:4)


וּגְזַר אַבְרָהָם יָת יִצְחָק בְּרֵיהּ בַּר תְּמַנְיָא יוֹמִין כְּמָא דִי פַקִּיד יָתֵיהּ יְיָ:


Avraham circumcised his son, Yitzchok, when he was eight days old, as Elohim had commanded him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:5)


וְאַבְרָהָם בַּר מְאָה שְׁנִין כַּד אִתְיְלִיד לֵיהּ יָת יִצְחָק בְּרֵיהּ:


Avraham was one hundred years old when his son Yitzchok was born to him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:6)


וַאֲמֶרֶת שָׂרָה **חֶדְוָא** עֲבַד לִי יְיָ כָּל דִּשְׁמַע **יֶחֱדֵּי** לִי:


Sarah said, Elohim has given me laughter [**gladness**]. All who hear will laugh [**rejoice**] with me.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:7)


וַאֲמֶרֶת **מָאן מְהֵימָן דַּאֲמַר לְאַבְרָהָם קְיָם** דְּתוֹנִיק בְּנִין שָׂרָה אֲרֵי יְלֵידִית בַּר לְסִיבְתוֹהִי:


She said, Who said to Avraham [**Who is faithful that he said to Avraham and fulfills His promise**], that Sarah would nurse children? For I have given birth to a son in his old age.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:8)


וּרְבָא רַבְיָא וְאִתְחֲסִיל וַעֲבַד אַבְרָהָם מִשְׁתְּיָא רַבָּא בְּיוֹמָא דְּאִתְחֲסִיל יָת יִצְחָק:


The child grew and was weaned. Avraham made a great feast on the day Yitzchok was weaned.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:9)


וַחֲזַת שָׂרָה יָת בַּר הָגָר מִצְרֵיתָא דִּיְלִידַת לְאַבְרָהָם מְחָיִיךְ:


Sarah saw that the son of Hagar, the Egyptian, that she had born to Avraham, was mocking.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:10)


וַאֲמֶרֶת לְאַבְרָהָם תָּרֵךְ אַמְתָא הָדָא וְיַת בְּרַהּ אֲרֵי לָא יֵרַת בַּר אַמְתָא הָדָא עִם בְּרִי עִם יִצְחָק:


She said to Avraham, Drive out this slave-woman and her son, for the son of this slave-woman will not inherit with my son, with Yitzchok.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:11)


וּבְאֵישׁ פִּתְגָּמָא לַחֲדָא בְּעֵינֵי אַבְרָהָם עַל עֵיסַק בְּרֵיהּ:


This thing was very wrong in the eyes of Avraham, on account of his son.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:12)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְאַבְרָהָם לָא יַבְאֵשׁ בְּעֵינָיךְ עַל עוּלֵימָא וְעַל אַמְתָךְ כֹּל דִּי תֵימַר לָךְ שָׂרָה **קַבֵּל מִנַּהּ** אֲרֵי בְיִצְחָק יִתְקְרוּן לָךְ בְּנִין:


Elohim said to Avraham, Do not consider this wrong in your eyes on account of the boy and your slave-woman. Regarding all that Sarah tells you, listen to her voice [**accept from her**], for [only] through Yitzchok will seed be considered yours.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:13)


וְאַף יָת בַּר אַמְתָא לְעַם אֲשַׁוִּנֵּיהּ אֲרֵי בְנָךְ הוּא:


[But] also the son of the slave-woman I will make into a nation, for he is [of] your seed.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:14)


וְאַקְדֵּים אַבְרָהָם בְּצַפְרָא וּנְסִיב לַחֲמָא וְרָקְבָּא דְמַיָּא וִיהַב לְהָגָר שַׁוִּי עַל כַּתְפַּהּ וְיָת רַבְיָא וְשַׁלְחַהּ וַאֲזָלַת וְתָעַת בְּמַדְבְּרָא (נ"י בְּמַדְבֵּר) בְּאֵר שָׁבַע:


Avraham got up early in the morning. He took bread and a skin [pouch] of water, and gave it to Hagar. He placed it on her shoulder with the lad, and sent her away. She went and lost her way in the desert of Beer Sheva.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:15)


וּשְׁלִימוּ מַיָּא מִן רָקְבָּא וּרְמַת יָת רַבְיָא תְּחוֹת חַד מִן אִילָנַיָּא:


The water in the skin was used up, and she threw the lad under one of the bushes.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:16)


וַאֲזַלַּת וִיתִיבַת לַהּ מִקֳּבֵל אַרְחֵיקַת (נ"י אַרְחִיק) כְּמֵיגַר קַשְׁתָּא אֲרֵי אֲמֶרֶת לָא אֶחֱזֵי בְּמוֹתָא דְרַבְיָא וִיתִיבַת מִקֳּבֵל וַאֲרִימַת יָת קָלַהּ וּבְכָת:


She went and sat facing him, about [the distance] of bow-shots away. She said, Let me not see the lad die. She sat facing him and wept in a loud voice.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:17)


**וּשְׁמִיעַ קֳדָם יְיָ** יָת קָלֵיהּ דְּרַבְיָא וּקְרָא מַלְאֲכָא דַיְיָ לְהָגָר מִן שְׁמַיָּא וַאֲמַר לַהּ מָה לִיךְ הָגָר לָא תִדְחֲלִי אֲרֵי **שְׁמִיעַ קֳדָם יְיָ** יָת קָלֵיהּ דְּרַבְיָא בַּאֲתַר דְּהוּא תַמָּן:


Elohim heard the voice of the lad [**was heard before Elohim**]. An angel of God called to Hagar from heaven and said to her, What is the matter, Hagar? Do not fear. Elohim has heard the voice of the lad [**has been heard before Elohim**] in the place where he is.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:18)

Get up and lift up the lad. Keep your hand strong on him, for I will make him a great nation.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:19

Elohim opened [**uncovered**] her eyes, and she saw a well of water. She went and filled the skin with water, and gave the lad to drink.

###### Onkelos Genesis 21:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:21)

He lived in the desert of Paran, and his mother took a wife for him from the land of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:22)


וַהֲוָה בְּעִדָּנָא הַהִיא וַאֲמַר אֲבִימֶלֶךְ וּפִיכֹל רַב חֵילֵיהּ לְאַבְרָהָם לְמֵימָר **מֵימְרָא דַיְיָ בְּסַעֲדָךְ בְּכֹל דִּי אַתְּ עָבֵד**:


It was at this time, Avimelech and Pichol, his general spoke to Avraham, saying, Elohim is with you in all that you do [**the Word of Elohim is your support in all that you do**].

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:23)

Now, swear to me here, by [**the Word of**] Elohim, that you will not deal falsely with me, with my son, or my grandson. The kindness [**goodness**] that I have done to you, do to me and to the land in which you lived for a while.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:24)

Avraham then reprimanded Avimelech regarding the well of water that Avimelech’s servants had taken by force.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:26)


וַאֲמַר אֲבִימֶלֶךְ לָא יְדָעִית מָאן עֲבַד יָת פִּתְגָּמָא הָדֵין וְאַף אַתְּ לָא חַוֵּיתָ לִּי וְאַף אֲנָא לָא שְׁמָעִית אֶלָּהֵין יוֹמָא דֵין:


Avimelech said, I do not know who did this thing. You also never told me, and I also heard nothing of it until today.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:27)


וּדְבַר אַבְרָהָם עָאן וְתוֹרִין וִיהַב לַאֲבִימֶלֶךְ וּגְזָרוּ תַרְוֵיהוֹן קְיָם:


Avraham took sheep and cattle and gave them to Avimelech. The two of them made a covenant.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:28)


וַאֲקִים אַבְרָהָם יָת שְׁבַע חוּרְפַן דְּעָאן בִּלְחוֹדֵיהֶן:


Avraham set seven ewes apart by themselves.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:29)


וַאֲמַר אֲבִימֶלֶךְ לְאַבְרָהָם מָה אִנּוּן שְׁבַע חוּרְפַן אִלֵּין דַּאֲקֵמְתָּא בִּלְחוֹדֵיהֶן:


Avimelech said to Avraham. What is the reason for these seven ewes that you have set apart?


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:30)


וַאֲמַר אֲרֵי יָת שְׁבַע חוּרְפַן תְּקַבֵּל מִן יְדִי בְּדִיל דִּתְהֵי לִי לְסַהֲדוּ אֲרֵי חֲפָרִית יָת בֵּירָא הָדֵין (נ"י הָדָא):


He [Avraham] said, Take these seven ewes from my hand so that it will be proof for me, that I dug this well.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:31)


עַל כֵּן קְרָא לְאַתְרָא הַהוּא בְּאֵר שָׁבַע אֲרֵי תַמָּן קַיָּמוּ תַּרְוֵיהוֹן:


Therefore he called that place Beer Sheva, since the two had made an oath there.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:32)


וּגְזָרוּ קְיָם בִּבְאֵר שָׁבַע וְקָם אֲבִימֶלֶךְ וּפִיכֹל רַב חֵילֵיהּ וְתָבוּ לְאַרַע פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי:


They made a covenant in Beer Sheva. Avimelech, and Pichol, his general, then rose and returned to the land of the Philistines.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:33)


וּנְצֵיב נִצְבָּא (נ"י אִילָנָא) בִּבְאֵר שָׁבַע **וְצַלִּי תַמָּן בִּ**שְׁמָא דַיְיָ אֱלָהָא דְעַלְמָא:


Avraham planted an *eishel* [tree] in Beer Sheva, and there he proclaimed [**and there he prayed in**] the Name Adonoy, Almighty of the universe.


###### Onkelos Genesis 21:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/21:34)


וְאִתּוֹתַב אַבְרָהָם בְּאַרַע פְּלִשְׁתָּאֵי יוֹמִין סַגִּיאִין:


Avraham lived in the land of the Philistines for many days.



## Chapter 22

###### Onkelos Genesis 22:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:1)


וַהֲוָה בָּתַר פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין וַייָ נַסִּי יָת אַבְרָהָם וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ אַבְרָהָם וַאֲמַר הָא אֲנָא:


After these events, Elohim tested Avraham and said to him, Avraham! and he [Avraham] said, Here I am.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:2)


וַאֲמַר דְּבַר **כְּעַן** יָת בְּרָךְ יָת יְחִידָךְ דִּי רְחַמְתָּ יָת יִצְחָק וְאִיזֵל לָךְ לְאַרְעָא **פּוּלְחָנָא** וְאַסְקֵיהּ **קֳדָמַי** תַּמָּן לַעֲלָתָא עַל חַד מִן טוּרַיָּא דִּי אֵימָר לָךְ:


He said, Please [**Now**] take your son, your only one, who you love—Yitzchok—and go to the land of Moriah [**worship**]. Sacrifice him [**before me**] as a burnt-offering on one of the mountains which I will designate to you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:3)


וְאַקְדֵּים אַבְרָהָם בְּצַפְרָא וְזָרֵז יָת חֲמָרֵיהּ וּדְבַר יָת תְּרֵין עוּלֵימוֹהִי עִמֵּיהּ וְיָת יִצְחָק בְּרֵיהּ וְצַלַּח אָעֵי לַעֲלָתָא וְקָם וְאָזַל לְאַתְרָא דִּי אֲמַר לֵיהּ יְיָ:


Avraham awoke early in the morning, saddled his donkey, and took his two assistants with him, and also his son, Yitzchok. He split the wood of the burnt-offering, rose, and went to the place that Elohim had designated to him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:4)


בְּיוֹמָא תְלִיתָאָה וּזְקַף אַבְרָהָם יָת עֵינוֹהִי וַחֲזָא יָת אַתְרָא מֵרָחִיק:


On the third day, Avraham raised his eyes and saw the place from afar.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:5)


וַאֲמַר אַבְרָהָם לְעוּלֵימוֹהִי אוֹרִיכוּ לְכוֹן הָכָא עִם חֲמָרָא וַאֲנָא וְעוּלֵימָא נִתְמְטֵי עַד כָּא וְנִסְגּוּד וּנְתוּב לְוַתְכוֹן:


Avraham said to his assistants, Remain here with the donkey and I and the lad will go to that place. We will prostrate ourselves [in worship] and return to you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:6)


וּנְסֵיב אַבְרָהָם יָת אָעֵי דַעֲלָתָא וְשַׁוִּי עַל יִצְחָק בְּרֵיהּ וּנְסֵיב בִּידֵיהּ יָת אֶשָּׁתָא וְיָת סַכִּינָא וַאֲזָלוּ תַרְוֵיהוֹן בַּתְרָא (כַּחֲדָא):


Avraham took the wood of the burnt-offering and placed it on his son Yitzchok. In his hand he took the fire and the knife, and they both went together.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:7)


וַאֲמַר יִצְחָק לְאַבְרָהָם אֲבוּהִי וַאֲמַר אַבָּא וַאֲמַר הָא אֲנָא בְרִי וַאֲמַר הָא אֶשָּׁתָא וְאָעַיָּא וְאָן אִימְרָא לַעֲלָתָא:


Yitzchok spoke to Avraham, his father, and said, Father, and he [Avraham] said, Here I am, my son. He said, Here are the fire and the wood, but where is the lamb for the burnt-offering?


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:8)


וַאֲמַר אַבְרָהָם **קֳדָם יְיָ גְּלֵי לֵיהּ אִימְרָא** לַעֲלָתָא בְּרִי וַאֲזָלוּ תַרְוֵיהוֹן כַּחֲדָא:


Avraham said, Elohim will show the lamb [**the lamb is revealed before Elohim**] for a burnt-offering, my son. And the two of them went together.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:9)


וְאָתוּ לְאַתְרָא דִּי אֲמַר לֵיהּ יְיָ וּבְנָא תַמָּן אַבְרָהָם יָת מַדְבְּחָא וְסַדַּר יָת אָעַיָּא וַעֲקַד יָת יִצְחָק בְּרֵיהּ וְשַׁוִּי יָתֵיהּ עַל מַדְבְּחָא עֵיל מִן אָעַיָּא:


They came to the place that Elohim had designated to him. Avraham built the altar there, and arranged the wood. He bound his son Yitzchok, and placed him on the altar, on top of the wood.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:10)


וְאוֹשֵׁיט אַבְרָהָם יָת יְדֵיהּ וּנְסֵיב יָת סַכִּינָא לְמֵיכַס יָת בְּרֵיהּ:


Avraham extended his hand and took the knife to slaughter his son.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:11)


וּקְרָא לֵיהּ מַלְאָכָא דַיְיָ מִן שְׁמַיָּא וַאֲמַר אַבְרָהָם אַבְרָהָם וַאֲמַר הָא אֲנָא:


An angel of Adonoy called to him from heaven and said, Avraham, Avraham! and he said, Here I am.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:12)


וַאֲמַר לָא תוֹשֵׁיט יְדָךְ לְעוּלֵימָא וְלָא תַעְבֵּד לֵיהּ מִדָּעָם אֲרֵי כְּעַן יְדַעְנָא (נ"י יְדָעִית) אֲרֵי דַחֲלָא דַיְיָ אַתְּ וְלָא מְנַעְתָּ יָת בְּרָךְ יָת יְחִידָךְ מִנִּי:


He [God] said, Do not touch the lad, nor do anything to [harm] him; for now I know that you are one who fears Elohim and have not withheld your son, your only one, from Me.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:13)


וּזְקַף אַבְרָהָם יָת עֵינוֹהִי **בָּתַר אִלֵּין** וַחֲזָא וְהָא דִכְרָא בָּתַר אֲחִיד בְּאִילָנָא בְּקַרְנוֹהִי וַאֲזַל אַבְרָהָם וּנְסֵיב יָת דִּכְרָא וְאַסְקֵיהּ לַעֲלָתָא חֲלַף בְּרֵיהּ:


Avraham looked up [**afterwards**] and beheld a ram after it had been caught in the thicket by its horns. Avraham went and took the ram and sacrificed it as a burnt-offering instead of his son.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:14)


**וּפְלַח וְצַלִּי אַבְרָהָם תַּמָּן בְּאַתְרָא הַהוּא וַאֲמַר קֳדָם יְיָ הָכָא יְהוֹן פַּלְחַן דָּרַיָּא בְּכֵן יִתְאַמַּר בְּיוֹמָא הָדֵין בְּטוּרָא הָדֵין אַבְרָהָם קֳדָם יְיָ פְּלָח**:


Avraham called the name of that place, Adonoy will see; as it is said [to] this day, On Adonoy’s mountain, He will be seen. [**Avraham worshiped and prayed there in that place, and he said before Adonoy, “Here will generations worship”. Therefore, as it is said [to] this day, “On this mountain Avraham worshiped before Adonoy”.**]


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:15)


וּקְרָא מַלְאָכָא דַיְיָ לְאַבְרָהָם תִּנְיָנוּת מִן שְׁמַיָּא:


An angel of Adonoy called to Avraham, a second time, from heaven.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:16)


וַאֲמַר בְּמֵימְרֵי קַיֵּמִית אֲמַר יְיָ אֲרֵי חֲלַף דִּי עֲבַדְתָּא יָת פִּתְגָּמָא הָדֵין וְלָא מְנַעְתָּא יָת בְּרָךְ יָת יְחִידָךְ:


And said, I have sworn by Myself [**My Word**], declares Adonoy, that because you performed this deed, and did not withhold your only son.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:17)


אֲרֵי בָרָכָא אֲבָרְכִנָּךְ וְאַסְגָּאָה אַסְגֵּי יָת בְּנָךְ כְּכוֹכְבֵי שְׁמַיָּא וּכְחָלָא דִּי עַל כֵּיף יַמָּא וְיִרְתוּן בְּנָךְ יָת **קִרְוֵי** סַנְאֵיהוֹן:


I will greatly bless you and make your descendants as numerous as the stars of the sky and like the sand on the seashore. Your descendants will inherit the gate [**cities**] of their enemies.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:18)


וְיִתְבָּרְכוּן **בְּדִיל** בְּנָךְ כֹּל עַמְמַיָּא דְאַרְעָא חֲלַף דִּי קַבֵּלְתָּא **לְמֵימְרִי**:


Through [**Because of**] your children, will be blessed all the nations of the world, because you heeded My voice [**Word**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:19)


וְתַב אַבְרָהָם לְוַת עוּלֵימוֹהִי וְקָמוּ וַאֲזָלוּ כַּחֲדָא לִבְאֵר שָׁבַע וִיתֵיב אַבְרָהָם בִּבְאֵר שָׁבַע:


Avraham returned to his assistants, and they rose and went together to Beer Sheva. Avraham dwelt in Beer Sheva.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:20)


וַהֲוָה בָּתַר פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין וְאִתְחֲוָא לְאַבְרָהָם לְמֵימָר הָא יְלֵידַת מִלְכָּה אַף הִיא בְּנִין לְנָחוֹר אָחוּךְ:


After these words, it was told to Avraham, saying, Behold, Milkah has also born children to Nachor, your brother.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:21)


יָת עוּץ בּוּכְרֵיהּ וְיָת בּוּז אָחוּהִי וְיָת קְמוּאֵל אֲבוּהִי דַאֲרָם:


Utz, his first born, Buz his brother, and Kemueil, the father of Aram.


###### Onkelos Genesis 22:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/22:22)


וְיָת כֶּשֶׂד וְיָת חֲזוֹ וְיָת פִּלְדָּשׁ וְיָת יִדְלָף וְיָת בְּתוּאֵל:

Avraham was old, advanced in days [years], and Adonoy had blessed Avraham in all things.

I will have you swear by [**the Word of**] Adonoy, God of heaven and God of earth, that you will not take a wife for my son from the daughters of the Canaanites, among whom I live.

Instead, to my [native] land, to my birthplace, shall you go, and take a wife for my son, for Yitzchok,


###### Onkelos Genesis 24:5

Avraham said to him, Take care, not to bring my son back there.


###### Onkelos Genesis 24:7

יְיָ אֱלָהָא דִשְׁמַיָּא דִּי דַבְּרַנִי מִבֵּית אַבָּא וּמֵאֲרַע יַלְדוּתִי וְדִי מַלֵּל לִי וְדִי קַיֵּם לִי לְמֵימַר לִבְרָךְ אֶתֵּן יָת אַרְעָא הָדָא הוּא יִשְׁלַח מַלְאָכֵיהּ קֳדָמָךְ וְתִסַּב אִתְּתָא לִבְרִי מִתַּמָּן:


Adonoy, God of heaven, Who took me from my father’s house, and from the land of my birth, Who spoke to me, and Who swore to me, saying, To your descendants I will give this land—He will send His angel before you, and you shall take a wife for my son from there.


###### Onkelos Genesis 24:8

וְאִם לָא תֵיבֵי אִתְּתָא לְמֵיתֵי בַתְרָךְ וּתְהֵי זַכָּאָה מִמּוֹמָתִי דָּא לְחוֹד יָת בְּרִי לָא תָתֵב לְתַמָּן:

## Chapter 25

###### Onkelos Genesis 25:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/25:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/25:1)

Avraham again took a wife. Her name was Keturah.


###### Onkelos Genesis 25:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/25:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/25:2)

She bore him Zimran, Yokshon, Medan, Midian, Yishbok, and Shuach.

## Chapter 26

###### Onkelos Genesis 26:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/26:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/26:1)


וַהֲוָה כַפְנָא בְּאַרְעָא בַּר מִכַּפְנָא קַדְמָאָה דִּי הֲוָה בְּיוֹמֵי אַבְרָהָם וַאֲזַל יִצְחָק לְוַת אֲבִימֶלֶךְ מַלְכָּא דִפְלִשְׁתָּאֵי לִגְרָר:

###### Onkelos Genesis 28:1

Yitzchok called Yaakov and blessed him. He commanded him and said to him, Do not marry a woman of the daughters of Canaan.

Set out and go to Padan Aram, to the house of Besueil, your mother’s father, and marry one of the daughters of Lavan, your mother’s brother.

May the Almighty, Shaddai, bless you, make you fruitful and multiply you. May you become an assembly of peoples [**tribes**].

May He give you the blessing of Avraham, to you and to your descendants with you, that you may inherit the land of your dwelling which God gave to Avraham.


###### Onkelos Genesis 28:5

###### Onkelos Genesis 28:11

וַעֲרַע בְּאַתְרָא וּבָת תַּמָּן אֲרֵי עַל שִׁמְשָׁא וּנְסִיב מֵאַבְנֵי אַתְרָא וְשַׁוִּי אִסָּדוֹהִי וּשְׁכִיב בְּאַתְרָא הַהוּא:


He reached the place and spent the night there because the sun had set. He took some of the stones of that place, and arranged them around his head, and lay down [to sleep] in that place.


###### Onkelos Genesis 28:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:12)


וַחֲלַם וְהָא סֻלְמָא **נָעִיץ** בְּאַרְעָא וְרֵישֵׁיהּ מָטֵי עַד צֵית שְׁמַיָּא וְהָא מַלְאָכַיָּא דַיְיָ סָלְקִין וְנָחֲתִין בֵּהּ:


He dreamed, and behold a ladder was set up on [**planted in**] the earth and the top of it reached toward heaven; and behold angels of Elohim were ascending and descending on it.


###### Onkelos Genesis 28:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:13)


וְהָא **יְקָרָא דַ**יְיָ מְעַתַּד עִלָּווֹהִי וַאֲמַר אֲנָא יְיָ אֱלָהֵהּ דְּאַבְרָהָם אָבוּךְ וֵאֱלָהֵהּ דְּיִצְחָק אַרְעָא דִּי אַתְּ שָׁרֵי עֲלַהּ לָךְ אֶתְּנִנַּהּ וְלִבְנָךְ:


And behold [**the Glory of**] Adonoy stood over him, and said, I am Adonoy, God of Avraham, your father, and God of Yitzchok. The land upon which you are lying, I will give to you and to your descendants.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:14)


וִיהוֹן בְּנָךְ **סַגִּיאִין** כְּעַפְרָא דְאַרְעָא **וְתִתְקֵף** לְמַעַרְבָא וּלְמָדִינְחָא וּלְצִפּוּנָא וּלְדָרוֹמָא וְיִתְבָּרְכוּן **בְּדִילָךְ** כֹּל זַרְעֲיַת אַרְעָא **וּבְדִיל** בְּנָךְ:


Your descendants shall be [**many**] as the dust of the earth. You shall spread [**be strong**] to the west, to the east, to the north and to the south. Through [**Because of**] you shall be blessed all the families of the earth, and through [**because of**] your descendants.


###### Onkelos Genesis 28:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:15)


וְהָא **מֵימְרִי בְּסַעְדָּךְ** וְאֶטְּרִנָּךְ **בְּכָל אֲתַר** דִּי תְהַךְ וַאֲתֵיבִנָּךְ לְאַרְעָא הָדָא אֲרֵי לָא אֶשְׁבְּקִנָּךְ עַד דִּי אֶעְבֵּיד יָת דְּמַלֵּלִית לָךְ:


Behold, I am with you [**My Word is your support**], and I will guard you wherever [**every place**] you go, and I will bring you back to this land. I will not forsake you until I have done that which I have spoken for you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 28:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:16)


וְאִתְּעַר יַעֲקֹב מִשִּׁנְתֵּיהּ וַאֲמַר בְּקוּשְׁטָא (אִית) **יְקָרָא דַיְיָ שָׁרֵי** בְּאַתְרָא הָדֵין וַאֲנָא **לָא הֲוֵיתִי יְדָע**:


Yaakov awoke from his sleep, and said, In truth, [**the Glory of**] Adonoy is [**dwells**] in this place, and I did not know it [**would not have known it**].

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:17)


וּדְחִיל וַאֲמַר מָה דְּחִילוּ אַתְרָא הָדֵין **לֵית דֵּין אֲתַר הֶדְיוֹט אֶלָּהֵין אֲתַר דְּרַעֲוָא בֵיהּ מִן קֳדָם יְי**ָ וְדֵין תְּרַע **קֳבֵל** שְׁמַיָּא:


He was afraid and said, How awesome is this place! This is none other than the house of Elohim [**not a regular place but a place where it is the will of Elohim to receive prayers**], and this is the gate of [**opposite**] heaven.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:18)


וְאַקְדֵּים יַעֲקֹב בְּצַפְרָא וּנְסִיב יָת אַבְנָא דִּי שַׁוִּי אִסָּדוֹהִי וְשַׁוִּי יָתַהּ קָמָא וַאֲרִיק מִשְׁחָא עַל רֵישַׁהּ:


Yaakov rose early in the morning and took the stone that he had placed at his head; and he set it as a monument, and poured oil on its top.

וּקְרָא יָת שְׁמָא דְאַתְרָא הַהוּא בֵּית אֵל וּבְרַם לוּז שְׁמָא דְקַרְתָּא בְּקַדְמֵיתָא:


He named that place Beis Eil, but Luz was the original name of the city.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:20)


וְקַיִּים יַעֲקֹב קְיָם לְמֵימָר אִם יְהֵא **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ **בְּסַעְדִּי** וְיִטְּרִנַּנִי בְּאָרְחָא הָדֵין דִּי אֲנָא אָזֵל וְיִתֶּן לִי לַחֲמָא (נ"א לְחֵם) לְמֵיכַל וּכְסוּ לְמִלְבָּשׁ:


Yaakov made a vow, saying, If [**the Word of**] Elohim will be with me [**my support**], and guards me on this path that I am going, and gives me bread to eat and clothing to wear;

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:21)

And if I return in peace to my father’s house, and [**the Word of**] Adonoy will be my God;

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/28:22)

[Then] this stone which I have set [as] a monument will become a House of God [**place where I will worship upon it before God**], and of all that You give, I will surely give a tenth to [**before**] You.



## Chapter 29

###### Onkelos Genesis 29:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:1)


וּנְטַל יַעֲקֹב רַגְלוֹהִי (נ"א רִיגְלוֹהִי) וַאֲזַל לַאֲרַע בְּנֵי מָדִינְחָא:


Yaakov [then] lifted his feet and went toward the land of the people of the East.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:2)


וַחֲזָא וְהָא בֵירָא בְחַקְלָא וְהָא תַמָּן תְּלָתָא עֶדְרִין דְּעָן רְבִיעִין עֲלַהּ אֲרֵי מִן בֵּירָא הַהִיא מַשְׁקָן עֲדָרַיָּא וְאַבְנָא רַבְּתָא עַל פּוּמָא דְבֵירָא:


He looked and [saw] a well in the field; and behold there were three flocks of sheep lying beside it, for from that well the flocks are watered. There was a large stone over the mouth of the well.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:3)


וּמִתְכַּנְּשִׁין לְתַמָּן כָּל עֲדָרַיָּא וּמְגַנְדְּרִין יָת אַבְנָא מֵעַל פּוּמָא דְבֵירָא וּמַשְׁקָן יָת עָנָא וּמְתִיבִין יָת אַבְנָא עַל פּוּמָא דְבֵירָא לְאַתְרַהּ:


When all the flocks would gather, [the shepherds] would roll the stone from the well’s mouth, and water the sheep. They would [then] return the stone to its place over the mouth of the well.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:4)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן יַעֲקֹב אַחַי מְנָן אַתּוּן וַאֲמָרוּ מֵחָרָן אֲנָחְנָא:


Yaakov said to them, My brothers, from where do you come? They said, We are from Charan.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:5)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן הַיְדַעְתּוּן יָת לָבָן בַּר נָחוֹר וַאֲמָרוּ יְדַעְנָן:


He said to them, Do you know Lavan, the son of Nachor? They said, We know him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:6)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן הַשְׁלַם לֵיהּ וַאֲמָרוּ שְׁלַם וְהָא רָחֵל בְּרַתֵּיהּ אָתְיָא עִם עָנָא:


He said to them, Is he doing well? They said, He is doing well. Behold Rochel, his daughter, is coming with the sheep.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:7)


וַאֲמַר הָא עוֹד יוֹמָא סַגִּי לָא עִדַּן לְמִכְנַשׁ בְּעִיר אַשְׁקוּ עָנָא וְאִזִּילוּ רְעוּ:


He said to them, The day is yet long, it is not yet time for the sheep to be gathered, water the sheep and go pasture them.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:8)


וַאֲמָרוּ לָא נִכּוּל עַד דִּי מִתְכַּנְּשִׁין כָּל עֲדָרַיָּא וִיגַנְדְּרוּן יָת אַבְנָא מֵעַל פּוּמָא דְבֵירָא וְנַשְׁקֵי עָנָא:


They said, We cannot [do so] until all the flocks have gathered and [the shepherds] roll the stone from the mouth of the well. Then we can water the sheep.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:9)


עַד דְּהוּא מְמַלֵּל עִמְּהוֹן וְרָחֵל אָתַת עִם עָנָא דִּי לַאֲבוּהָ אֲרֵי רָעִיתָא הִיא:


While he was still speaking with them, Rochel came with her father’s sheep, for she was a shepherdess.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:10)


וַהֲוָה כַּד חֲזָא יַעֲקֹב יָת רָחֵל בַּת לָבָן אֲחוּהָא דְאִמֵּיהּ וְיָת עָנָא דְלָבָן אֲחוּהָא דְאִמֵּיהּ וּקְרֵב יַעֲקֹב וְגַנְדַּר יָת אַבְנָא מֵעַל פּוּמָא דְבֵירָא וְאַשְׁקֵי יָת עָנָא דְלָבָן אֲחוּהָא דְאִמֵּיהּ:


When Yaakov saw Rochel, the daughter of Lavan, his mother’s brother, with the sheep of Lavan, his mother’s brother, he stepped near and rolled the stone from the mouth of the well. He then watered the sheep of Lavan, his mother’s brother.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:11)


וּנְשַׁק יַעֲקֹב לְרָחֵל וַאֲרֵם יָת קָלֵיהּ וּבְכָא:


Yaakov kissed Rochel and cried in a loud voice.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:12)


וְחַוִּי יַעֲקֹב לְרָחֵל אֲרֵי **בַר אֲחָה דְאָבוּהָ** הוּא וַאֲרֵי בַר רִבְקָה הוּא וּרְהָטַת וְחַוִּיאַת לְאָבוּהָ:


Yaakov told Rochel that he was a relative of her father [**the son of her father’s sister**], that he was the son of Rivkah. She ran and told her father.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:13)


וַהֲוָה כַּד שְׁמַע לָבָן יָת שֵׁמַע יַעֲקֹב בַּר אֲחָתֵיהּ וּרְהַט לְקַדָּמוּתֵיהּ וְגַפֵּף לֵיהּ וְנַשִּׁיק לֵיהּ וְאַעֲלֵיהּ לְבֵיתֵיהּ וְאִשְׁתָּעִי לְלָבָן יָת כָּל פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין:


When Lavan heard the news of Yaakov, his sister’s son, he ran to greet him. He embraced him and kissed him, and brought him to his house. [Yaakov] told Lavan all that had happened.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:14)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ לָבָן בְּרַם **קָרִיבִי** וּבִשְׂרִי אָתְּ וִיתֵיב עִמֵּיהּ יְרַח יוֹמִין:


Lavan said to him, Nevertheless, you are my bone [**relative**] and flesh. [Yaakov] lived with him for a month.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:15)


וַאֲמַר לָבָן לְיַעֲקֹב הֲמִדְּאָחִי אַתְּ וְתִפְלְחִנַּנִי מַגָּן חַוִּי לִי מָה אַגְרָךְ:


Lavan said to Yaakov, Just because you are my relative, must you work for me without pay? Tell me, [what should] your wages [be]?


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:16)


וּלְלָבָן תַּרְתֵּין בְּנָן שׁוּם רַבְּתָא לֵאָה וְשׁוּם זְעֶרְתָּא רָחֵל:


Lavan had two daughters. The name of the older one was Leah, and the name of the younger one was Rochel.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:17)


וְעֵינֵי לֵאָה **יָאֲיָן** וְרָחֵל הֲוַת שַׁפִּירָא בְרֵיוָא וְיָאֲיָא בְחֶזְוָא:


The eyes of Leah were tender [**beautiful**]. Rochel was of beautiful form and of beautiful appearance.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/29:18)


וּרְחֵם יַעֲקֹב יָת רָחֵל וַאֲמַר אֶפְלְחִנָּךְ שְׁבַע שְׁנִין בְּרָחֵל בְּרַתָּךְ זְעֶרְתָּא:


Yaakov loved Rochel. and he said, I will work for you seven years for Rochel, your younger daughter.


###### Onkelos Genesis 29:19

Lavan said, Better that I give her to you than I should give her to another man. [Remain] living with me.

Yaakov worked seven years for Rochel, but they seemed to him like a few days, so much did he love her.

Yaakov said to Lavan, Give me my wife, for my term [**of labor**] is completed; and thus I will consummate the marriage with her.

Lavan gathered all the local people and he made a [wedding] feast.

When it was evening, he took Leah, his daughter, and brought her to him [Yaakov]. He consummated the marriage with her.

Lavan gave Zilpah, his servant to her, to Leah, his daughter as a handmaid.

Complete the [marriage] week for this one. We will then give you the other also—in return for the work that you will do with me for another seven years.

To his daughter, Rochel, Lavan gave his servant Bilhah, to be her handmaid.

Adonoy saw that Leah was the hated one, and He opened her womb [**allowed her to conceive**]. Rochel remained barren.

Leah conceived and gave birth to a son. She named him Reuvein, as she said, Adonoy has seen my affliction [**was revealed before Adonoy**]; for now my husband will love me.

She conceived again and gave birth to a son. She said, Since Adonoy [**it was**] heard [**before Adonoy**] that I am the hated one, He also gave me this one, and she named him Shimon.

She conceived again and gave birth to a son. She said, This time my husband will become attached to me, for I have born him three sons. He therefore named him Leivi.
