She conceived again and gave birth to a son. She said, This time I will [**give**] praise [**before**] Adonoy. She therefore named him Yehudah. She then stopped giving birth.



## Chapter 30

###### Onkelos Genesis 30:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:1)


וַחֲזַת רָחֵל אֲרֵי לָא יְלִידַת לְיַעֲקֹב וְקַנִּיאַת רָחֵל בַּאֲחָתַהּ וַאֲמֶרֶת לְיַעֲקֹב הַב לִי בְנִין וְאִם לָא מֵיתָא אֲנָא:


Rochel saw that she was not bearing children to Yaakov. Rochel became jealous of her sister, and she said to Yaakov, Give me children; if not I am [considered] dead.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:2)


וּתְקֵיף רוּגְזָא דְיַעֲקֹב בְּרָחֵל וַאֲמַר **הָא מִנִּי אַתְּ בַּעְיָא הֲלָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ תִּבְעִין** דִּי מְנַע מִנִּיךְ וַלְדָא דִמְעִין:


Yaakov became very angry with Rochel, and he said, Am I in God’s place? [**Are you asking me? Is it not from before God that you should ask?**] [It is He] who has withheld from you the fruit of the womb.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:3)


וַאֲמֶרֶת הָא אַמְתִי בִלְהָה עוּל לְוָתַהּ וּתְלִיד וַאֲנָא **אֱרַבֵּי וְאֶתְבְּנֵי** אַף אֲנָא מִנַּהּ:


She said, Here is my handmaid, Bilhah, consummate a marriage with her. Let her give birth upon my knees [**and I will rear them**], and I too will have a son [**be built up**] through her.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:4)


וִיהָבַת לֵיהּ יָת בִּלְהָה אַמְתַהּ לְאִנְתּוּ וְעַל לְוָתַהּ יַעֲקֹב:


She gave him Bilhah, her handmaid, as a wife and Yaakov consummated the marriage with her.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:5)


וְעַדִּיאַת בִּלְהָה וִילִידַת לְיַעֲקֹב בָּר:


Bilhah conceived and bore Yaakov a son.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:6)


וַאֲמֶרֶת רָחֵל דְּנַנִּי יְיָ וְאַף **קַבִּיל צְלוֹתִי** וִיהַב לִי בָּר עַל כֵּן קְרַת שְׁמֵיהּ דָּן:


Rochel said, Elohim has judged me. He has also heard my voice [**accepted my prayer**], and has given me a son. She therefore named him Don.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:7)


וְעַדִּיאַת עוֹד וִילִידַת בִּלְהָה אַמְתָא דְרָחֵל בָּר תִּנְיָן לְיַעֲקֹב:


She conceived again, and Bilhah, Rochel’s handmaid, bore a second son to Yaakov.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:8)


וַאֲמֶרֶת רָחֵל **קַבִּיל יְיָ בָּעוּתִי בְּאִתְחַנָּנוּתִי בִּצְלוֹתִי חֲמֵידַת דִּיהֵי לִי וְלַד כַּאֲחָתִי אַף אִתְיְהִיב לִי** וּקְרַת שְׁמֵיהּ נַפְתָּלִי:


Rochel said, With Elohim’s bonds, I have been joined to my sister, and I have also prevailed [**Elohim accepted my request, when I pleaded in my prayer, I desired that I would have offspring like my sister, it was also given to me**]. She [therefore] called him Naftali.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:9)


וַחֲזַת לֵאָה אֲרֵי קָמַת מִלְּמֵילַד וּדְבָרַת יָת זִלְפָּה אַמְתַהּ וִיהָבַת יָתַהּ לְיַעֲקֹב לְאִנְתּוּ:


Leah realized that she was no longer bearing children. She [therefore] took Zilpah, her handmaid, and gave her to Yaakov as a wife.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:10)


וִילִידַת זִלְפָּה אַמְתָא דְלֵאָה לְיַעֲקֹב בָּר:


Zilpah, Leah’s handmaid, bore Yaakov a son.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:11)


וַאֲמֶרֶת לֵאָה אֲתָא גָד וּקְרַת יָת שְׁמֵיהּ גָּד:


Leah said, Unexpected success has come, and she named him Gad.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:12)


וִילִידַת זִלְפָּה אַמְתָא דְלֵאָה בָּר תִּנְיָן לְיַעֲקֹב:


Zilpah, Leah’s handmaid bore a second son to Yaakov.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:13)


וַאֲמֶרֶת לֵאָה **תֻּשְׁבַּחְתָּא הֲוַת לִי אֲרֵי בְכֵן יְשַׁבְּחֻנַּנִי נְשַׁיָּא** וּקְרַת יָת שְׁמֵיהּ אָשֵׁר:


Leah said, It is in my good fortune that daughters [women] will consider me fortunate. [**Praise is mine, because women will praise me from now.**] She named him Asher.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:14)


וַאֲזַל רְאוּבֵן בְּיוֹמֵי חֲצַד חִטִּין וְאַשְׁכַּח יַבְרוּחִין בְּחַקְלָא וְאַיְתִי יָתְהוֹן לְלֵאָה אִמֵּיהּ וַאֲמֶרֶת רָחֵל לְלֵאָה הָבִי **כְעַן** לִי מִיַּבְרוּחֵי דִּבְרִיךְ:


Reuvein went in the days of the wheat harvest, and found jasmine flowers in the field. He brought them to Leah, his mother. Rochel said to Leah, Please [**Now**] give me some of your son’s jasmine flowers.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:15)


וַאֲמֶרֶת לַהּ הַזְעֵיר דִּדְבַרְתְּ יָת בַּעְלִי וְתִסְּבִין אַף יָת יַבְרוּחֵי דִּבְרִי וַאֲמֶרֶת רָחֵל בְּכֵן יִשְׁכּוּב עִמָךְ בְּלֵילְיָא חֳלַף יַבְרוּחֵי דִבְרִיךְ:


She [Leah] said to her, Isn’t it enough that you took my husband? Would you also take my son’s jasmine flowers? Rochel said, Therefore, he shall be with you tonight in exchange for your son’s jasmine flowers.


###### Onkelos Genesis 30:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/30:16)


וַאֲתָא (נ"א וְעַל) יַעֲקֹב מִן חַקְלָא בְּרַמְשָׁא וּנְפָקַת לֵאָה לְקַדָּמוּתֵיהּ וַאֲמֶרֶת לְוָתִי תֵיעוֹל אֲרֵי מֵיגַר אֲגַרְתִּיךְ בְּיַבְרוּחֵי דִּבְרִי וּשְׁכִיב עִמַּהּ בְּלֵילְיָא הוּא:


Yaakov came from the field in the evening, and Leah went out to meet him. She said, You will come to me, for I have hired you with my son’s jasmine flowers, and he was with her at night.

וַאֲמֶרֶת לַאֲבוּהָא לָא יִתְקֵף בְּעֵינֵי רִבּוֹנִי אֲרֵי לָא אִכּוּל לְמֵיקַם מִן קֳדָמָךְ אֲרֵי אֹרַח נְשִׁין לִי וּבְלַשׁ וְלָא אַשְׁכַּח יָת צַלְמָנַיָּא:


She said to her father, Let there not be anger in the eyes of my master that I cannot rise before you, for the way of women is upon me. He searched but did not find the idols.


###### Onkelos Genesis 31:36

וּתְקֵיף לְיַעֲקֹב וּנְצָא עִם לָבָן וַאֲתֵיב יַעֲקֹב וַאֲמַר לְלָבָן מַה חוֹבִי מַה סוּרְחָנִי אֲרֵי רְדַפְתָּא בַּתְרָי:


Yaakov was angry and he argued with Lavan. Yaakov replied and said to Lavan, What is my crime? What sin did I commit that you were in such hot pursuit of me?

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/31:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/31:37)

When you searched all my goods, what did you find of all your household goods? Place it here in the presence of my brethren and your brethren, and let them decide between the two of us.


###### Onkelos Genesis 31:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/31:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/31:38)


דְּנַן עֶסְרִין שְׁנִין אֲנָא עִמָּךְ רְחֵלָךְ וְעִזָּךְ לָא אַתְכִּילוּ וְדִכְרֵי עָנָךְ לָא אֲכָלִית:


These twenty years that I was with you, your ewes and she-goats never miscarried, and I did not eat any rams of your flocks.


###### Onkelos Genesis 31:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/31:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/31:39)


דִּתְבִירָא לָא אַיְתֵיתִי לְוָתָךְ **דַּהֲוָה שָׁגְיָא מִמִּנְיָנָא מִנִּי אַתְּ בָּעֵי לַהּ נְטָרִית בִּימָמָא וּנְטָרִית בְּלֵילְיָא**:


I never brought you a mutilated animal. I will take the blame for it. You demanded compensation from my hand whether [an animal] was stolen from me by day or whether it was stolen from me by night. [**You demanded compensation from me for that which was missing from the total. I guarded by day and I guarded by night.**]

I was consumed by the burning heat by day and ice [**frost came down upon me**] at night. My sleep was taken from my eyes.

דְּנַן לִי עֶסְרִין שְׁנִין בְּבֵיתָךְ פְּלָחִתָּךְ אַרְבַּע עֶסְרֵי שְׁנִין בְּתַרְתֵּין בְּנָתָיךְ וְשִׁתְּ שְׁנִין בְּעָנָךְ וְאַשְׁנֵיתָא יָת אַגְרִי עֲשַׂר זִמְנִין:


These twenty years that I have been in your house, I served you fourteen years for your two daughters, and six years for your sheep, but you changed my wages ten times.


###### Onkelos Genesis 31:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/31:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/31:42)

Had not the God of my father, the God of Avraham and the Fear of Yitzchok been with me [**my support**], you would have sent me away empty-handed. My misery [**toil**] and the labor [**weariness**] of my hands was seen by [**revealed before**] Elohim, and He reprimanded you last night.

Lavan replied and said to Yaakov, The daughters are my daughters, and the sons are my sons, and the[se] flocks are my flocks. All that you see is mine. What can I do unto these, my daughters, today, or to their children that they have born.

Yaakov said to his brethren, Gather stones. They took stones and made a mound, and they ate on the mound.

Lavan called it Yegar Sohadusa, but Yaakov called it Galeid.

Lavan said, This mound is witness between me and you this day. He therefore called its name Galeid.

And [he also called it] Mitzpah [Watchtower] because he said, [**The Word of**] Adonoy will watch between me and you, when we are concealed from each other[’s sight].

Lavan said to Yaakov, Here is this mound, and here is this monument that I have cast [**established**] between me and you.

This mound is witness, and the monument is witness, that I will not cross over to you beyond this mound; and you are not to cross over to me beyond this mound and this monument for harmful purposes.

The God of Avraham and the god of Nachor, the god of their fathers will judge between us. Yaakov swore by the Fear of his father, Yitzchok [**He who Yitchok his father feared**].

וּנְכֵס יַעֲקֹב נִכְסָתָא בְּטוּרָא וּקְרָא לַאֲחוֹהִי לְמֵיכַל לַחֲמָא וַאֲכָלוּ לַחֲמָא וּבִיתוּ בְּטוּרָא:


Yaakov slaughtered animals on the mountain, and invited his brethren to eat bread [a meal]. They ate bread and spent the night on the mountain.



## Chapter 32

###### Onkelos Genesis 32:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:1)


וְאַקְדֵם לָבָן בְּצַפְרָא וּנְשַׁק לִבְנוֹהִי וְלִבְנָתֵיהּ וּבָרִיךְ יָתְהֵן וַאֲזַל וְתַב לָבָן לְאַתְרֵיהּ:


Lavan rose early in the morning. He kissed his sons and daughters and blessed them. Lavan departed and returned to his place.


###### Onkelos Genesis 32:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:2)


וְיַעֲקֹב אָזַל לְאָרְחֵיהּ וַעֲרָעוּ בֵיהּ מַלְאָכַיָּא דַיְיָ:


Yaakov went on his way, and the angels of Elohim met him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 32:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:3)


וַאֲמַר יַעֲקֹב כַּד חֲזָנוּן **מַשְׁרִיתָא מִן קֳדָם יְיָ דֵּין** וּקְרָא שְׁמָא דְאַתְרָא הַהוּא מַחֲנָיִם:


When Yaakov saw them, he said, This is Elohim’s camp. [**This is a camp from before Elohim.**] He named the place Machanayim.


###### Onkelos Genesis 32:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:4)


וּשְׁלַח יַעֲקֹב אִזְגַּדִּין קֳדָמוֹהִי לְוַת עֵשָׂו אֲחוּהִי לְאַרְעָא דְשֵׂעִיר לַחֲקַל אֱדוֹם:


Yaakov sent messengers before him to Eisov, his brother, to the Land of Seir, to the field of Edom.


###### Onkelos Genesis 32:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:5)


וּפַקִּיד יָתְהוֹן לְמֵימַר כְּדֵין תֵּימְרוּן לְרִבּוֹנִי לְעֵשָׂו כִּדְנַן אֲמַר עַבְדָּךְ יַעֲקֹב עִם לָבָן דָּרִית וְאוֹחָרִית עַד כְּעָן:


He commanded them saying, This is what you should say to my master, Eisov. Your servant, Yaakov says, I lived as a stranger with Lavan, and was delayed until now.


###### Onkelos Genesis 32:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:6)


וַהֲוָה לִי תּוֹרִין וַחֲמָרִין עָאן וְעַבְדִּין וְאַמְהָן וּשְׁלָחִית לְחַוָּאָה לְרִבּוֹנִי לְאַשְׁכָּחָא רַחֲמִין בְּעֵינָךְ:


I acquired oxen, donkeys, sheep, servants and maidservants. I have sent [these messengers] to tell my master, to find favor in your eyes.


###### Onkelos Genesis 32:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:7)


וְתָבוּ אִזְגַּדַּיָּא לְוַת יַעֲקֹב לְמֵימָר אֲתֵינָא לְוַת אָחוּךְ לְוַת עֵשָׂו וְאַף אָתֵי לְקַדָּמוּתָךְ וְאַרְבַּע מְאָה גַבְרָא עִמֵּיהּ:


The messengers returned to Yaakov saying, We came to your brother, to Eisov, and he is also coming to meet you; and there are four hundred men with him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 32:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:8)


וּדְחִיל יַעֲקֹב לַחֲדָא וַעֲקַת לֵיהּ וּפַלֵּיג יָת עַמָּא דִי עִמֵּיהּ וְיָת עָנָא וְיָת תּוֹרֵי וְגַמְלַיָּא לִתְרֵין מַשִּׁירְיָן:


Yaakov was very frightened, and distressed. He divided the people that were with him, along with the sheep, cattle and camels, into two camps.


###### Onkelos Genesis 32:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:9)


וַאֲמַר אִם יֵיתֵי עֵשָׂו לְמַשְׁרִיתָא חֲדָא וְיִמְחִנֵּיהּ וִיהֵי (נ"א וְיִמְחֵנַהּ וּתְהִי) מַשְׁרִיתָא דְתִשְׁתָּאַר לְשֵׁיזָבָא:


He said, If Eisov comes to one camp and attacks it, the remaining camp will survive.


###### Onkelos Genesis 32:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/32:10)


וַאֲמַר יַעֲקֹב אֱלָהֵהּ דְּאַבָּא אַבְרָהָם וֵאלָהֵהּ דְּאַבָּא יִצְחָק יְיָ דִּי אֲמַר לִי תּוּב לְאַרְעָךְ וּלְיַלָּדוּתָךְ וְאוֹטֵיב עִמָּךְ:


Yaakov said, God of my father Avraham, and God of my father Yitzchok, Adonoy Who said to me, Return to your land, to your birthplace, and I will do good with you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 32:11

## Chapter 33

###### Onkelos Genesis 33:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/33:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/33:1)

Yaakov raised his eyes and saw that Eisov was coming, and with him there were four hundred men. He then divided the children among Leah, Rochel and the two handmaids [**concubines**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 33:2

He placed the handmaids [**concubines**] and their children foremost, Leah and her sons behind them, and Rochel and Yoseif in the rear.


###### Onkelos Genesis 33:3

He went ahead of them, and he prostrated himself to the earth seven times, until he approached his brother.

Eisov ran to meet him. He hugged him and fell on his neck and kissed him. They [both] wept.


###### Onkelos Genesis 33:5

וַאֲתָא חֲמוֹר וּשְׁכֶם בְּרֵיהּ לִתְרַע קַרְתְּהוֹן וּמַלִּילוּ עִם אֲנָשֵׁי קַרְתְּהוֹן לְמֵימָר:


Chamor and his son, Shechem came to the gate of their city, and they spoke to the men of their city saying,


###### Onkelos Genesis 34:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/34:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/34:21)


גֻּבְרַיָּא הָאִלֵּין שָׁלְמִין אִנּוּן עִמָּנָא וִיתִיבוּן בְּאַרְעָא **וְיַעְבְּדוּן** בַּהּ סְחוֹרְתָא וְאַרְעָא הָא פְתָיוּת יְדִין קֳדָמֵיהוֹן יָת בְּנָתֵיהוֹן נִסַּב לָנָא לִנְשִׁין וְיָת בְּנָתָנָא נִתֵּן לְהוֹן:


These men are completely at peace with us. Let them live in the land and [**do**] trade in [**with**] it. The land has ample room to be open to them. We will take their daughters for wives, and we will give them our daughters.


###### Onkelos Genesis 34:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/34:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/34:22)


בְּרַם בְּדָא יִתָּפְסוּן לָנָא גוּבְרַיָּא לְמִתַּב עִמָּנָא לְמֵהֲוֵי לְעַמָּא חַד לְמִגְזַר לָנָא כָּל דְּכוּרָא כְּמָא דִּי אִנּוּן גְּזִירִין:


But only on these terms will the men consent to live with us, to become one people: every male among us must be circumcised, just as they are circumcised.


###### Onkelos Genesis 34:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/34:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/34:23)


גֵּיתֵיהוֹן וְקִנְיָנְהוֹן וְכָל בְּעִירְהוֹן הֲלָא דִּילָנָא אִנּוּן בְּרַם נִתְפֵּס לְהוֹן וִיתִיבוּן עִמָּנָא:


Their livestock, their possessions and all their cattle, will it not be ours? Only let us consent to them, and they will live with us.


###### Onkelos Genesis 34:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/34:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/34:24)

They listened to [**obeyed**] Chamor, and to his son, Shechem—all those who passed through the gate of his city. Every male was circumcised—all who passed through the gate of his city.


###### Onkelos Genesis 34:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/34:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/34:25)


וַהֲוָה בְיוֹמָא תְלִיתָאָה **כַּד תְּקִיפוּ עֲלֵיהוֹן כֵּאבֵיהוֹן** וּנְסִיבוּ תְרֵין בְּנֵי יַעֲקֹב שִׁמְעוֹן וְלֵוִי אֲחֵי דִינָה גְּבַר חַרְבֵּיהּ וְעַלּוּ עַל קַרְתָּא **דְּיָתְבוּן לְרָחֲצָן** וּקְטָלוּ כָּל דְּכוּרָא:


On the third day when they were in pain [**their pain was strong upon them**], two of Yaakov’s sons, Shimon and Leivi, brothers of Deenah, each took his sword. They approached the city with confidence [**whose inhabitants dwell in security**], and killed every male.

They killed Chamor and his son Shechem at the point of the sword. They took Deenah from the house of Shechem and they left.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/34:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/34:27)


בְּנֵי יַעֲקֹב עַלוּ **לְחַלָּצָא קָטִילַיָּא** וּבְזוֹ קַרְתָּא דִּי סָאִיבוּ אֲחָתְהוֹן:


The sons of Yaakov came upon the corpses [**to strip the corpses**], and they plundered the city that had defiled their sister.

Their sheep, their cattle, their donkeys, whatever was in the city and whatever was in the field—they took [**plundered**].

All their wealth, all their children, and their wives—they took captive, and plundered—including everything in the houses.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/34:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/34:30)

Yaakov said to Shimon and Leivi, You have made trouble for me, making me obnoxious to [**putting hatred between us and**] the inhabitants of the land, the Canaanites and the Perizzites. [Since] I am few in number, they will gather together and attack me. I and my house [family] will be destroyed.

They said, Should he make our sister [**be treated**] into a harlot [**like one who goes outside**]?



## Chapter 35

###### Onkelos Genesis 35:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:1)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְיַעֲקֹב קוּם סַק לְבֵית אֵל וְתִיב תַּמָּן וְעִבֵד תַּמָּן מַדְבְּחָא לֵאלָהָא **דְּאִתְגְּלִי** לָךְ בְּמֵעִרְקָךְ מִן קֳדָם עֵשָׂו אָחוּךְ:


Elohim said to Yaakov, Arise, go up to Beis Eil and live there; make an altar there to the Almighty Who appeared [**became revealed**] to you when you were fleeing from your brother Eisov.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:2)


וַאֲמַר יַעֲקֹב לֶאֱנַשׁ בֵּיתֵיהּ וּלְכֹל דִּי עִמֵּיהּ אַעְדוּ יָת **טַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא** דִּי בֵינֵיכוֹן וְאִדַּכּוּ וְשַׁנּוּ כְּסוּתְכוֹן:


Yaakov [then] said to his household, and to everyone that was with him, Get rid of the foreign gods [**false gods of the nations**] in your midst. Purify yourselves and change your garments.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:3)


וְנֵקוֹם וְנִסַּק לְבֵית אֵל וְאֶעְבֵּד תַּמָּן מַדְבְּחָא לֵאלָהָא **דְּקַבֵּל צְלוֹתִי** בְּיוֹמָא דְעַקְתִי וַהֲוָה **מֵימְרֵיהּ בְּסַעְדִּי** בְּאָרְחָא דִּי אֲזָלִית:


Let us arise and go up to Beis Eil. There I will make an altar to the Almighty, Who answered me [**accepted my prayer**] in the day of my distress, and Who was with me [**whose Word was my support**] along the way I traveled.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:4)


וִיהָבוּ לְיַעֲקֹב יָת כָּל **טַעֲוַת עַמְמַיָּא** דִי בִידֵיהוֹן וְיָת קָדָשַׁיָּא דִּי בְאוּדְנֵיהוֹן וְטַמַּר יָתְהוֹן יַעֲקֹב תְּחוֹת בָּטְמָא דִּי עִם שְׁכֶם:


They gave Yaakov all the foreign gods [**false gods of the nations**] that were in their hands, and [also] the rings in their ears. Yaakov buried them under the oak [tree] which was near Shechem.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:5)


וּנְטָלוּ וַהֲוַת דַּחֲלָא דַיְיָ עַל **עַמְמַיָּא דִּי בְּ**קִרְוֵי סַחֲרָנֵיהוֹן וְלָא רְדָפוּ בָּתַר בְּנֵי יַעֲקֹב:


They began their journey. The terror of Elohim was upon the [**people who were in the**] cities that were around them, and they did not pursue the sons of Yaakov.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:6)


וַאֲתָא יַעֲקֹב לְלוּז דִּי בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנַעַן הִיא בֵּית אֵל הוּא וְכָל עַמָּא דִּי עִמֵּיהּ:


Yaakov came to Luz, which was in the land of Canaan—that is, to Beis Eil—he and all the people that were with him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:7)


וּבְנָא תַמָּן מַדְבְּחָא וּקְרָא לְאַתְרָא אֵל בֵּית אֵל אֲרֵי תַמָּן אִתְגְּלִי לֵיהּ יְיָ בְּמֵעִרְקֵיהּ מִן קֳדָם אָחוּהִי:


There he built an altar, and he called the place Eil Beis Eil, for there Elohim was revealed to him when he was fleeing from his brother.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:8)


וּמִיתַת דְּבוֹרָה מֵנִקְתָּא דְרִבְקָה וְאִתְקְבָרַת מִלְּרַע לְבֵית אֵל **בְּשִׁפּוֹלֵי מֵישְׁרָא** וּקְרָא שְׁמֵיהּ **מֵישַׁר בָּכִיתָא**:


Devorah, Rivkah’s nurse, died, and she was buried below Beis Eil, under Allon [**on the lower plain**]. He named it Allon of Weeping [**Plain of the Weeping**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:9)


**וְאִתְגְּלִי** יְיָ לְיַעֲקֹב עוֹד בְּמֵיתוֹהִי מִפַּדַּן אֲרָם וּבָרִיךְ יָתֵיהּ:


Elohim again appeared [**became revealed**] to Yaakov when he came from Padan Aram, and He blessed him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:10)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ יְיָ שְׁמָךְ יַעֲקֹב לָא יִתְקְרֵי שְׁמָךְ עוֹד יַעֲקֹב אֶלָּהֵין יִשְׂרָאֵל יְהֵא שְׁמָךְ וּקְרָא יָת שְׁמֵיהּ יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Elohim said to him. Your name is Yaakov. No longer will your name be Yaakov, but Yisrael will be your name; and He named him Yisrael.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:11)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ יְיָ אֲנָא אֵל שַׁדַּי פּוּשׁ וּסְגֵי עַם **וְכִנְשַׁת שִׁבְטִין** יְהֵא מִנָּךְ וּמַלְכִין **דְּשַׁלִּיטִין בְּעַמְמַיָּא** **מִנָּךְ יִפְּקוּן**:


Elohim said to him, I am Almighty Shaddai. Be fruitful and increase, a nation and a community of nations [**an assembly of tribes**] will come from you, and kings [**who rule over nations**] will come out of your loins [**descend from you**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:12)


וְיָת אַרְעָא דִּי יְהָבִית לְאַבְרָהָם וּלְיִצְחָק לָךְ אֶתְּנִנַהּ וְלִבְנָךְ בַּתְרָךְ אֶתֵּן יָת אַרְעָא:


The land that I gave to Avraham and Yitzchok, I will give to you; and to your offspring after you I will give the land.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:13)


וְאִסְתַּלַּק מֵעִלָּו‍ֹהִי **יְקָרָא** דַיְיָ בְּאַתְרָא דִּי מַלִּיל עִמֵּיהּ:


[**The Glory of**] Elohim ascended from him at the place where He had spoken to him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:14)


וַאֲקֵים יַעֲקֹב קַמְתָא בְּאַתְרָא דִּי מַלִּיל עִמֵּיהּ קָמַת אַבְנָא וְאַסִּיךְ עֲלַהּ **נִסּוּכִין** וַאֲרִיק עֲלַהּ מִשְׁחָא:


Yaakov set up a monument in the place where He had spoken to him, it was a monument of [a single] stone. He poured a libation [**libations**] on it, and he [also] poured oil on it.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:15)


וּקְרָא יַעֲקֹב יָת שְׁמָא דְאַתְרָא דִּי מַלִּיל עִמֵּיהּ תַּמָּן יְיָ בֵּית אֵל:


Yaakov named the place where Elohim had spoken to him; Beis Eil, [the House of the Almighty].


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:16)


וּנְטָלוּ מִבֵּית אֵל וַהֲוָה עוֹד כְּרוּבַת אַרְעָא לְמֵיעַל לְאֶפְרָת וִילִידַת רָחֵל וְקַשִּׁיאַת בְּמֵילְדַהּ:


They journeyed on from Beis Eil, and there was yet a stretch of land before [they would reach] Ephros when Rochel began to give birth. Her labor was very difficult.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:17)


וַהֲוָה בְּקַשְׁיוּתַהּ בְּמֵילְדַהּ וַאֲמֶרֶת לַהּ חָיְתָא לָא תִדְחֲלִי אֲרֵי אַף דֵּין לִיךְ בָּר:


When her labor was at its most difficult stage, the midwife said to her, Do not fear, for this one will also be a son for you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:18)


וַהֲוָה בְּמִפַּק נַפְשַׁהּ אֲרֵי מִיתַת (נ"א מַיְתָא) וּקְרַת שְׁמֵיהּ בַּר דְּוָי וַאֲבוּהִי קְרָא לֵיהּ בִּנְיָמִין:


As her soul was departing, for she died, she named him Ben Oni [son of my sorrow], but his father named him Binyomin.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:19)


וּמִיתַת רָחֵל וְאִתְקְבָרַת בְּאֹרַח אֶפְרָת הִיא בֵּית לָחֶם:


Rochel died and was buried on the way to Ephros, which is Beis Lechem [Bethlehem].


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:20)


וַאֲקֵים יַעֲקֹב קַמְתָא עַל קְבֻרְתַּהּ הִיא קָמַת קְבֻרְתָּא דְרָחֵל עַד יוֹמָא דֵין:


Yaakov set up a monument on her grave. This monument is on Rochel’s grave to this very day.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:21)


וּנְטַל יִשְׂרָאֵל **וּפְרַס** מַשְׁכְּנֵיהּ מִלְּהַלָּא לְמִגְדְּלָא דְעֵדֶר:


Yisrael traveled on, and set up [**spread**] his tent beyond Migdal Eider [Tower of the herds].


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:22)


וַהֲוָה כַּד שְׁרָא יִשְׂרָאֵל בְּאַרְעָא הַהִיא וַאֲזַל רְאוּבֵן וּשְׁכִיב עִם בִּלְהָה לְחֵינָתָא דַאֲבוּהִי וּשְׁמַע יִשְׂרָאֵל וַהֲווֹ בְנֵי יַעֲקֹב תְּרֵי עֲסַר:


When Yaakov was settled in that land. Reuvein went and lay near Bilhah, his father’s concubine, and Yisrael heard [about it]. The sons of Yaakov were twelve.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:23)


בְּנֵי לֵאָה בּוּכְרָא דְיַעֲקֹב רְאוּבֵן וְשִׁמְעוֹן וְלֵוִי וִיהוּדָה וְיִשָׂשכָר וּזְבֻלוּן:


The sons of Leah [were]: Reuvein, Yaakov’s firstborn, Shimon, Leivi, Yehudah, Yissachar and Zevulun.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:24)


בְּנֵי רָחֵל יוֹסֵף וּבִנְיָמִן:


The sons of Rochel [were]: Yoseif and Binyomin.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:25)


וּבְנֵי בִלְהָה אַמְתָא דְרָחֵל דָּן וְנַפְתָּלִי:


The sons of Bilhah, Rochel’s handmaid, [were]: Don and Naftali.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:26)


וּבְנֵי זִלְפָּה אַמְתָא דְלֵאָה גָּד וְאָשֵׁר אִלֵּין בְּנֵי יַעֲקֹב דִּי אִתְיְלִידַן לֵיהּ בְּפַדַּן אֲרָם:


The sons of Zilpah, Leah’s handmaid, [were]: Gad and Asher. These are the sons of Yaakov that were born to him in Padan Aram.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:27)


וַאֲתָא יַעֲקֹב לְוַת יִצְחָק אֲבוּהִי מַמְרֵא קִרְיַת אַרְבַּע הִיא חֶבְרוֹן דִּי דָר תַּמָּן אַבְרָהָם וְיִצְחָק:


Yaakov came to Yitzchok, his father, in Mamrei at Kiryas Arba, which is Chevron where Avraham and Yitzchok lived.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:28)


וַהֲווֹ יוֹמֵי יִצְחָק מְאָה וּתְמָנָן שְׁנִין:


The days of [the life of] Yitzchok were one hundred and eighty years.


###### Onkelos Genesis 35:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/35:29)


וְאִתְנְגִיד יִצְחָק וּמִית וְאִתְכְּנִישׁ לְעַמֵּיהּ סִיב וּשְׂבַע יוֹמִין וּקְבָרוּ יָתֵיהּ עֵשָׂו וְיַעֲקֹב בְּנוֹהִי:


Yitzchok expired and died. He was gathered to his people old and in the fullness of days. His sons, Eisov and Yaakov buried him.



## Chapter 36

###### Onkelos Genesis 36:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:1)


וְאִלֵּין תּוּלְדַת עֵשָׂו הוּא אֱדוֹם:


These are the descendants of Eisov, also known as Edom.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:2)


עֵשָׂו נְסִיב יָת נְשׁוֹהִי מִבְּנַת כְּנָעַן יָת עָדָה בַּת אֵילוֹן חִתָּאָה וְיָת אָהֳלִיבָמָה בַּת עֲנָה בַּת צִבְעוֹן חִוָּאָה:


Eisov took his wives from the daughters of Canaan: Adah, the daughter of Eylon, the Chittite, and Oholivomoh, the daughter of Anoh, daughter of Tzivon, the Chivite.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:3)


וְיָת בָּשְׂמַת בַּת יִשְׁמָעֵאל אֲחָתֵיהּ דִּנְבָיוֹת:


[He also took] Bosmas, the daughter of Yishmael, the sister of Nevayos.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:4)


וִילִידַת עָדָה לְעֵשָׂו יָת אֱלִיפָז וּבָשְׂמַת יְלִידַת יָת רְעוּאֵל:


Adah bore to Eisov, [his son] Eliphaz, and Bosmas bore Reueil.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:5)


וְאָהֳלִיבָמָה יְלִידַת יָת יְעוּשׁ וְיָת יַעְלָם וְיָת קֹרַח אִלֵּין בְּנֵי עֵשָׂו דִּי אִתְיְלִידוּ לֵיהּ בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן:


Oholivomoh bore Yeush, Yalom, and Korach. These are the sons of Eisov that were born to him in the land of Canaan.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:6)


וּדְבַר עֵשָׂו יָת נְשׁוֹהִי וְיָת בְּנוֹהִי וְיָת בְּנָתֵיהּ וְיָת כָּל נַפְשַׁת בֵּיתֵיהּ וְיָת גֵּיתוֹהִי וְיָת כָּל בְּעִירֵיהּ וְיָת כָּל קִנְיָנֵיהּ דִּי קְנָא בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן וַאֲזַל לְאַרְעָא **אָחֲרֵי** מִן קֳדָם יַעֲקֹב אָחוּהִי:


Eisov took his wives, his sons, his daughters, all the people of his household, his livestock, all his animals, and all his possessions that he had acquired in the land of Canaan, and he went to a [**different**] land away from his brother, Yaakov.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:7)


אֲרֵי הֲוָה קִנְיָנְהוֹן סַגִּי מִלְּמִתַּב כַּחֲדָא וְלָא יְכִילַת אֲרַע תּוֹתָבוּתְהוֹן לְסוֹבָרָא יָתְהוֹן מִן קֳדָם גֵּיתֵיהוֹן:


For their wealth was too great for them to live together. The land in which they lived temporarily could not support them because of their livestock.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:8)


וִיתֵיב עֵשָׂו בְּטוּרָא דְשֵׂעִיר עֵשָׂו הוּא אֱדוֹם:


Eisov [therefore] settled in Mount Seir. Eisov is Edom.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:9)


וְאִלֵּין תּוּלְדַת עֵשָׂו אֲבוּהוֹן **דֶאֱדוֹמָאֵי** בְּטוּרָא דְּשֵׂעִיר:


These are the descendants of Eisov, the ancestor of Edom [**the Edomites**] in Mount Seir.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:10)


אִלֵּין שְׁמָהַת בְּנֵי עֵשָׂו אֱלִיפַז בַּר עָדָה אִתַּת עֵשָׂו רְעוּאֵל בַּר בָּשְׂמַת אִתַּת עֵשָׂו:


These are the names of Eisov’s sons: Eliphaz, the son of Adah, the wife of Eisov, Reueil, the son of Bosmas, the wife of Eisov.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:11)


וַהֲווֹ בְּנֵי אֱלִיפָז תֵּימָן אוֹמָר צְפוֹ וְגַעְתָּם וּקְנַז:


The sons of Eliphaz were Teiman, Omar, Tzepho, Gatam and Kenaz.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:12)


וְתִמְנַע הֲוַת לְחֵינָתָא לֶאֱלִיפַז בַּר עֵשָׂו וִילִידַת לֶאֱלִיפַז יָת עֲמָלֵק אִלֵּין בְּנֵי עָדָה אִתַּת עֵשָׂו:


Timna became a concubine to Eliphaz, Eisov’s son, and she bore to Eliphaz [a son], Amaleik. These are the sons of Adah, the wife of Eisov.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:13)


וְאִלֵּין בְּנֵי רְעוּאֵל נַחַת וָזֶרַח שַׁמָּה וּמִזָּה אִלֵּין הֲווֹ בְּנֵי בָשְׂמַת אִתַּת עֵשָׂו:


These are the sons of Reueil, Nachas, Zerach, Shamoh, and Mizzoh. These were the sons of Bosmas, the wife of Eisov.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:14)


וְאִלֵּין הֲווֹ בְּנֵי אָהֳלִיבָמָה בַת עֲנָה בַּת צִבְעוֹן אִתַּת עֵשָׂו וִילִידַת לְעֵשָׂו יָת יְעוּשׁ וְיָת יַעְלָם וְיָת קֹרַח:


These are the sons of Oholivomoh, the daughter of Anoh, the daughter of Tzivon, the wife of Eisov; she bore to Eisov: Ye’ush, Yalom and Korach.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:15)


אִלֵּין רַבְרְבֵי בְּנֵי עֵשָׂו בְּנֵי אֱלִיפַז בּוּכְרָא דְעֵשָׂו רַבָּא תֵימָן רַבָּא אוֹמָר רַבָּא צְפוֹ רַבָּא קְנַז:


These are the chiefs of the sons of Eisov: the sons of Eliphaz, Eisov’s first born: Chief Teiman, Chief Omar, Chief Tzepho, Chief Kenaz.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:16)


רַבָּא קֹרַח רַבָּא גַעְתָּם רַבָּא עֲמָלֵק אִלֵּין רַבְרְבֵי דֶאֱלִיפַז בְּאַרְעָא דֶאֱדוֹם אִלֵּין בְּנֵי עָדָה:


Chief Korach, Chief Gatam, Chief Amaleik. These are the chiefs of Eliphaz in the land of Edom. These are the sons of Adah.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:17)


וְאִלֵּין בְּנֵי רְעוּאֵל בַּר עֵשָׂו רַבָּא נַחַת רַבָּא זֶרַח רַבָּא שַׁמָּה רַבָּא מִזָּה אִלֵּין רַבְרְבֵי רְעוּאֵל בְּאַרְעָא (דֶ)אֱדוֹם אִלֵּין בְּנֵי בָשְׂמַת אִתַּת עֵשָׂו:


These are the sons of Reu’eil, Eisov’s son: Chief Nachas, Chief Zerach. Chief Shamoh, Chief Mizzoh. These are the Chiefs of Reu’eil in the land of Edom. These are sons of Bosmas, wife of Eisov.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:18)


וְאִלֵּין בְּנֵי אָהֳלִיבָמָה אִתַּת עֵשָׂו רַבָּא יְעוּשׁ רַבָּא יַעְלָם רַבָּא קֹרַח אִלֵּין רַבְרְבֵי אָהֳלִיבָמָה בַּת עֲנָה אִתַּת עֵשָׂו:


These are the sons of Oholivomoh, wife of Eisov: Chief Ye’ush, Chief Yalom, Chief Korach. These are the Chiefs of Oholivomoh, daughter of Anoh, wife of Eisov.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:19)


אִלֵּין בְּנֵי עֵשָׂו וְאִלֵּין רַבְרְבָנֵיהוֹן הוּא אֱדוֹם:


These are the sons of Eisov, and these are their chiefs, he is Edom.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:20)


אִלֵּין בְּנֵי שֵׂעִיר חוֹרָאָה יָתְבֵי דְּאַרְעָא לוֹטָן וְשׁוֹבָל וְצִבְעוֹן וַעֲנָה:


These are the sons of Se’ir, the Chorite, the inhabitants of the land: Lotan, Shoval, Tzivon, and Anoh.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:21)


וְדִשׁוֹן וְאֵצֶר וְדִישָׁן אִלֵּין רַבְרְבֵי חוֹרָאֵי בְּנֵי שֵׂעִיר בְּאַרְעָא דֶאֱדוֹם:


Dishon, Eitzer and Dishan, these are the chiefs of the Chorites, the sons of Se’ir, in the land of Edom.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:22)


וַהֲווֹ בְנֵי לוֹטָן חוֹרִי וְהֵימָם וַאֲחָתֵיהּ דְּלוֹטָן תִּמְנָע:


The sons of Lotan were Chori and Heimam. Lotan’s sister was Timna.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:23)


וְאִלֵּין בְּנֵי שׁוֹבָל עַלְוָן וּמָנַחַת וְעֵיבָל שְׁפוֹ וְאוֹנָם:


These are the sons of Shoval: Alvan, Manachas, Eival, Shefo and Onom.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:24)


וְאִלֵּין בְּנֵי צִבְעוֹן וְאַיָּה וַעֲנָה הוּא עֲנָה דִּי אַשְׁכַּח יָת **גִּבָּרַיָּא** בְּמַדְבְּרָא כַּד הֲוָה רָעֵי יָת חֲמָרַיָּא לְצִבְעוֹן אֲבוּהִי:


These are the sons of Tzivon: Ayoh and Anoh. He is the same Anoh who found the mules [**mighty ones**] in the desert while tending the donkeys for his father, Tzivon.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:25)


וְאִלֵּין בְּנֵי עֲנָה דִּשֹׁן וְאָהֳלִיבָמָה בַּת עֲנָה:


These are the children of Anoh: Dishon, and Oholivomoh the daughter of Anoh.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:26)


וְאִלֵּין בְּנֵי דִישָׁן חֶמְדָּן וְאֶשְׁבָּן וְיִתְרָן וּכְרָן:


These are the sons of Dishon: Chemdan, Eshban, Yisran and Keran.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:27)


אִלֵּין בְּנֵי אֵצֶר בִּלְהָן וְזַעֲוָן וַעֲקָן:


These are the sons of Eitzer: Bilhan, Za’avan and Akan.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:28

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:28](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:28)


אִלֵּין בְּנֵי דִישָׁן עוּץ וַאֲרָן:


These are the sons of Dishon: Utz and Aran.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:29

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:29](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:29)


אִלֵּין רַבְרְבֵי חוֹרָאָה רַבָּא לוֹטָן רַבָּא שׁוֹבָל רַבָּא צִבְעוֹן רַבָּא עֲנָה:


These are the chiefs of the Chorites: Chief Lotan, Chief Shoval, Chief Tzivon, Chief Anoh.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:30

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:30)


רַבָּא דִשֹׁן רַבָּא אֵצֶר רַבָּא דִישָׁן אִלֵּין רַבְרְבֵי חוֹרָאָה לְרַבְרְבָנֵיהוֹן בְּאַרְעָא דְשֵׂעִיר:


Chief Dishon, Chief Eitzer, Chief Dishan. These are the chiefs of the Chorites based on the ranks of chiefs in the Land of Se’ir.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:31)


וְאִלֵּין מַלְכַיָּא דִּי מְלִיכוּ בְּאַרְעָא דֶאֱדוֹם קֳדָם דִּי מְלַךְ מַלְכָּא לִבְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל:


These are the kings who reigned in the Land of Edom before any king reigned over the Children of Israel.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:32)


וּמְלַךְ בֶּאֱדוֹם בֶּלַע בַּר בְּעוֹר וְשׁוּם קַרְתֵּיהּ דִּנְהָבָה:


Bela, son of Be’or reigned in Edom, and the name of his city was Dinhavah.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:33)


וּמִית בָּלַע וּמְלַךְ תְּחוֹתוֹהִי יוֹבָב בַּר זֶרַח מִבָּצְרָה:


Bela died, and he was succeeded as king by Yovav, son of Zerach, from Botzroh.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:34

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:34](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:34)


וּמִית יוֹבָב וּמְלַךְ תְּחוֹתוֹהִי חֻשָׁם מֵאַרְעָא **דָרוֹמָא**:


Yovav died, and he was succeeded as king by Chusham from the land of the Teimanites [**South**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:35

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:35](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:35)


וּמִית חֻשָׁם וּמְלַךְ תְּחוֹתוֹהִי הֲדַד בַּר בְּדַד **דִּקְטִיל** **יָת מִדְיָנָאֵי** בְּחַקְלָא דְמוֹאָב וְשׁוּם קַרְתֵּיהּ עֲוִית:


Chusham died, and he was succeeded as king by Hadad, son of Bedad, who attacked [**killed**] Midian [**the Midianites**] in the field of Moav, and the name of his city was Avis.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:36

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:36](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:36)


וּמִית הֲדָד וּמְלַךְ תְּחוֹתוֹהִי שַׂמְלָה מִמַּשְׂרֵקָה:


Hadad died, and he was succeeded as king by Samlah of Masriekah.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:37

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:37](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:37)


וּמִית שַׂמְלָה וּמְלַךְ תְּחוֹתוֹהִי שָׁאוּל מֵרְחוֹבֵי דְעַל **פְּרָת**:


Samlah died, and he was succeeded as king by Sha’ul from Rechovos-by-the-river [**P’ras**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:38

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:38](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:38)


וּמִית שָׁאוּל וּמְלַךְ תְּחוֹתוֹהִי בַּעַל חָנָן בַּר עַכְבּוֹר:


Sha’ul died, and he was succeeded as king by Ba’al-Chanan, son of Achbor.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:39

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:39](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:39)


וּמִית בַּעַל חָנָן בַּר עַכְבּוֹר וּמְלַךְ תְּחוֹתוֹהִי הֲדַר וְשׁוּם קַרְתֵּיהּ פָּעוּ וְשׁוּם אִתְּתֵיהּ מְהֵיטַבְאֵל בַּת מַטְרֵד בַּת **מְצָרֵף דַּהֲבָא**:


Ba’al-Chanan, son of Achbor died, and he was succeeded as king by Hadar. The name of his city was Pa’u. His wife’s name was Meheitaveil, the daughter of Matreid, daughter of Mei-Zahav [**a goldsmith**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:40

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:40](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:40)


וְאִלֵּין שְׁמָהַת רַבְרְבֵי עֵשָׂו לְזַרְעֲיָתְהוֹן לְאַתְרֵיהוֹן בִּשְׁמָהָתְהוֹן רַבָּא תִמְנָע רַבָּא עַלְוָה רַבָּא יְתֵת:


These are the names of the chiefs of Eisov, each with their families, according to their places, by their names: Chief Timna, Chief Alvoh, Chief Yeseis.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:41

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:41](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:41)


רַבָּא אָהֳלִיבָמָה רַבָּא אֵלָה רַבָּא פִינֹן:


Chief Oholivomoh, Chief Eilah, Chief Pinon.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:42

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:42](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:42)


רַבָּא קְנַז רַבָּא תֵימָן רַבָּא מִבְצָר:


Chief Kenaz, Chief Teimon, Chief Mivtzar.


###### Onkelos Genesis 36:43

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:43](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/36:43)


רַבָּא מַגְדִּיאֵל רַבָּא עִירָם אִלֵּין רַבְרְבֵי אֱדוֹם לְמוֹתְבָנְהוֹן בְּאַרְעָא אַחֲסַנְתְּהוֹן הוּא עֵשָׂו אֲבוּהוֹן דֶּאֱדוֹמָאֵי:


Chief Magdiel, Chief Iram. These are the chiefs of Edom according to their places of residence, in the land of their possession. Thus was Eisov the ancestor of the Edomites.



## Chapter 37

###### Onkelos Genesis 37:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:1)


וִיתֵיב יַעֲקֹב בְּאַרְעָא תּוֹתָבוּת אֲבוּהִי בְּאַרְעָא דִּכְנָעַן:


Yaakov settled in the land of his father’s residence, in the land of Canaan.


###### Onkelos Genesis 37:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:2)


אִלֵּין תּוּלְדַת יַעֲקֹב יוֹסֵף בַּר שְׁבַע עַסְרֵי שְׁנִין (כַּד) הֲוָה רָעֵי עִם אֲחוֹהִי בַּעֲנָא **וְהוּא מְרַבֵּי** עִם בְּנֵי בִלְהָה וְעִם בְּנֵי זִלְפָּה נְשֵׁי אֲבוּהִי וְאַיְתִי יוֹסֵף יָת דִּבְּהוֹן בִּישָׁא לְוַת אֲבוּהוֹן:


This is the history of Yaakov; Yoseif at the age of seventeen years, would tend the sheep with his brothers, and the lad was [**he had been raised**] with the sons of Bilhah, and the sons of Zilpah, his father’s wives. Yoseif brought back bad reports about them to their father.


###### Onkelos Genesis 37:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:3)


וְיִשְׂרָאֵל רְחֵם יָת יוֹסֵף מִכָּל בְּנוֹהִי אֲרֵי בַר **חַכִּים** הוּא לֵיהּ וַעֲבַד לֵיהּ כִּתּוּנָא דְפַסֵּי:


Yisrael loved Yoseif more than any of his sons, for he was a [**wise**] son of his old age to him, and he made him a long, colorful cloak.


###### Onkelos Genesis 37:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:4)


וַחֲזוֹ אֲחוֹהִי אֲרֵי יָתֵיהּ רְחֵם אֲבוּהוֹן מִכָּל אֲחוֹהִי וּסְנוֹ יָתֵיהּ וְלָא **צָבָן** לְמַלָּלָא עִמֵּיהּ לִשְׁלָם:


His brothers saw that their father loved him more than all his brothers, and they hated him. They could not [**were not willing to**] speak to him peaceably.


###### Onkelos Genesis 37:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:5)


וַחֲלַם יוֹסֵף חֶלְמָא וְחַוִּי לַאֲחוֹהִי וְאוֹסִיפוּ עוֹד סְנוֹ יָתֵיהּ:


Yoseif had a dream and he told his brothers, and they hated him even more.


###### Onkelos Genesis 37:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:6)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן שְׁמָעוּ **כְעַן** חֶלְמָא הָדֵין דִּי חֲלֵמִית:


He said to them, Listen [**now**] to this dream that I dreamt.


###### Onkelos Genesis 37:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:7)


וְהָא אֲנַחְנָא מְאַסְּרִין אֱסָרָן בְּגוֹ חַקְלָא וְהָא קָמַת אֱסַרְתִּי וְאַף אִזְדְּקָפַת וְהָא מִסְתַּחֲרָן אֱסָרָתְכוֹן וְסָגְדָן לֶאֱסַרְתִּי:


Behold, we were binding sheaves in the middle of the field. Behold my sheaf rose and stood up straight; and behold your sheaves surrounded it and prostrated themselves to my sheaf.


###### Onkelos Genesis 37:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:8)


וַאֲמָרוּ לֵיהּ אֲחוֹהִי הֲמַלְכוּ **אַתְּ מְדַמֵּי לְמִמְלַךְ עָלָנָא אוֹ שׁוּלְטָן אַתְּ סְבִיר לְמִשְׁלַט בָּנָא** וְאוֹסִיפוּ עוֹד סְנוֹ יָתֵיהּ עַל חֶלְמוֹהִי וְעַל פִּתְגָּמוֹהִי:


His brothers said to him, Will you then be a king over us? [**Do you imagine that you will reign over us?**] Will you indeed rule over us? [**Or do you think that you will rule over us?**] They hated him even more because of his dreams and his words.


###### Onkelos Genesis 37:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:9)


וַחֲלַם עוֹד חֶלְמָא אָחֲרָנָא וְאִשְׁתָּעֵי יָתֵיהּ לַאֲחוֹהִי וַאֲמַר הָא חֲלֵמִית חֶלְמָא עוֹד וְהָא שִׁמְשָׁא וְסִהֲרָא וְחַד עֲשַׂר כּוֹכְבַיָּא סָגְדָן לִי:


He had another dream and told it to his brothers. He said, Behold! I dreamed another dream. The sun, the moon, and eleven stars were prostrating themselves to me.


###### Onkelos Genesis 37:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:10)


וְאִשְׁתָּעֵי לַאֲבוּהִי וְלַאֲחוֹהִי וּנְזַף בֵּיהּ אֲבוּהִי וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ מָה חֶלְמָא הָדֵין דִּי חֲלֵמְתָּא הֲמֵיתָא נֵיתֵי אֲנָא וְאִמָּךְ וְאַחָיךְ לְמִסְגֻּד לָךְ עַל אַרְעָא:


He told it to his father and to his brothers. His father rebuked him, and said to him, What is this dream that you dreamed? Shall I, your mother and your brothers come and prostrate themselves on the ground to you?


###### Onkelos Genesis 37:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:11)


וְקַנִּיאוּ בֵיהּ אֲחוֹהִי וַאֲבוּהִי נְטַר יָת פִּתְגָּמָא:


His brothers were jealous of him but his father kept the matter in mind.


###### Onkelos Genesis 37:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:12)


וַאֲזָלוּ אֲחוֹהִי לְמִרְעֵי יָת עָנָא דַאֲבוּהוֹן בִּשְׁכֶם:


His brothers went off to pasture their father’s sheep in Shechem.


###### Onkelos Genesis 37:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/37:13)


וַאֲמַר יִשְׂרָאֵל לְיוֹסֵף הֲלָא אַחָיךְ רָעָן בִּשְׁכֶם אֱתָא וְאֶשְׁלְחִנָּךְ לְוָתְהוֹן וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ הָא אֲנָא:


Yisrael said to Yoseif, Aren’t your brothers pasturing [the sheep] in Shechem? Come, I will send you to them. He [Yoseif] said to him, Here I am.


###### Onkelos Genesis 37:14

###### Onkelos Genesis 38:1

## Chapter 39

###### Onkelos Genesis 39:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:1)


וְיוֹסֵף אִתָּחַת לְמִצְרָיִם וּזְבָנֵיהּ פּוֹטִיפַר רַבָּא דְפַרְעֹה רַב קָטוֹלַיָּא גְּבַר מִצְרָאָה מִידָא **דַּעֲרָבָאֵי** דִּי אֲחִתוֹהִי לְתַמָּן:


Yoseif was brought down to Egypt. He was purchased by Potiphar—an officer of Pharaoh, the chief of the slaughterers, an Egyptian—from the Yishmaelites [**Arabs**] who had brought him down there.


###### Onkelos Genesis 39:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:2)


וַהֲוָה **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ **בְּסַעְדֵּיהּ** דְּיוֹסֵף וַהֲוָה גְּבַר מַצְלָח וַהֲוָה בְּבֵית רִבּוֹנֵיהּ מִצְרָאָה:


[**The Word of**] Adonoy was with Yoseif[**’s support**], and he was successful. He was [a servant] in the house of his master, the Egyptian.


###### Onkelos Genesis 39:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:3)


וַחֲזָא רִבּוֹנֵיהּ אֲרֵי **מֵימְרָא** דַיְיָ **בְּסַעְדֵּיהּ** וְכֹל דִּי הוּא עָבֵד יְיָ מַצְלַח בִּידֵיהּ:


His master saw that [**the Word of**] Adonoy was with him [**supported him**], and that whatever he did, Adonoy made it succeed through his hand.


###### Onkelos Genesis 39:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:4)


וְאַשְׁכַּח יוֹסֵף רַחֲמִין בְּעֵינוֹהִי וְשַׁמֵּשׁ יָתֵיהּ וּמַנְיֵהּ עַל בֵּיתֵיהּ וְכָל דִּי אִית לֵיהּ מְסַר בִּידֵיהּ:


Yoseif found favor in his eyes, and he served him personally. He appointed him supervisor over his household, and all that he possessed he placed in his hand.


###### Onkelos Genesis 39:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:5)


וַהֲוָה מֵעִדַּן דְּמַנֵּי יָתֵיהּ בְּבֵיתֵיהּ וְעַל כָּל דִּי אִית לֵיהּ וּבָרִיךְ יְיָ יָת בֵּית מִצְרָאָה בְּדִיל יוֹסֵף וַהֲוָה בִּרְכְּתָא דַיְיָ בְּכָל דִּי אִית לֵיהּ בְּבֵיתָא וּבְחַקְלָא:


From the time he appointed him supervisor over his household and over all that he possessed, Adonoy blessed the house of the Egyptian for Yoseif’s sake. The blessing of Adonoy was in all that he possessed, in the house and in the field.


###### Onkelos Genesis 39:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:6)


וּשְׁבַק כָּל דִּי לֵיהּ בִּידָא דְיוֹסֵף וְלָא יְדַע עִמֵּיהּ מִדַּעַם אֶלָּהֵין לַחְמָא דִּי הוּא אָכֵל וַהֲוָה יוֹסֵף שַׁפִּיר בְּרֵיוָא וְיָאֵי בְחֶזְוָא:


He left everything he possessed in the hand of Yoseif. He did not concern himself with anything about him, except for the bread he ate. Yoseif was well-built and good looking.


###### Onkelos Genesis 39:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:7)


וַהֲוָה בָּתַר פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין וּזְקָפַת אִתַּת רִבּוֹנֵיהּ יָת עֵינָהָא לְיוֹסֵף וַאֲמֶרֶת שְׁכוּב עִמִּי:


After these events, his master’s wife cast her eyes upon Yoseif, and she said, Be with me.


###### Onkelos Genesis 39:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/39:8)


וְסָרֵיב וַאֲמַר לְוַת אִתַּת רִבּוֹנֵיהּ הָא רִבּוֹנִי לָא יְדַע עִמִּי מָה דִבְבֵיתָא וְכָל דִּי אִית לֵיהּ מְסַר בִּידִי:


He refused and said to his master’s wife, Behold, my master knows nothing about what I am doing in the house, and all that he possess, he has placed in my hands.

###### Onkelos Genesis 40:1

I was kidnaped from the land of the Hebrews, and here I have also done nothing that they should have put me in this dungeon [**prison**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 40:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/40:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/40:16)


וַחֲזָא רַב נַחְתּוֹמֵי אֲרֵי יָאוּת פָּשַׁר וַאֲמַר לְיוֹסֵף אַף אֲנָא בְּחֶלְמִי וְהָא תְּלָתָא סַלִּין דְּחִירוּ עַל רֵישִׁי:


The chief baker saw that he interpreted well. He said to Yoseif, I, too, dreamed. Behold there were three fancy baskets on my head.


###### Onkelos Genesis 40:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/40:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/40:17)


וּבְסַלָּא עִלָּאָה מִכֹּל מֵיכַל פַּרְעֹה עוֹבַד נַחְתּוֹם וְעוֹפָא אָכֵל יָתְהוֹן מִן סַלָּא מֵעִלָּוֵי רֵישִׁי:


In the top basket there were various kinds of food for Pharaoh, consisting of baked goods, and the bird was eating them from the basket that was on my head.


###### Onkelos Genesis 40:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/40:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/40:18)


וַאֲתֵב יוֹסֵף וַאֲמַר דֵּין פִּשְׁרָנֵיהּ תְּלָתָא סַלִּין תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין אִנּוּן:


Yoseif replied and said, This is its interpretation: The three baskets represent three days.


###### Onkelos Genesis 40:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/40:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/40:19)

In another [**At the end of**] three days, Pharaoh will lift [**remove**] your head from off you. He will hang you on a tree, and the bird will eat your flesh from off you.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/40:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/40:20)


וַהֲוָה בְּיוֹמָא תְלִיתָאָה יוֹמָא בֵּית וַלְדָא דְפַרְעֹה וַעֲבַד מִשְׁתְּיָא לְכָל עַבְדּוֹהִי **וְאִדְכַּר** יָת רֵישׁ רַב שָׁקֵי וְיָת רֵישׁ רַב נַחְתּוֹמֵי בְּגוֹ עַבְדּוֹהִי:


It was on the third day, which was Pharaoh’s birthday, that he [Pharaoh] made a feast for all his servants. He lifted [**remembered**] the head of the chief butler, and the head of the chief baker among his servants.

He restored the chief butler to his position, and he presented the cup into Pharaoh’s hand.

The chief baker, he hung, just as Yoseif had interpreted to them.

[However] the chief butler did not remember Yoseif, but forgot him.



## Chapter 41

###### Onkelos Genesis 41:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:1)


וַהֲוָה מִסּוֹף תַּרְתֵּין שְׁנִין וּפַרְעֹה חָלֵם וְהָא קָאֵם עַל נַהֲרָא:


It was at the end of two full years, that Pharaoh had a dream, and behold, he was standing on the bank of the river.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:2)


וְהָא מִן נַהֲרָא סָלְקָן שְׁבַע תּוֹרָן שַׁפִּירָן לְמֶחֱזֵי וּפַטִּימָן בְּשָׂר וְרָעְיָן בְּאַחֲוָה:


Behold, from the river emerged seven cows, fine-looking and well-fleshed, and they were grazing in the reed grass.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:3)


וְהָא שְׁבַע תּוֹרָן אָחֳרָנְיָן סָלְקָן בַּתְרֵיהוֹן מִן נַהֲרָא בִּישָׁן לְמֶחֱזֵי וַחֲסִירַן בְּשָׂר וְקָמָן **לְקִבְלֵיהוֹן** דְּתוֹרָן עַל כֵּיף נַהֲרָא:


And behold, seven other cows emerged after them, from the river, bad-looking and thin-fleshed, and they stood next to [**opposite**] the cows that were [already] on the bank of the river.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:4)


וַאֲכָלַן תּוֹרְתָּא בִּישָׁן לְמֶחֱזֵי וַחֲסִירַן בְּשַׂר יָת שְׁבַע תּוֹרְתָּא שַׁפִּירָן לְמֶחֱזֵי וּפַטִּימָתָא וְאִתְּעַר פַּרְעֹה:


They ate—the bad looking, thin-fleshed cows [then ate] the seven fine looking, healthy cows, and Pharaoh woke up.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:5)


וּדְמוּךְ וַחֲלַם תִּנְיָנוּת וְהָא שְׁבַע שֻׁבְּלַיָּא סָלְקָן בְּקַנְיָא חַד פַּטִּימָן וְטָבָן:


He fell asleep and had a second dream. Behold, seven ears of grain [corn] came up on a single stalk, wholesome and good.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:6)


וְהָא שְׁבַע שֻׁבְּלַיָּא **לָקְיָן וּשְׁקִיפָן** קִדּוּם צָמְחָן בַּתְרֵיהוֹן:


And behold, seven ears, thin [**smitten**] and scorched [**beaten**] by the east wind, grew up after them.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:7)


וּבְלָעָא שֻׁבְּלַיָּא **לָקְיָתָא** יָת שְׁבַע שֻׁבְּלַיָּא פַּטִּימָתָא וּמָלְיָתָא וְאִתְּעַר פַּרְעֹה וְהָא חֶלְמָא:


The seven thin [**smitten**] ears swallowed the seven wholesome, full ears. Pharaoh woke up, and behold it was a dream.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:8)


וַהֲוָה בְצַפְרָא **וּמִטַּרְפָּא רוּחֵיהּ** וּשְׁלַח וּקְרָא יָת כָּל חָרָשֵׁי מִצְרַיִם וְיָת כָּל חַכִּימָהָא וְאִשְׁתָּעִי פַרְעֹה לְהוֹן יָת חֶלְמֵיהּ וְלֵית דְּפָשַׁר יָתְהוֹן לְפַרְעֹה:


In the morning, he was agitated [**his spirit was beaten**]. He sent and summoned all the wizards of Egypt and all its wise men. Pharaoh related his dream to them, but none could interpret them for Pharaoh.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:9)


וּמַלִּיל רַב שָׁקֵי (עִם) לְפַרְעֹה לְמֵימָר יָת סָרְחָנִי אֲנָא מַדְכַּר יוֹמָא דֵין:


The chief butler spoke to Pharaoh saying, I recall my sins today:


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:10)


פַּרְעֹה רְגֵז עַל עַבְדוֹהִי וִיהַב יָתִי בְּמַטְּרַת בֵּית רַב קָטוֹלַיָּא יָתִי וְיָת רַב נַחְתּוֹמֵי:


Pharaoh was enraged at his servants, and he placed me under guard in the house of the chief executioner; me and the chief baker.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:11)


וַחֲלֵמְנָא חֶלְמָא בְּלֵילְיָא חַד אֲנָא וָהוּא גְּבַר כְּפִשְׁרַן חֶלְמֵיהּ חֲלֵמְנָא:


We had a dream on the same night, I and he, each according to the interpretation of his dream, did we dream.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:12)


וְתַמָּן עִמָּנָא עוּלֵם עִבְרָאָה עַבְדָא לְרַב קָטוֹלַיָּא וְאִשְׁתָּעֵינָא לֵיהּ וּפָשַׁר לָנָא יָת חֶלְמָנָא גְּבַר כְּחֶלְמֵיהּ פָּשָׁר:


With us there was a lad, a Hebrew, a slave of the chief of the slaughterers. We told him [about our dreams], and he interpreted our dreams, he interpreted each man’s dream accordingly.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:13)


וַהֲוָה כְּמָא דִי פָּשַׁר לָנָא כֵּן הֲוָה יָתִי אֲתֵיב עַל שִׁמּוּשִׁי וְיָתֵיהּ צְלָב:


It came to pass, that as he interpreted for us, so did it occur; he restored me to my position, and him he hanged.

וּשְׁלַח פַּרְעֹה וּקְרָא יָת יוֹסֵף וְאַרְהִיטוֹהִי מִן בֵּית אֲסִירֵי **וְסַפַּר** וְשַׁנִּי כְסוּתֵיהּ וְעַל לְוַת פַּרְעֹה:


Pharaoh sent and summoned Yoseif. They hurried him out of the dungeon [**prison**], but [Yoseif first] shaved [**trimmed his hair**] and changed his clothes. And then came to Pharaoh.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:15)

Pharaoh said to Yoseif, I had a dream and there is no one to interpret it. I have heard it said about you that you hear a dream so as to interpret it.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:16)


וַאֲתֵיב יוֹסֵף יָת פַּרְעֹה לְמֵימַר **בַּר מִן חָכְמְתִי אֶלָּהֵן מִן קֳדָם** יְיָ **יִתּתַב** יָת שְׁלָמָא דְפַרְעֹה:


Yoseif answered Pharaoh, saying, Not I. [**from my wisdom but from before**] God will respond [**there be a response**] to the peace of Pharaoh.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:17)

Pharaoh spoke to Yoseif, In my dream, I was standing on the bank of the river.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:18)


וְהָא מִן נַהֲרָא סָלְקָן שְׁבַע תּוֹרְתָּא פַּטִּימָן בְּשַׂר וְשַׁפִּירָן לְמֶחֱזֵי וְרָעְיָן בְּאַחֲוָה:


Behold from the river there emerged seven cows, well-fleshed and fine looking, and they were grazing in the reed grass.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:19)


וְהָא שְׁבַע תּוֹרְתָּא אָחֳרָנְיָן סָלְקָן בַּתְרֵיהוֹן חֲסִיכָן וּבִישָׁן לְמֶחֱזֵי לַחֲדָא וַחֲסִירָן בְּשָׂר לָא חֲזֵתִי כְוָתְהֵן בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם לְבִישׁוּ:


And behold seven other cows emerged after them, poor, very bad-looking and thin-fleshed. I never saw in the entire land of Egypt, such bad looking [cows.]


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:20)

The thin, bad-looking cows, ate the first seven cows, which were the healthy ones.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:21)

###### Onkelos Genesis 41:24

וּשְׁרִיאַת שְׁבַע שְׁנֵי כַפְנָא לְמֵיעַל כְּמָא דִּי אֲמַר יוֹסֵף וַהֲוָה כַפְנָא בְּכָל אַרְעֲתָא וּבְכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם הֲוָה לַחְמָא:


The seven years of famine started to come, just as Yoseif had said. There was famine in all the lands, but in all the land of Egypt there was bread.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:55

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:55](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:55)


וּכְפָנַת כָּל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם וּצְוַח עַמָּא (קֳדָם) לְפַרְעֹה (עַל) לְלַחְמָא וַאֲמַר פַּרְעֹה לְכָל **מִצְרָאֵי** אִיזִילוּ לְוַת יוֹסֵף דִּי יֵימַר לְכוֹן תַּעְבְּדוּן:


When all the land of Egypt was famished, the people cried out to Pharaoh for bread. Pharaoh said to all of Egypt [**the Egyptians**], Go to Yoseif. Whatever he says to you, do.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:56

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:56](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:56)


וְכַפְנָא הֲוָה עַל כָּל אַפֵּי אַרְעָא וּפְתַח יוֹסֵף יָת כָּל (אוֹצְרַיָּא) דִּי בְהוֹן עִיבוּרָא וְזַבִּין לְ**מִצְרַיִם** וּתְקֵיף כַּפְנָא בְּאַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


The famine spread over the entire face of the land. Yoseif opened everything that held grain, and sold [grain] to the Egyptians [**Egypt**]. The famine became severe in the land of Egypt.


###### Onkelos Genesis 41:57

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:57](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/41:57)


וְכָל **דָּיְרֵי** אַרְעָא עַלּוּ לְמִצְרַיִם לְמִזְבַּן עִיבוּרָא מִן יוֹסֵף אֲרֵי תְקֵיף כַּפְנָא בְּכָל אַרְעָא:


All [countries] [**inhabitants**] of the land came to Egypt to buy [grain] from Yoseif, for the famine was severe in all the land.



## Chapter 42

###### Onkelos Genesis 42:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:1)


וַחֲזָא יַעֲקֹב אֲרֵי אִית **עִיבוּרָא** מִזְדַּבַּן בְּמִצְרָיִם וַאֲמַר יַעֲקֹב לִבְנוֹהִי לְמָא תִּתְחֲזוּן:


Yaakov saw that food [**grain**] was being sold in Egypt. Yaakov said to his sons, Why would you have everyone gazing at you?


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:2)


וַאֲמַר הָא שְׁמָעִית אָמְרִין אֲרֵי אִית **עִיבוּרָא** מִזְדַּבַּן בְּמִצְרָיִם חוּתוּ תַמָּן וּזְבוּנוּ לָנָא מִתַּמָּן וְנֵחֵי וְלָא נְמוּת:


He said, Behold, I have heard that there is food [**grain**] for sale in Egypt. Go down there and buy for us from there, so that we will live and not die.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:3)


וּנְחָתוּ אֲחֵי יוֹסֵף עַסְרָא לְמִזְבַּן עִיבוּרָא מִמִּצְרָיִם:


Yoseif’s ten brothers went down to buy grain from Egypt.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:4)


וְיָת בִּנְיָמִין אֲחוּהִי דְיוֹסֵף לָא שְׁלַח יַעֲקֹב עִם אֲחוּהִי אֲרֵי אֲמַר דִּלְמָא יְעַרְעִנֵּיהּ **מוֹתָא**:


But Binyamin, Yoseif’s brother, Yaakov did not send along with his brothers, for he [Yaakov] said, Misfortune [**Death**] might befall him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:5)


וַאֲתוֹ בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמִזְבַּן **עִיבוּרָא** בְּגוֹ עָלַיָּא אֲרֵי הֲוָה כַפְנָא בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן:


The sons of Yisrael came to buy [**grain**] among the others who came, for there was famine in the land of Canaan.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:6)


וְיוֹסֵף הוּא דְּשַׁלִּיט עַל אַרְעָא הוּא דְזַבִּין **עִיבוּרָא** לְכָל עַמָּא דְאַרְעָא וַאֲתוֹ אֲחֵי יוֹסֵף וּסְגִידוּ לֵיהּ עַל אַפַּיָּא עַל אַרְעָא:


Yoseif was the ruler over the land; he was the one who sold [**grain**] to all the people of the land. Yoseif’s brothers came and they prostrated themselves to him with their faces to the ground.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:7)


וַחֲזָא יוֹסֵף יָת אֲחוֹהִי וְאִשְׁתְּמוֹדְעִנּוּן **וְחַשִּׁיב מָה דְמַלֵּיל** לְהוֹן וּמַלִּיל עִמְּהוֹן קַשְׁיָא וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן מְנָן אֲתֵיתוּן וַאֲמָרוּ מֵאַרְעָא דִכְנַעַן לְמִזְבַּן **עִיבוּרָא**:


Yoseif saw his brothers and he recognized them, but he acted like a stranger [**considered what he should say**] to them. He spoke harshly to them, and said to them, Where did you come from? They said, From the land of Canaan to buy food [**grain**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:8)


וְאִשְׁתְּמוֹדַע יוֹסֵף יָת אֲחוֹהִי וְאִנּוּן לָא אִשְׁתְּמוֹדְעֵיהּ:


Yoseif recognized his brothers, but they did not recognize him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:9)


וּדְכִיר יוֹסֵף יָת חֶלְמַיָּא דִּי חֲלִים לְהוֹן וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן מְאַלְלִין אַתּוּן לְמֶחֱזֵי יָת **בִּדְקָא דְאַרְעָא** אֲתֵיתוּן:


Yoseif recalled the dreams that he had dreamt about them, and said to them, You are spies. You have come to see where the land is exposed [**the defective part of the land**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:10)


וַאֲמָרוּ לֵיהּ לָא רִבּוֹנִי וְעַבְדָיךְ עַלּוּ לְמִזְבַּן **עִיבוּרָא**:


They said to him, No my master. Your servants have come to buy food [**grain**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:11)


כֻּלָּנָא בְּנֵי גַבְרָא חַד נָחְנָא כֵּיוָנֵי אֲנַחְנָא לָא הֲווֹ עַבְדָיךְ אַלִּילֵי:


We are all the sons of one man. We are honest [men]. Your servants have never been spies.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:12)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן לָא אֲרֵי **בִדְקָא דְאַרְעָא** אֲתֵיתוּן לְמֶחֱזֵי:


He said to them, No, You have come to see where the land is exposed [**the defective part of the land**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:13)


וַאֲמָרוּ תְּרֵין עֲסַר עַבְדָיךְ אַחִין אֲנַחְנָא בְּנֵי גַבְרָא חַד בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן וְהָא זְעֵירָא עִם אָבוּנָא יוֹמָא דֵין וְחַד לֵיתוֹהִי:


They said, Your servants are twelve brothers, the sons of one man in the land of Canaan. Behold the youngest one is this day with our father, and one is no more.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:14)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן יוֹסֵף הוּא דִּי מַלֵּילִית עִמְּכוֹן לְמֵימַר מְאַלִּילֵי אַתּוּן:


Yoseif said to them, That is just what I said to you, saying: you are spies.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:15)


בְּדָא תִּתְבַּחֲנוּן חֵי פַרְעֹה אִם תִּפְּקוּן מִכָּא אֶלָּהֵין בְּמֵתֵי אֲחוּכוֹן זְעֵירָא הָכָא:


You shall be tested in this manner. By Pharaoh’s life, you shall not leave from here unless your youngest brother comes here.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:16)


שְׁלָחוּ מִנְּכוֹן חַד וִידַבֵּר יָת אֲחוּכוֹן וְאַתּוּן הִתְאַסְּרוּן וְיִתְבַּחֲנוּן פִּתְגָּמֵיכוֹן הַקְשׁוֹט **אַתּוּן אָמְרִין** וְאִם לָא חֵי פַרְעֹה אֲרֵי מְאַלִּילֵי אַתּוּן:


Send one of you and let him bring your brother. You will remain locked up and your words will be tested whether there is [**you speak**] any truth with you. If not, by Pharaoh’s life, you are spies.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:17)


וּכְנַשׁ יָתְהוֹן (לְבֵית) לְמַטְּרָא תְּלָתָא יוֹמִין:


He [then] put them together in prison for three days.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:18)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן יוֹסֵף בְּיוֹמָא תְלִיתָאָה דָּא עִיבִידוּ וְקַיִּימוּ **מִן קֳדָם יְיָ** אֲנָא דָחֵל:


Yoseif said to them on the third day, Do this and live. [**From before God**] I fear God.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:19)


אִם כֵּיוָנֵי אַתּוּן אֲחוּכוֹן חַד יִתְאַסַּר בְּבֵית מַטַּרְתְּכוֹן וְאַתּוּן אִיזִילוּ אוֹבִילוּ **עִיבוּרָא דְּחַסִּיר בְּ**בָתֵּיכוֹן:


If you are honest, one of your brothers will be imprisoned in your place of guarding, and you [the others] go bring food for your famished [**grain that is lacking in your**] households.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:20)


וְיָת אֲחוּכוֹן זְעֵירָא תַּיְתוּן לְוָתִי וִיהֵימְנוּן פִּתְגָּמֵיכוֹן וְלָא תְמוּתוּן וַעֲבָדוּ כֵן:


Bring your youngest brother to me, so that your words will be verified, and you will not die. They decided to do so.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:21)


וַאֲמָרוּ גְּבַר לַאֲחוּהִי בְּקוּשְׁטָא חַיָּבִין אֲנַחְנָא עַל אָחוּנָא דִּי חֲזֵינָא עָקַת נַפְשֵׁיהּ כַּד הֲוָה מִתְחַנֶּן לָנָא וְלָא **קַבֵּלְנָא** מִנֵּיהּ עַל כֵּן אֲתָא (נ"א אָתֵת) לְוָתָנָא עַקְתָא הָדָא:


They said to one another, In truth, we are guilty regarding our brother. We saw the anguish of his soul when he pleaded with us and we did not listen [**obey him**]. That is why this trouble has come upon us.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:22)


וַאֲתֵיב רְאוּבֵן יָתְהוֹן לְמֵימַר הֲלָא אֲמָרִית לְוָתְכוֹן לְמֵימַר לָא תְחוֹבוּן (נ"א תֶחְטָאוּן) בְּעוּלֵימָא וְלָא **קַבֶּלְתּוּן** וְאַף דְּמֵיהּ הָא מִתְבְּעֵי:


Reuvein answered them saying, Did I not say to you to the following: Do not sin against the lad, but you did not listen [**obey**]; and now his blood is being avenged.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:23)


וְאִנּוּן לָא יָדְעוּן אֲרֵי שְׁמִיעַ יוֹסֵף אֲרֵי מְתֻרְגְּמָן הֲוָה בֵינֵיהוֹן:


They did not know that Yoseif listened [understood], because the interpreter was between them.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:24)


וְאִסְתַּחַר מֵעִלָּוֵיהוֹן וּבְכָא וְתָב לְוָתְהוֹן וּמַלֵּיל עִמְּהוֹן וּדְבַר מִלְּוָתְהוֹן יָת שִׁמְעוֹן וַאֲסַר יָתֵיהּ לְעֵינֵיהוֹן:


He turned away from them and cried. He returned to them and spoke to them. He took Shimon from them, and had him bound before their eyes.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:25)


וּפַקִּיד יוֹסֵף וּמְלוֹ יָת מָנֵיהוֹן עִיבוּרָא וְלַאֲתָבָא כַסְפֵּיהוֹן גְּבַר לְסַקֵּיהּ וּלְמִתַּן לְהוֹן זְוָדִין לְאָרְחָא וַעֲבַד לְהוֹן כֵּן:


Yoseif gave orders—and their vessels were filled with grain, and each one’s money was replaced in his sack; and they were given provisions for the journey. This is what he did for them.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:26)


וּנְטָלוּ יָת עִיבוּרְהוֹן עַל חֲמָרֵיהוֹן וַאֲזָלוּ מִתַּמָּן:


They placed their purchases of grain on their donkeys and they departed from there.


###### Onkelos Genesis 42:27

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:27](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/42:27)


וּפְתַח חַד יָת סַקֵּיהּ לְמִתַּן כִּסְתָא לַחֲמָרֵיהּ בְּבֵית מְבָתָא וַחֲזָא יָת כַּסְפֵּיהּ וְהָא הוּא בְּפוּם טוֹעֲנֵיהּ:


The one opened his sack, to feed his donkey where they stayed overnight, and he saw his money, for behold it was in the opening of his bag.

He said to his brothers. My money has been returned, and behold it is also in my bag. [**The understanding of their**] Their hearts failed [**left**] them and they trembled, saying one to another, What is this that Elohim has done to us?

They came to their father Yaakov, to the land of Canaan, and they told him all that had happened to them, saying,

The man, the master of the land spoke to us harshly; and considered us as spies of the land.

We said to him, We are honest people, we have never been spies.

We are twelve brothers, sons of our [the same] father. One is no more, and the youngest is this day with our father in the land of Canaan.

The man, the master of the land, said to us, This is how I shall know that you are honest. Leave one of your brothers with me, and take food for your famished [**grain that is lacking in your**] households and go.

Yaakov, their father, said to them, You have deprived me of children; Yoseif is no more [**not here**], Shimon is no more, and you would take Binyamin: All this has happened to me.

Reuvein spoke to his father saying, Put my two sons to death if I do not bring him [Binyamin] back to you. Give him into my hands, and I will return him to you.
