## Chapter 43

###### Onkelos Genesis 43:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:1)


וְכַפְנָא תַּקִּיף בְּאַרְעָא:


The famine was severe in the land.


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:2)


וַהֲוָה כַּד סַפִּיקוּ (שֵׁצִיאוּ עי' רש"י) לְמֵיכַל יָת **עִיבוּרָא** דְּאַיְתִיאוּ מִמִּצְרָיִם וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן אֲבוּהוֹן תּוּבוּ זְבוּנוּ לָנָא זְעֵיר עִיבוּרָא:


When they had finished eating [**entirely comsumed**] the food [**grain**] that they had brought from Egypt, their father said to them, Go back and buy a little food [**grain**] for us.


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:3)


וַאֲמַר לֵיהּ יְהוּדָה לְמֵימָר אַסְהָדָא אַסְהֵד בָּנָא גַבְרָא לְמֵימַר לָא תֶחֱזוּן אַפַּי אֶלָּהֵין כַּד אֲחוּכוֹן עִמְּכוֹן:


Yehudah spoke to him saying, The man warned us repeatedly, saying, You had better not see my face unless your brother is with you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:4)


אִם אִיתָךְ מְשַׁלַּח יָת אָחוּנָא עִמָּנָא נֵיחוֹת וְנִזְבֵּן לָךְ עִיבוּרָא:


If you will send our brother with us, we will go down and buy food [**grain**] for you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:5)


וְאִם לֵיתָךְ מְשַׁלַּח לָא נֵיחוֹת אֲרֵי גַבְרָא אֲמַר לָנָא לָא תֶחֱזוּן אַפַּי אֶלָּהֵין כַּד אֲחוּכוֹן עִמְּכוֹן:


But if you will not send [him] we will not go down; for the man said to us, You had better not see my face unless your brother is with you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:6)


וַאֲמַר יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמָה אַבְאֶשְׁתּוּן לִי לְחַוָּאָה לְגַבְרָא הַעַד כְּעַן לְכוֹן אָחָא:


Yisrael said, Why did you do such harm to me, by telling the man that you had another brother.


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:7)


וַאֲמָרוּ מִשְׁאַל שָׁאֵל גַּבְרָא לָנָא וּלְיַלָּדוּתָנָא לְמֵימַר הַעַד כְּעַן אֲבוּכוֹן קַיָּם הַאִית לְכוֹן אָחָא וְחַוֵּינָא לֵיהּ עַל מֵימַר פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין הֲמִדַּע הֲוֵינָא יָדְעִין אֲרֵי יֵימַר אוֹחִיתוּ יָת אֲחוּכוֹן:


They said, The man asked us repeatedly, about ourselves and about our family, saying. Is your father still alive? Do you have a brother? We told him according to his words. Could we have known that he would say, Bring your brother down?


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:8)


וַאֲמַר יְהוּדָה לְיִשְׂרָאֵל אֲבוּהִי שְׁלַח עוּלֵימָא עִמִּי וְנֵקוּם וְנֵזֵיל וְנֵיחֵי וְלָא נְמוּת אַף אֲנַחְנָא אַף אַתְּ אַף טַפְלָנָא:


Yehudah said to his father, Yisrael, Send the lad with me, and we will get up and go; let us live and not die, we, you and our children.


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:9)

I will be security for him. You will demand him from my hand. If I do not bring him to you, and set him before you, I will have sinned [**be a sinner**] to you for all time.


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:10)


אֲרֵי אִלּוּלָפוֹן בְּדָא אִתְעַכָּבְנָא אֲרֵי כְעַן תַּבְנָא דְּנַן תַּרְתֵּין זִמְנִין:


If we had not hesitated until now, we could have been there and back twice.


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:11)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲבוּהוֹן אִם כֵּן הָכָא דָּא עִיבִידוּ סִיבוּ מִדִּמְשַׁבַּח בְּאַרְעָא בְּמָנֵיכוֹן וְאוֹחִיתוּ לְגַבְרָא תִּקְרֻבְתָּא זְעֵיר קְטַף וּזְעֵיר דְּבַשׁ שְׁעַף וּלְטוֹם בָּטְנִין וְשִׁגְדִין:


Their father Yisrael said to them, If so, here, this is what you must do: Take of the best fruits of the land in your vessels, and take an offering to the man, a little balsam, a little honey, gum, labdanum, pistachios and almonds.


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:12)


וְכַסְפָּא עַל חַד תְּרֵין סִיבוּ בְיֶדְכוֹן וְיָת כַּסְפָּא דְּאִתּוֹתַב בְּפוּם טוֹעֲנֵיכוֹן תְּתִיבוּן בְּיֶדְכוֹן מָאִים שָׁלוּ הוּא:


Take double the money in your hands; and the money that was returned in the opening of your bags return by your own hands. Perhaps it was an oversight.


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:13)


וְיָת אֲחוּכוֹן דְּבָרוּ וְקוּמוּ תּוּבוּ לְוַת גַּבְרָא:


Take your brother, get up and return to the man.


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:14)


וְאֵל שַׁדַּי יִתֵּן לְכוֹן רַחֲמִין לָקֳדָם גַּבְרָא וְיִפְטַר לְכוֹן יָת אֲחוּכוֹן אָחֳרָנָא וְיָת בִּנְיָמִין וַאֲנָא כְּמָא דְאִתְכֵּלִית תְּכֵלִית:


May the Almighty, Shaddai, grant you compassion in the presence of the man, that he may release to you your other brother along with Binyamin. If I have been bereft of my children, I will be bereft.


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:15)


וּנְסִיבוּ גֻבְרַיָּא יָת תִּקְרֻבְתָּא הָדָא וְעַל חַד תְּרֵין כַּסְפָּא נְסִיבוּ בִידֵיהוֹן וּדְבָרוּ יָת בִּנְיָמִן וְקָמוּ וּנְחָתוּ לְמִצְרַיִם וְקָמוּ קֳדָם יוֹסֵף:


The men took that offering, and they took double money in their hands, and [they took] Binyamin. They rose and went down to Egypt, and they stood before Yoseif.


###### Onkelos Genesis 43:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/43:16)


וַחֲזָא יוֹסֵף עִמְּהוֹן יָת בִּנְיָמִין וַאֲמַר לְדִי מְמַנָּא עַל בֵּיתֵיהּ אָעֵיל יָת גֻּבְרַיָּא לְבֵיתָא וּנְכוֹס נִכְסָתָא וְאַתְקֵין אֲרֵי עִמִּי יֵיכְלוּן גֻּבְרַיָּא **בְּשֵׁירוּתָא**:


When Yoseif saw Binyamin with them he said to the one in charge of his house, Bring these men to the house. Slaughter an animal and prepare it, for these men shall dine with me at noon [**the morning meal**].

## Chapter 44

###### Onkelos Genesis 44:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:1)


וּפַקֵּיד יָת דִּי מְמַנָּא עַל בֵּיתֵיהּ לְמֵימַר מְלֵי יָת טוֹעֲנֵי גֻבְרַיָּא **עִיבוּרָא** כְּמָה דִי יָכְלִין לְמַטְּלֵי וְשַׁוִּי כְּסַף גְּבַר בְּפוּם טוֹעֲנֵיהּ:


He commanded the one in charge of his house, saying, Fill the men’s bags with as much food [**grain**] as they can carry, and place each man’s money in the opening of his bag.


###### Onkelos Genesis 44:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:2)

And my goblet, the silver goblet, put in the opening of the youngest one’s bag, together with the money for his purchase of food. He acted according to the word Yoseif had spoken.


###### Onkelos Genesis 44:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:3)


צַפְרָא נְהָר וְגֻבְרַיָּא אִתְפַּטָּרוּ אִנּוּן וַחֲמָרֵיהוֹן:


With the morning light, the men were sent [on their way] together with their donkeys.


###### Onkelos Genesis 44:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:4)


אִנּוּן נְפָקוּ מִן קַרְתָּא לָא אַרְחִיקוּ וְיוֹסֵף אֲמַר לְדִי מְמַנָּא עַל בֵּיתֵיהּ קוּם רְדַף בָּתַר גֻּבְרַיָּא וְתַדְבְּקִנּוּן וְתֵימַר לְהוֹן לְמָה אַשְׁלֶמְתּוּן בִּישָׁא חֳלָף טַבְתָא:


They had just left the city, they had not gone far, when Yoseif said to the one in charge of his house, Get up, pursue the men, and when you catch up with them, say to them, Why did you repay good with evil.


###### Onkelos Genesis 44:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:5)


הֲלָא דֵין דְּשָׁתֵי רִבּוֹנִי בֵּיהּ וְהוּא **בָּדָקָא מְבָדֵק** בֵּיהּ אַבְאֶשְׁתּוּן דִּי עֲבַדְתּוּן:


Why, this is [the goblet] from which my master drinks. He uses it for divination [**investigating**]. You have acted badly in what you have done.


###### Onkelos Genesis 44:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:6)

He caught up with them, and spoke these exact words to them.


###### Onkelos Genesis 44:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:7)


וַאֲמָרוּ לֵיהּ לְמָה יְמַלֵּל רִבּוֹנִי כְּפִתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין **חַס לְעַבְדָיךְ מִלְּמֶעְבַּד** כְּפִתְגָּמָא הָדֵין:


They said to him, Why does my master speak such words? It would be degrading for your servants to do [**May your servants be spared from doing**] such a thing.


###### Onkelos Genesis 44:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:8)


הָא כַסְפָּא דִּי אַשְׁכַּחְנָא בְּפוּם טוֹעֲנָנָא אֲתֵיבְנוֹהִי לָךְ מֵאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן וְאֶכְדֵּין נִגְנוּב מִבֵּית רִבּוֹנָךְ כַּסְפָּא אוֹ דַהֲבָא (**מָנִין** דִּכְסַף אוֹ **מָנִין** דִּדְהָב):


Behold the money that we found in the opening of our bags, we brought back to you from the land of Canaan. How then could we have stolen silver [**vessels**] or gold [**vessels**] from your master’s house?


###### Onkelos Genesis 44:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:9)


דִּי יִשְׁתְּכַח עִמֵּיהּ מֵעַבְדָיךְ וּמִית וְאַף אֲנַחְנָא נְהֵי לְרִבּוֹנִי לְעַבְדִּין:


He among your servants with whom it is found, he shall die. We will also be servants to my master.


###### Onkelos Genesis 44:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:10)


וַאֲמַר אַף כְּעַן כְּפִתְגָּמֵיכוֹן כֶּן הוּא דִּי יִשְׁתְּכַח עִמֵּיהּ יְהֵי לִי עַבְדָּא וְאַתּוּן תְּהוֹן זַכָּאִין:


And he said, Your words are now also correct. The one with whom it is found, he shall be my slave, and you shall be guiltless.


###### Onkelos Genesis 44:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/44:11)


וְאוֹחִיאוּ וּנְחִיתוּ (ס"א וְאַחִיתוּ) גְּבַר יָת טוֹעֲנֵיהּ לְאַרְעָא וּפְתָחוּ גְּבַר טוֹעֲנֵיהּ:


They hurried, and each man lowered his bag to the ground, and each man opened his bag.

## Chapter 45

###### Onkelos Genesis 45:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:1)


וְלָא יְכִיל יוֹסֵף לְאִתְחַסָּנָא לְכֹל דְּקָיְמִין עִלָּו‍ֹהִי וּקְרָא אַפִּיקוּ כָל אֱנַשׁ מֵעִלָּוָי וְלָא קָם אֱנַשׁ עִמֵּיהּ כַּד אִתְיְדַע יוֹסֵף לְוַת אֲחוֹהִי:


Yoseif could not contain his emotions in the presence of all who stood before him, and he cried out Let everyone leave my presence. No man remained with him, when Yoseif made himself known to his brothers.


###### Onkelos Genesis 45:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:2)

He wept aloud, and the Egyptians heard about it, and the [**people of the**] house of Pharaoh [also] heard.


###### Onkelos Genesis 45:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:3)

Yoseif said to his brothers, I am Yoseif, is my father still alive? His brothers could not answer [**a word to**] him for they were shocked at his presence.


###### Onkelos Genesis 45:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:4)

[Then] Yoseif said to his brothers, Please [**Now**] come close to me. They came close [to him] and he said, I am Yoseif your brother, whom you sold into Egypt.


###### Onkelos Genesis 45:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:5)


וּכְעַן לָא תִתְּנְסוּן וְלָא יִתְקָף בְּעֵינֵיכוֹן אֲרֵי זַבִּנְתּוּן יָתִי הָכָא אֲרֵי לְקַיָּמָא שַׁלְּחַנִי יְיָ קֳדָמֵיכוֹן:


Now do not worry, and do not be angry with yourselves that you sold me here; for it was to preserve life that Elohim sent me [here] before you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 45:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:6)

For it is [now] two years that there has been famine in the land; and for another five years there will be no plowing [**sowing**] or harvest.


###### Onkelos Genesis 45:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:7)


וְשַׁלְּחַנִי יְיָ קֳדָמֵיכוֹן לְשַׁוָּאָה לְכוֹן שְׁאָרָא בְּאַרְעָא וּלְקַיָּמָא לְכוֹן לְשֵׁיזָבָא רַבָּא:


Elohim sent me [here] before you to insure your survival in the land, and to keep you alive for a great deliverance.


###### Onkelos Genesis 45:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/45:8)


וּכְעַן לָא אַתּוּן שְׁלַחְתּוּן יָתִי הָכָא אֶלָּהֵן **מִן קֳדָם** יְיָ וְשַׁוְּיַנִי לְאַבָּא לְפַרְעֹה וּלְרִבּוֹן לְכָל **אֱנַשׁ** בֵּיתֵיהּ וְשַׁלִּיט בְּכָל אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


Now [then] it was not you that sent me here, but [**it was from before**] Elohim; and He has made me as a father to Pharaoh, and master of all [**the people of**] his house, and ruler over all the land of Egypt.

## Chapter 46

###### Onkelos Genesis 46:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:1)


וּנְטַל יִשְׂרָאֵל וְכָל דִּי לֵיהּ וַאֲתָא לִבְאֵר שָׁבַע וּדְבַח דִּבְחִין לֵאלָהֵהּ אֲבוּהִי יִצְחָק:


Yisrael journeyed with all that he possessed, and he came to Beer Sheva. He offered sacrifices [there] to the God of his father Yitzchok.


###### Onkelos Genesis 46:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:2)


וַאֲמַר יְיָ לְיִשְׂרָאֵל בְּחֶזְוֵי דְלֵילְיָא וַאֲמַר יַעֲקֹב יַעֲקֹב וַאֲמַר הָא אֲנָא:


Elohim said to Yisrael in a night vision, and He said, Yaakov, Yaakov. And he said, Here I am.


###### Onkelos Genesis 46:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:3)


וַאֲמַר אֲנָא אֵל אֱלָהָא דְאָבוּךְ לָא תִדְחַל מִלְּמֵיחַת לְמִצְרַיִם אֲרֵי לְעַם סַגִּי אֲשַׁוִּנָּךְ תַּמָּן:


He said I am the Almighty, God of your father. Do not be afraid to do down to Egypt, for there I will make you into a great nation.


###### Onkelos Genesis 46:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:4)


אֲנָא אֵחוֹת עִמָּךְ לְמִצְרַיִם וַאֲנָא אַסֵּקִנָּךְ אַף אַסָּקָא וְיוֹסֵף יְשַׁוֵּי יְדוֹהִי עַל עֵינָיךְ:


I will go down with you to Egypt, and I will also surely bring you up again. Yoseif shall place his hand upon your eyes.


###### Onkelos Genesis 46:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:5)


וְקָם יַעֲקֹב מִבְּאֵרָא דְשָׁבַע וּנְטָלוּ בְנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל יָת יַעֲקֹב אֲבוּהוֹן וְיָת טַפְלְהוֹן וְיָת נְשֵׁיהוֹן בַּעֲגַלְתָּא דִּי שְׁלַח פַּרְעֹה לְמִטַּל יָתֵיהּ:


Yaakov rose up from Beer Sheva. The sons of Yisrael transported their father Yaakov, their children, their wives in the wagons that Pharaoh had sent to carry him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 46:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:6)


וּדְבָרוּ יָת גֵּיתֵיהוֹן וְיָת קִנְיָנְהוֹן דִּי קְנוֹ בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנַעַן וַאֲתוֹ לְמִצְרָיִם יַעֲקֹב וְכָל בְּנוֹהִי עִמֵּיהּ:


They took their livestock and their possessions that they had acquired in the land of Canaan, and they came to Egypt; Yaakov and all his descendants with him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 46:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:7)

His sons and grandsons were with him. His daughters and his granddaughters, and all his descendants he brought with him to Egypt.


###### Onkelos Genesis 46:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:8)


וְאִלֵּין שְׁמָהַת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל דְּעַלּוּ לְמִצְרַיִם יַעֲקֹב וּבְנוֹהִי בּוּכְרָא דְיַעֲקֹב רְאוּבֵן:


These are the names of the sons of Yisrael who were coming to Egypt, Yaakov and his sons. The firstborn of Yaakov was Reuvein.


###### Onkelos Genesis 46:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:9)

The sons of Reuvein [were]: Chanoch, Phallu, Chetzron and Carmi.


###### Onkelos Genesis 46:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/46:10)


וּבְנֵי שִׁמְעוֹן יְמוּאֵל וְיָמִין וְאֹהַד וְיָכִין וְצֹחַר וְשָׁאוּל בַּר כְּנַעֲנֵיתָא:


The sons, of Shimon [were]: Yemueil, Yamin, Ohad, Yachin, Tzochar and Shaul, the son of the Canaanite woman.

## Chapter 47

###### Onkelos Genesis 47:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:1)


וַאֲתָא יוֹסֵף וְחַוִּי לְפַרְעֹה וַאֲמַר אַבָּא וְאַחַי וְעָנְהוֹן וְתוֹרְהוֹן וְכָל דִּי לְהוֹן אֲתוֹ מֵאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן וְהָא אִנּוּן בְּאַרְעָא דְגשֶׁן:


Yoseif came and told Pharaoh, and he said, My father, and my brothers, their sheep, their cattle, and all their possessions, have come from the land of Canaan, and they are now in the land of Goshen.


###### Onkelos Genesis 47:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:2)


וּמִקְצַת מִן אֲחוֹהִי דְּבַר חַמְשָׁא גֻבְרִין וַאֲקֵימִנּוּן קֳדָם פַּרְעֹה:


From among his brothers, he took five men, and he presented them to Pharaoh.


###### Onkelos Genesis 47:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:3)


וַאֲמַר פַּרְעֹה לַאֲחוֹהִי מָה עוֹבָדֵיכוֹן וַאֲמָרוּ לְפַרְעֹה רָעֵי עָנָא עַבְדָּיךְ אַף אֲנַחְנָא אַף אֲבְהָתָנָא:


Pharaoh said to his brothers, What is your occupation? They said to Pharaoh, Your servants are shepherds, we and our fathers.


###### Onkelos Genesis 47:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:4)


וַאֲמָרוּ לְוַת פַּרְעֹה לְאִתּוֹתָבָא בְאַרְעָא אֲתֵינָא אֲרֵי לֵית רַעֲיָא לְעָנָא דִּי לְעַבְדָּיךְ אֲרֵי תַקִּיף כַּפְנָא בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן וּכְעַן יֵתְבוּן **כְּעַן** עַבְדָּיךְ בְּאַרְעָא דְגשֶׁן:


They said to Pharaoh, We have come to live in the land temporarily, since there is no pasture for your servant’s flocks, because the famine is severe in the land of Canaan. Now then, please let your servants [**now**] settle in the land of Goshen.


###### Onkelos Genesis 47:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:5)


וַאֲמַר פַּרְעֹה לְיוֹסֵף לְמֵימָר אֲבוּךְ וְאַחָיךְ אֲתוֹ לְוָתָךְ:


Pharaoh said to Yoseif, Your father and your brothers have come to you.


###### Onkelos Genesis 47:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:6)


אַרְעָא דְמִצְרַיִם קֳדָמָךְ הִיא בִּדְשַׁפִּיר בְּאַרְעָא אוֹתֵיב יָת אֲבוּךְ וְיָת אַחָיךְ יֵתְבוּן בְּאַרְעָא דְגשֶׁן וְאִם יָדַעְתְּ וְאִית בְּהוֹן גֻּבְרִין דְּחֵילָא וּתְמַנִּנּוּן רַבָּנֵי גֵיתֵי עַל דִּי לִי:


The land of Egypt is before you. In the best of the land, settle your father and your brothers. Let them settle in the land of Goshen. If you know of capable men among them, appoint them livestock officers over my [herds.]


###### Onkelos Genesis 47:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:7)


וְאַיְתִי יוֹסֵף יָת יַעֲקֹב אֲבוּהִי וַאֲקִימִנֵּיהּ קֳדָם פַּרְעֹה וּבָרִיךְ יַעֲקֹב יָת פַּרְעֹה:


Yoseif [then] brought in Yaakov, his father, and presented him to Pharaoh; and Yaakov blessed Pharaoh.


###### Onkelos Genesis 47:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:8)


וַאֲמַר פַּרְעֹה לְיַעֲקֹב כַּמָּה יוֹמֵי שְׁנֵי חַיָּךְ:


Pharaoh said to Yaakov, How many are the years of your life?


###### Onkelos Genesis 47:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/47:9)

## Chapter 48

###### Onkelos Genesis 48:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/48:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/48:1)


וַהֲוָה בָּתַר פִּתְגָּמַיָּא הָאִלֵּין וַאֲמַר לְיוֹסֵף הָא אֲבוּךְ **שְׁכִיב** מְרָע וּדְבַר יָת תְּרֵין בְּנוֹהִי עִמֵּיהּ יָת מְנַשֶּׁה וְיָת אֶפְרָיִם:


After these events, someone said to Yoseif, Behold your father is [**lying**] ill. He took his two sons with him, Menasheh and Ephraim.


###### Onkelos Genesis 48:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/48:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/48:2)


וְחַוִּי לְיַעֲקֹב וַאֲמַר הָא בְּרָךְ יוֹסֵף אֲתָא לְוָתָךְ וְאַתְקַף יִשְׂרָאֵל וִיתֵיב עַל עַרְסָא:


It was told to Yaakov, saying, Behold, your son, Yoseif, has come to you. Yisrael gathered his strength and sat up in bed.


###### Onkelos Genesis 48:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/48:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/48:3)


וַאֲמַר יַעֲקֹב לְיוֹסֵף אֵל שַׁדַּי **אִתְגְּלִי** לִי בְלוּז בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן וּבָרִיךְ יָתִי:


Yaakov said to Yoseif, Almighty Shaddai appeared [**became revealed**] to me in Luz, in the land of Canaan, and He blessed me.


###### Onkelos Genesis 48:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/48:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/48:4)

## Chapter 49

###### Onkelos Genesis 49:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:1)


וּקְרָא יַעֲקֹב לִבְנוֹהִי וַאֲמַר אִתְכַּנָּשׁוּ וַאֲחַוֵּי לְכוֹן יָת דִּי יְעַרַע יָתְכוֹן בְּסוֹף יוֹמַיָּא:


Yaakov called for his sons and said, Gather around and I will tell you what will happen to you in latter days.


###### Onkelos Genesis 49:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:2)


אִתְכַּנָּשׁוּ וּשְׁמָעוּ בְּנֵי יַעֲקֹב וְקַבִּילוּ אוּלְפַן מִן יִשְׂרָאֵל אֲבוּכוֹן:


Come together and listen, sons of Yaakov; listen to [**accept chastisement from**] Yisrael, your father.


###### Onkelos Genesis 49:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:3)


רְאוּבֵן בּוּכְרִי אַתְּ חֵילִי וְרֵישׁ תָּקְפִּי לָךְ הֲוָה חָזֵי לְמִסַּב תְּלָתָא חוּלָקִין בְּכֵירוּתָא כְּהֻנְתָּא וּמַלְכוּתָא:


Reuvein, you are my firstborn, you are my strength, and the beginning of my manhood [**power**]; superior in rank and superior in power [**it would have been right for you to take three portions—the birthright, the priesthood, and the kingship**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 49:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:4)


עַל דַּאֲזַלְתָּא לָקֳבֵל אַפָּיךְ הָא כְמַיָּא בְּרַם לָא אַהֲנֵיתָא חוֹלָק יַתִּיר לָא תִסָּב אֲרֵי סְלֶקְתָּא בֵּית מִשְׁכְּבֵי אָבוּךְ בְּכֵן אֲחֶלְתָּא לְשִׁוּוּיִי בְּרִי סְלֶקְתָּא:


Unstable as water, you shall no longer be superior, [**However, because you went towards your anger like flowing water, you will not benefit, you will not receive an additional portion,**] for you have gone up [**to the place of**] your father’s bed. You profaned He Who went up on my couch[**, my son, you went up**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 49:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:5)


שִׁמְעוֹן וְלֵוִי אַחִין גַּבְרִין גִּבָּרִין בְּאַרַע תּוֹתָבוּתְהוֹן עֲבָדוּ גְבוּרָא:


Shimon and Levi are brothers[**, mighty men**]. Instruments of violence are their wares. [**In the land of their residence they did mighty deeds.**]


###### Onkelos Genesis 49:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:6)


בְּרָזְהוֹן לָא הֲוַת נַפְשִׁי בְּאִתְכַּנְּשֵׁהוֹן לִמְהַךְ לָא נְחָתִית מִן יְקָרִי אֲרֵי בְרָגְזְהוֹן קְטָלוּ קְטוֹל וּבִרְעוּתְהוֹן תַּרָעוּ שׁוּר סַנְאָה:


My soul will not enter [**was not in**] their secret council, let my honor not be identified with their assembly [**when they gathered to go to war against Shechem I did not descend from my honor and join them**]. For in their anger they killed a [**”dead”**] man, and through their willfulness they maimed an ox [**demolished the enemy’s wall**].


###### Onkelos Genesis 49:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:7)

Cursed be their anger for it is powerful, and their fury for it is cruel. I will divid them throughout Yaakov and scatter them throughout [the land of] Israel.

He commanded them, and said to them, I shall be gathered to my people; bring me for burial to my fathers, to the cave that is in the field of Ephron the Chittite.

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:30](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:30)


בִּמְעַרְתָּא דִּי בַחֲקַל כָּפֶלְתָּא דִּי עַל אַפֵּי מַמְרֵא בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנָעַן דִּי זְבַן אַבְרָהָם יָת חַקְלָא מִן עֶפְרֹן חִתָּאָה לְאַחֲסָנַת קְבוּרָא:


In the cave that is in the field of Machpeilah, which faces Mamrei in the land of Canaan; which Avraham bought, along with the field, from Ephron, the Chittite, for a possession as a burial place.


###### Onkelos Genesis 49:31

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:31](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:31)


תַּמָּן קְבָרוּ יָת אַבְרָהָם וְיָת שָׂרָה אִתְּתֵיהּ תַּמָּן קְבָרוּ יָת יִצְחָק וְיָת רִבְקָה אִתְּתֵיהּ וְתַמָּן קְבָרִית יָת לֵאָה:


There they buried Avraham and Sarah, his wife, there they buried Yitzchok and Rivkah, his wife, and there I buried Leah.


###### Onkelos Genesis 49:32

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:32](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:32)


זְבִינֵי חַקְלָא וּמְעַרְתָּא דִּי בֵיהּ מִן בְּנֵי חִתָּאָה:


The purchase of the field and the cave that is in it was made from the sons of Cheis.


###### Onkelos Genesis 49:33

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:33](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/49:33)


וְשֵׁיצֵי יַעֲקֹב לְפַקָּדָא יָת בְּנוֹהִי וּכְנַשׁ רַגְלוֹהִי לְעַרְסָא וְאִתְנְגִיד וְאִתְכְּנִישׁ לְעַמֵּיהּ:


Yaakov concluded his commands to his sons, and he gathered up his feet, to the bed. He expired and was gathered to his people.



## Chapter 50

###### Onkelos Genesis 50:1

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:1](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:1)


וּנְפַל יוֹסֵף עַל אַפֵּי אָבוּהִי וּבְכָא עַלּוֹהִי וּנְשַׁק לֵיהּ:


Yoseif fell on the face of his father, and wept over him and kissed him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:2

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:2](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:2)


וּפַקִּיד יוֹסֵף יָת עַבְדּוֹהִי יָת אַסְוָתָא לְמֶחְנַט יָת אָבוּהִי וְחָנְטוּ אַסְוָתָא יָת יִשְׂרָאֵל:


Yoseif commanded his servants, the physicians, to embalm his father and the physicians embalmed Yisrael.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:3

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:3](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:3)


וּשְׁלִימוּ לֵיהּ אַרְבְּעִין יוֹמִין אֲרֵי כֵּן שְׁלִימוּ יוֹמֵי חֲנִטַּיָּא וּבְכוֹ יָתֵיהּ מִצְרָאֵי שַׁבְעִין יוֹמִין:


They completed forty days [of embalmment] for him, for that is the number of days required for embalming. The Egyptians wept for him seventy days.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:4

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:4](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:4)


וַעֲבָרוּ יוֹמֵי בְכִיתֵיהּ וּמַלֵּיל יוֹסֵף עִם בֵּית פַּרְעֹה לְמֵימָר אִם כְּעַן אַשְׁכָּחִית רַחֲמִין בְּעֵינֵיכוֹן מַלִּילוּ **כְעַן קֳדָם** פַּרְעֹה לְמֵימָר:


[When] the days of weeping were over, Yoseif spoke to the house of Pharaoh saying, If I have found favor in your eyes, please [**now**] speak in [**before**] Pharaoh’s ears, saying.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:5

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:5](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:5)


אַבָּא קַיִּים עָלַי לְמֵימַר הָא אֲנָא מָאִית בְּקִבְרִי דְּאַתְקֵנִית לִי בְּאַרְעָא דִכְנַעַן תַּמָּן תִּקְבְּרִנָּנִי וּכְעַן אֶסַּק **כְּעַן** וְאֶקְבַּר יָת אַבָּא וְאֵיתוּב:


My father made me swear [an oath] saying, Behold I am dying. In my grave that I have prepared for myself in the land of Canaan, there you shall bury me. Now therefore, please [**now**], I would go up and bury my father, and then I will return.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:6

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:6](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:6)


וַאֲמַר פַּרְעֹה סַק וּקְבוֹר יָת אָבוּךְ כְּמָא דְקַיִּים עֲלָךְ:


Pharaoh said, Go up and bury your father, just as he made you swear.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:7

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:7](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:7)


וּסְלֵיק יוֹסֵף לְמִקְבַּר יָת אָבוּהִי וּסְלִיקוּ עִמֵּיהּ כָּל עַבְדֵי פַרְעֹה סָבֵי בֵיתֵיהּ וְכֹל סָבֵי אַרְעָא דְמִצְרָיִם:


Yoseif went up to bury his father; and with him went up all Pharaohs servant’s, the elders of this house, and all the elders of the land of Egypt,


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:8

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:8](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:8)


וְכֹל בֵּית יוֹסֵף וַאֲחוֹהִי וּבֵית אָבוּהִי לְחוֹד טַפְלְהוֹן וְעָנְהוֹן וְתוֹרֵיהוֹן שְׁבָקוּ בְּאַרְעָא דְגשֶׁן:


And all Yoseif’s household, his brothers and his father’s household. Only their little ones, their sheep and their cattle did they leave behind in the land of Goshen.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:9

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:9](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:9)


וּסְלִיקוּ עִמֵּיהּ אַף רְתִכִּין אַף פָּרָשִׁין וַהֲוָה מַשְׁרִיתָא סַגִּיאָה לַחֲדָא:


With him also went up chariots and horsemen. It was a very imposing camp.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:10

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:10](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:10)


וַאֲתוֹ עַד בֵּית אִדְרֵי דְאָטָד דִּי בְּעִבְרָא דְיַרְדְּנָא וּסְפָדוּ תַמָּן מִסְפֵּד רַב וְתַקִּיף לַחֲדָא וְעָבַד לַאֲבוּהִי אֶבְלָא שַׁבְעָא יוֹמִין:


They came to the threshing place of Atad, which is on the other side of the Jordan, and there they eulogized [him and held] a very great and imposing funeral. He [Yoseif] made seven days of mourning for his father.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:11

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:11](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:11)


וַחֲזָא יָתֵב אַרְעָא כְנַעֲנָאָה יָת אֶבְלָא בְּבֵית אִדְרֵי דְאָטָד וַאֲמָרוּ אָבֵל תַּקִּיף דֵּין לְמִצְרָאֵי עַל כֵּן קְרָא שְׁמַהּ אָבֵל מִצְרַיִם דִּי בְּעִבְרָא דְיַרְדְּנָא:


The Canaanites who lived in the land saw the mourning in the threshing place of Atad, and they said, This is heavy mourning for Egypt. It was therefore named Egypt’s Mourning, which is on the other side of the Jordan.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:12

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:12](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:12)


וַעֲבָדוּ בְנוֹהִי לֵיהּ כֵּן כְּמָא דִי פַקֵּדִנּוּן:


His sons did for him as he commanded them.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:13

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:13](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:13)


וּנְטָלוּ יָתֵיהּ בְּנוֹהִי לְאַרְעָא דִכְנַעַן וּקְבָרוּ יָתֵיהּ בִּמְעָרַת חֲקַל כָּפֶלְתָּא דִּי זְבַן אַבְרָהָם יָת חַקְלָא לְאַחֲסָנַת קְבוּרָא מִן עֶפְרֹן חִתָּאָה עַל אַפֵּי מַמְרֵא:


His sons carried him to the land of Canaan, and buried him in the cave of the field of Machpeilah, which Avraham purchased along with the field, for a possession as a burial place, from Ephron, the Chittite, facing Mamrei.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:14

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:14](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:14)


וְתַב יוֹסֵף לְמִצְרַיִם הוּא וַאֲחוֹהִי וְכָל דִּסְלִיקוּ עִמֵּיהּ לְמִקְבַּר יָת אָבוּהִי בָּתַר דִּקְבַר יָת אָבוּהִי:


Yoseif returned to Egypt, he and his brothers, and all those who went with him, to bury his father, after he had buried his father.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:15

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:15](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:15)


וַחֲזוֹ אֲחֵי יוֹסֵף אֲרֵי מִית אֲבוּהוֹן וַאֲמָרוּ דִּלְמָא יִטַּר לָנָא דְבָבוּ יוֹסֵף וַאֲתָבָא יָתֵיב לָנָא יָת כָּל בִּישְׁתָא דִּי גְמַלְנָא יָתֵיהּ:


Yoseif’s brothers saw that their father was dead, and they said, Perhaps Yoseif still bears a grudge against us. He will then certainly repay us for all the evil that we did him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:16

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:16](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:16)


וּפַקִּידוּ לְוַת יוֹסֵף לְמֵימָר אָבוּךְ פַּקֵּד קֳדָם מוֹתֵיהּ לְמֵימָר:


They sent a command to Yoseif saying, Your father issued a command before his death, saying,


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:17

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:17](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:17)


כְּדֵין תֵּימְרוּן לְיוֹסֵף בְּבָעוּ שְׁבוֹק כְּעַן לְחוֹבָא אַחָיךְ וְלַחֲטָאֵהוֹן אֲרֵי בִישְׁתָא גְמָלוּךְ וּכְעַן שְׁבוֹק **כְּעַן** לְחוֹבָא עַבְדֵי אֱלָהָא דְאָבוּךְ וּבְכָא יוֹסֵף בְּמַלָּלוּתְהוֹן עִמֵּיהּ:


This is what you should say to Yoseif, Please forgive the transgression of your brothers and their sin, for they did evil to you. And now please forgive [**now**] the transgr ession of the servants of the God of your father. Yoseif wept as his brothers spoke to him.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:18

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:18](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:18)


וַאֲזָלוּ אַף אֲחוֹהִי וּנְפָלוּ קֳדָמוֹהִי וַאֲמָרוּ הָא אֲנַחְנָא לָךְ לְעַבְדִּין:


His brothers also went and threw themselves down before him, and they said, Behold, we are your slaves.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:19

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:19](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:19)


וַאֲמַר לְהוֹן יוֹסֵף לָא תִדַּחֲלוּן **אֲרֵי דַּחֲלָא דַיְיָ אֲנָא**:


Yoseif said to them, Fear not. For am I in place of Elohim? [**For I am one who fears Elohim**]


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:20

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:20](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:20)


וְאַתּוּן חֲשַׁבְתּוּן עֲלַי בִּישָׁא מִן **קֳדָם** יְיָ **אִתְחַשְּׁבַת** לְטָבָא בְּדִיל לְמֶּעְבַּד כְּיוֹמָא הָדֵין לְקַיָּמָא עַם סַגִּי:


You meant to do evil to me, but [**from before**] Elohim [**it was meant**] meant it for good, in order to do as it is today, to preserve the lives of a great people.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:21

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:21](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:21)


וּכְעַן לָא תִדַּחֲלוּן אֲנָא אֵזוּן יָתְכוֹן וְיָת טַפְלְכוֹן וְנַחֵם יָתְהוֹן **וּמַלֵּל תַּנְחוּמִין** עַל לִבְּהוֹן:


And now, fear not. I will provide for you and your little ones. He comforted them and spoke [**words of consolation**] to their hearts.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:22

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:22](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:22)


וִיתֵיב יוֹסֵף בְּמִצְרַיִם הוּא וּבֵית אָבוּהִי וַחֲיָא יוֹסֵף מְאָה וַעֲסַר שְׁנִין:


Yoseif lived in Egypt, he and his father’s household. Yoseif lived one hundred and ten years.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:23

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:23](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:23)


וַחֲזָא יוֹסֵף לְאֶפְרַיִם בְּנִין תְּלִיתָאִין אַף בְּנֵי מָכִיר בַּר מְנַשֶּׁה אִתְיְלִידוּ **וְרַבִּי יוֹסֵף**:


Yoseif saw Ephraim’s children of the third generations. The children of Machir, Menasheh’s son, were born [**and Yoseif rasied them**] on Yoseif’s knees.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:24

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:24](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:24)


וַאֲמַר יוֹסֵף לַאֲחוֹהִי אֲנָא מָאִית וַייָ מִדְכַּר דְּכִיר יָתְכוֹן וְיִסַּק יָתְכוֹן מִן אַרְעָא הָדָא לְאַרְעָא דִּי קַיִּים לְאַבְרָהָם לְיִצְחָק וּלְיַעֲקֹב:


Yoseif said to his brothers, I will die, and Elohim will surely consider you and bring you up out of this land, to the land which He swore to Avraham, Yitzchok and to Yaakov.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:25

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:25](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:25)


וְאוֹמֵי יוֹסֵף יָת בְּנֵי יִשְׂרָאֵל לְמֵימָר מִדְכַּר דְּכִיר יְיָ יָתְכוֹן וְתַסְּקוּן יָת גַּרְמַי מִכָּא:


Yoseif made the sons of Yisrael swear, saying, Elohim will surely consider you, and you shall bring my bones up from here.


###### Onkelos Genesis 50:26

[https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:26](https://torahapp.org/share/book/Onkelos%20Genesis/r/50:26)


וּמִית יוֹסֵף בַּר מְאָה וַעֲסַר שְׁנִין וַחֲנָטוּ יָתֵיהּ וְשָׂמוּהִי בְאָרוֹנָא בְּמִצְרָיִם:


Yoseif died being a hundred and ten years old. They embalmed him and he was placed in a coffin in Egypt. **Chazak**
